    // === src/__tests__/adversarial/terminalB2BStage6.adversarial.test.ts === //
import fc from 'fast-check';
import { MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS } from '../../services/spotCollisionService';
import {
  buildPartnerPeriodOptions,
  classifyOfflineFlushOutcome,
  computePartnerSpeiStatement,
  mergeLiveStudioSpotsRoster,
  parseTerminalQrPacket,
} from '../../services/terminalSpeiAndStudioService';

declare const describe: (name: string, fn: () => void) => void;
declare const it: (name: string, fn: () => void) => void;

const expectTrue = (cond: boolean, msg: string): void => {
  if (!cond) throw new Error(msg);
};
const expectEq = <T>(actual: T, expected: T, msg: string): void => {
  if (actual !== expected) throw new Error(`${msg} (expected=${String(expected)}, actual=${String(actual)})`);
};

describe('Terminal B2B v2.6.7 — QR TOTP 60s, Corte SPEI 80/20 SAT, Sala 3D & Cola Offline (fast-check 500x)', () => {
  it('Propiedad 1: parseTerminalQrPacket valida ventana NTP TOTP 60s [-10s,+63s] y rechaza fuzzing/__proto__/expirados', () => {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(
        fc.uuid(),
        fc.integer({ min: 1_700_000_000_000, max: 1_900_000_000_000 }),
        fc.stringMatching(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{4,24}$/).filter((s) => !['__proto__', 'constructor', 'prototype'].includes(s.toLowerCase())),
        (uuid, nowMs, nonce) => {
          const curStep = Math.floor(nowMs / 60_000);
          const validFrame = `KINETIC_PASS|${uuid}|${curStep}|${nonce}`;
          const res = parseTerminalQrPacket(validFrame, nowMs);
          expectTrue(res.ok && !res.isExpired, 'current TOTP bucket must be valid');
          expectEq(res.qrIdentifier, uuid, 'extracted qrIdentifier');
          expectEq(res.totpStep, curStep, 'extracted totpStep');
          expectEq(res.qrNonce, nonce, 'extracted qrNonce');

          const staleStep = curStep - 3;
          const expRes = parseTerminalQrPacket(`KINETIC_PASS|${uuid}|${staleStep}|${nonce}`, nowMs);
          expectTrue(!expRes.ok && expRes.isExpired, 'expired bucket outside grace window must fail');

          const futureStep = curStep + 3;
          const futRes = parseTerminalQrPacket(`KINETIC_PASS|${uuid}|${futureStep}`, nowMs);
          expectTrue(!futRes.ok && futRes.isExpired, 'far-future bucket must fail');
        }
      ),
      { numRuns: 500 }
    );

    fc.assert(
      fc.property(
        fc.oneof(
          fc.constant('__proto__'),
          fc.constant('constructor'),
          fc.constant('prototype'),
          fc.constant('KINETIC_PASS|__proto__|29000000'),
          fc.constant('{"__proto__":{"polluted":true},"id":"KNT-12345"}'),
          fc.constant('{"id":"KNT-12345"'),
          fc.constant(''),
          fc.string({ maxLength: 5 }).filter((s) => s.trim().length < 6),
          fc.constant('🔥💀<script>alert(1)</script>')
        ),
        (badInput) => {
          const r = parseTerminalQrPacket(badInput, 1_760_000_000_000);
          expectTrue(!r.ok && r.format === 'invalid', `adversarial input must be rejected: ${badInput}`);
          expectEq(({} as any).polluted, undefined, 'zero prototype pollution');
        }
      ),
      { numRuns: 500 }
    );
  });

  it('Propiedad 2: computePartnerSpeiStatement mantiene invariantes exactos 80/20 + Retención SAT (0% / 10.1% / 36%)', () => {
    const period = buildPartnerPeriodOptions(1_760_000_000_000, false)[0].override;
    const txStampIso = new Date(period.startMs + 3_600_000).toISOString();

    fc.assert(
      fc.property(
        fc.constantFrom<'moral' | 'fisica_rfc' | 'sin_rfc'>('moral', 'fisica_rfc', 'sin_rfc'),
        fc.array(
          fc.record({
            id: fc.uuid(),
            estado: fc.constantFrom('completada', 'no_show', 'activa'),
            costo_total: fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 25_000 }),
          }),
          { minLength: 0, maxLength: 20 }
        ),
        (mode, rawRows) => {
          const biz =
            mode === 'moral'
              ? { id: 'b1', nombre: 'Kinetic Arena', rfc: 'KIN260101AAA', regimen_fiscal: '601 - Persona Moral' }
              : mode === 'fisica_rfc'
                ? { id: 'b2', nombre: 'Kinetic Studio', rfc: 'GODE561231GR8', regimen_fiscal: '612 - Persona Física' }
                : { id: 'b3', nombre: 'Kinetic Lab', rfc: '', regimen_fiscal: '' };

          const rows = rawRows.map((r) => ({
            ...r,
            negocio_nombre: biz.nombre,
            monto_escrow: Math.round(r.costo_total * 0.4),
            monto_puerta: r.costo_total - Math.round(r.costo_total * 0.4),
            updated_at: txStampIso,
            created_at: txStampIso,
          }));

          const { corteSocio: c } = computePartnerSpeiStatement(biz, rows, [], period);
          const sumSplit = Number((c.comision_kinetic_total_mxn + c.subtotal_socio_80_mxn).toFixed(2));
          expectEq(sumSplit, Number(c.volumen_bruto_mxn.toFixed(2)), '20% + 80% === 100% bruto');
          expectTrue(c.total_retenciones_sat_mxn >= 0, 'retenciones >= 0');

          if (mode === 'moral') {
            expectEq(c.regimen_fiscal, 'persona_moral', 'persona moral regime');
            expectEq(c.total_retenciones_sat_mxn, 0, 'persona moral 0% SAT retention');
          } else if (mode === 'fisica_rfc') {
            expectEq(c.regimen_fiscal, 'persona_fisica_con_rfc', 'persona fisica con rfc');
            const expectedRet = Number((Number((c.subtotal_socio_80_mxn * 0.021).toFixed(2)) + Number((c.subtotal_socio_80_mxn * 0.08).toFixed(2))).toFixed(2));
            expectEq(c.total_retenciones_sat_mxn, expectedRet, '10.1% SAT retention (2.1% ISR + 8% IVA)');
          } else {
            expectEq(c.regimen_fiscal, 'sin_rfc', 'sin rfc regime');
            const expectedRet36 = Number((Number((c.subtotal_socio_80_mxn * 0.2).toFixed(2)) + Number((c.subtotal_socio_80_mxn * 0.16).toFixed(2))).toFixed(2));
            expectEq(c.total_retenciones_sat_mxn, expectedRet36, '36% SAT retention (20% ISR + 16% IVA)');
          }

          const expectedNeto = Math.max(0, Number((c.subtotal_socio_80_mxn - c.total_retenciones_sat_mxn).toFixed(2)));
          expectEq(c.neto_dispersar_spei_mxn, expectedNeto, 'netoSpei === subtotal80 - retenciones >= 0');
        }
      ),
      { numRuns: 500 }
    );
  });

  it('Propiedad 3: mergeLiveStudioSpotsRoster garantiza 0 <= checkedIn <= occupied <= totalSpots <= MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS sin colisiones', () => {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(
        fc.integer({ min: 1, max: 45 }),
        fc.array(
          fc.record({
            id: fc.uuid(),
            spot_number: fc.option(fc.integer({ min: -5, max: 40 }), { nil: undefined }),
            checked_in: fc.boolean(),
            nombre_atleta: fc.string({ minLength: 1, maxLength: 40 }),
          }),
          { minLength: 0, maxLength: 35 }
        ),
        (maxCupo, items) => {
          const duplicated = items.length > 0 ? [...items, items[0]] : items;
          const roster = mergeLiveStudioSpotsRoster({
            discipline: 'Indoor Cycling',
            maxCupo,
            cloudSpots: duplicated,
            localCompletedPasses: items.slice(0, 2),
          });

          expectTrue(roster.checkedInCount >= 0, 'checkedInCount >= 0');
          expectTrue(roster.checkedInCount <= roster.occupiedCount, 'checkedInCount <= occupiedCount');
          expectTrue(roster.occupiedCount <= roster.totalSpots, 'occupiedCount <= totalSpots');
          expectTrue(roster.totalSpots >= 1 && roster.totalSpots <= MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS, 'totalSpots within [1, MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS]');

          const seenReservaIds = new Set<string>();
          const seenSpotNums = new Set<number>();
          for (const sp of roster.spots) {
            expectTrue(!seenSpotNums.has(sp.spotNumber), `no duplicate spot_number: ${sp.spotNumber}`);
            seenSpotNums.add(sp.spotNumber);
            if (sp.reservaId) {
              expectTrue(!seenReservaIds.has(sp.reservaId), `no duplicate reserva_id: ${sp.reservaId}`);
              seenReservaIds.add(sp.reservaId);
            }
          }
        }
      ),
      { numRuns: 500 }
    );
  });

  it('Propiedad 4: classifyOfflineFlushOutcome reintenta solo fallos de red y purga errores terminales de negocio/RLS', () => {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(
        fc.constantFrom(
          { message: 'Network request failed' },
          { message: 'Failed to fetch' },
          { message: 'Offline mode timeout ETIMEDOUT' },
          { code: 'ECONNABORTED', message: 'timeout' }
        ),
        (netErr) => {
          expectEq(classifyOfflineFlushOutcome(netErr), 'retry_later', 'network error must retry_later');
        }
      ),
      { numRuns: 500 }
    );

    fc.assert(
      fc.property(
        fc.constantFrom(
          { message: 'QR_YA_UTILIZADO: Este pase ya fue escaneado' },
          { code: '42501', message: 'Recepcion no autorizada para este club' },
          { code: 'PGRST116', message: 'Reserva no encontrada' },
          { message: 'SALDO_PUERTA_INSUFICIENTE' },
          { code: '23505', message: 'duplicate key value violates unique constraint' }
        ),
        (termErr) => {
          expectEq(classifyOfflineFlushOutcome(termErr), 'synced_or_terminal', 'terminal business/RLS error must purge');
        }
      ),
      { numRuns: 500 }
    );
  });
});

const CIUDADES_PILOTO = ['Ciudad de México', 'Monterrey', 'Guadalajara', 'Cancún', 'Tijuana', 'Querétaro'];
const CATEGORY_OPTIONS = SORTED_CATEGORIES;
const NO_SEL = Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as any) : null;

const getLocalISO = (offsetDays = 0, refDate?: Date) => addDaysToIso(getMazatlanNowParts(refDate).isoDate, offsetDays);

const extractLocalDatePart = (rawFecha: any): string => {
  const m = String(rawFecha || '').trim().match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/);
  return m ? `${m[1]}-${m[2]}-${m[3]}` : '';
};

const isInactiveServiceState = (srv: any): boolean => {
  if (srv?.activo === false) return true;
  const est = String(srv?.estado || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  return est === 'inactivo' || est === 'pausado' || est === 'cancelado' || est === 'inactive' || est === 'paused' || est === 'canceled';
};

const isRecurringService = (srv: any) => {
  const rawDesc = String(srv?.descripcion || '');
  const freqMatch = rawDesc.match(/\[Frecuencia Programada:\s*(.*?)\]/i);
  const freq = String(srv?.frecuencia || freqMatch?.[1] || 'Única Vez').toLowerCase();
  return /diario|semanal|mensual|daily|weekly|monthly/.test(freq);
};

const parseStartMinutes = (rawHora: any): number => {
  const m = String(rawHora || '08:00').trim().match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})\s*(am|pm)?/i);
  if (!m) return 8 * 60;
  let hh = parseInt(m[1], 10), mm = parseInt(m[2], 10);
  const mer = (m[3] || '').toLowerCase();
  if (mer === 'pm' && hh < 12) hh += 12;
  if (mer === 'am' && hh === 12) hh = 0;
  return (Number.isFinite(hh) ? hh : 8) * 60 + (Number.isFinite(mm) ? mm : 0);
};

export default function TerminalScreen() {
  const { user, session, profile, isLoading: isAuthLoading } = useAuth();
  const { resolvedTheme, themeColors, t, tx, td, fmtDate } = useKineticPreferences();
  const tm = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseTerminalStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const pulseAnim = useRef(new Animated.Value(1)).current;
  const refreshSpinVal = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
  const scrollViewRef = useRef<ScrollView>(null);

  const {
    isScreenMounted, permission, requestPermission, isVerifying, hasInitialLoaded, refreshing, hasBusiness, setHasBusiness,
    businessData, setBusinessData, isAdminView, pasesActivos, historialCompleto, misServicios, ingresosHoy,
    showScanner, setShowScanner, setScanned, isProcessing, setIsProcessing, showSuccessModal, setShowSuccessModal,
    successDetails, pendingOfflineCount, syncOfflineQueue, checkBusinessStatus, fetchOperacionEstadio, onRefresh, processScannedData,
  } = useTerminal(user, profile, session);
  const [isSyncingOffline, setIsSyncingOffline] = useState(false);
  const prevOfflineCountRef = useRef(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (pendingOfflineCount > 0 && pendingOfflineCount > prevOfflineCountRef.current) {
      triggerHaptic('Warning');
    }
    prevOfflineCountRef.current = pendingOfflineCount;
  }, [pendingOfflineCount]);

  const [nuevoSocio, setNuevoSocio] = useState({
    nombre: profile?.nombre || '', ubicacion: '', disciplina: CATEGORY_OPTIONS[0] || 'Fuerza',
    horarios: tx('Lunes a Sábado • 06:00 a 21:00', 'Mon to Sat • 06:00 to 21:00'),
  });
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);
  const [filtroCatalogo, setFiltroCatalogo] = useState<TerminalCatalogStatusFilter>('activos');
  const [catalogSearch, setCatalogSearch] = useState('');
  const [catalogFilter, setCatalogFilter] = useState<'all' | 'once' | 'recurring' | 'premium'>('all');
  const [catalogSort, setCatalogSort] = useState<TerminalCatalogSortOption>('soonest');
  const [filtroBitacora, setFiltroBitacora] = useState('all');
  const [isHistoryExpanded, setIsHistoryExpanded] = useState(false);
  const [activeTerminalSheet, setActiveTerminalSheet] = useState<TerminalSheetType>(null);
  const [pendingSearchQuery, setPendingSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [selectedAuditItem, setSelectedAuditItem] = useState<any>(null);
  const [visualEditorEvent, setVisualEditorEvent] = useState<any | null>(null);
  const [isVisualCreateMode, setIsVisualCreateMode] = useState(false);
  const [liveRoomService, setLiveRoomService] = useState<any | null>(null);
  const modalBridgeTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  const [isOperatorStoryOpen, setIsOperatorStoryOpen] = useState(false);
  const [showOnbCityPicker, setShowOnbCityPicker] = useState(false);
  const [showOnbCatPicker, setShowOnbCatPicker] = useState(false);
  const [hasScrolled, setHasScrolled] = useState(false);
  const [alertConfig, setAlertConfig] = useState<any>({ visible: false });

  const showTacticalAlert = useCallback((title: string, message: string, type = 'alert', onConfirm: (() => void) | null = null) => {
    setAlertConfig({ visible: true, title, message, type, onConfirm });
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    isScreenMounted.current = true;
    return () => { isScreenMounted.current = false; if (modalBridgeTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(modalBridgeTimerRef.current); };
  }, [isScreenMounted]);
  useFocusEffect(useCallback(() => {
    isScreenMounted.current = true;
    checkBusinessStatus(true, true, true);
    return () => { setShowScanner(false); };
  }, [checkBusinessStatus, isScreenMounted, setShowScanner]));

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!showSuccessModal) return;
    const anim = Animated.loop(
      Animated.sequence([
        Animated.timing(pulseAnim, { toValue: 1.2, duration: 550, useNativeDriver: Platform.OS !== 'web' }),
        Animated.timing(pulseAnim, { toValue: 1, duration: 550, useNativeDriver: Platform.OS !== 'web' }),
      ])
    );
    anim.start();
    return () => anim.stop();
  }, [showSuccessModal, pulseAnim]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!refreshing) { refreshSpinVal.stopAnimation(); refreshSpinVal.setValue(0); return; }
    refreshSpinVal.setValue(0);
    const loop = Animated.loop(Animated.timing(refreshSpinVal, { toValue: 1, duration: 760, easing: Easing.linear, useNativeDriver: Platform.OS !== 'web' }));
    loop.start();
    return () => loop.stop();
  }, [refreshing, refreshSpinVal]);

  const refreshClockwiseSpin = refreshSpinVal.interpolate({ inputRange: [0, 1], outputRange: ['0deg', '360deg'] });

  const activeBizName = useMemo(() => {
    if (!hasBusiness && !isAdminView) return '';
    const raw = String(businessData?.nombre || '').trim();
    if (raw === 'Terminal Global Kinetic') return 'Kinetic Oficial';
    return raw || (isAdminView ? 'Kinetic Oficial' : String(profile?.nombre || '').trim());
  }, [hasBusiness, businessData?.nombre, isAdminView, profile?.nombre]);

  const { serviciosActivos, serviciosExpirados } = useMemo(() => {
    type SrvItem = (typeof misServicios)[number];
    const activos: SrvItem[] = [], expirados: SrvItem[] = [], mz = getMazatlanNowParts();
    const todayIso = mz.isoDate;
    const nowMinutes = mz.hour * 60 + mz.minute;
    (misServicios || []).forEach((srv: any) => {
      if (isInactiveServiceState(srv)) { expirados.push(srv); return; }
      const fIso = extractLocalDatePart(srv?.fecha);
      const startMins = parseStartMinutes(srv?.hora_inicio);
      const endMins = startMins + (Number(srv?.duracion_minutos ?? srv?.duracion_min) || 60);
      const isRec = isRecurringService(srv);
      const isPast = !isRec && Boolean(fIso) && (fIso < todayIso || (fIso === todayIso && nowMinutes > endMins));
      if (isPast) { expirados.push(srv); return; }
      if (isRec) {
        const endIso = extractLocalDatePart(srv?.fecha_fin || srv?.atributos?.fecha_fin);
        const life = computeEventLocalLifecycle(srv);
        if ((endIso && endIso < todayIso) || life.isExpired) { expirados.push(srv); return; }
        activos.push({ ...srv, fecha_original: life.fechaOriginal, fecha: life.fechaProyectada, fecha_proyectada: life.fechaProyectada });
      } else {
        activos.push(srv);
      }
    });
    return { serviciosActivos: activos, serviciosExpirados: expirados };
  }, [misServicios, refreshing]);

  const validPasesActivos = useMemo(() => {
    const list = pasesActivos || [];
    if (!Array.isArray(misServicios) || misServicios.length === 0) return [];
    const validIds = new Set<string>(), validTitles = new Set<string>();
    misServicios.forEach((srv: any) => {
      if (srv?.id != null) validIds.add(String(srv.id).trim());
      const t1 = String(srv?.nombre_servicio || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const t2 = String(srv?.titulo || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      if (t1) validTitles.add(t1);
      if (t2) validTitles.add(t2);
    });
    return list.filter((p: any) => {
      const sid = p?.servicio_id != null ? String(p.servicio_id).trim() : '';
      if (sid) return validIds.has(sid);
      const pTitle = String(p?.servicio || p?.titulo_clase || p?.nombre_servicio || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      return Boolean(pTitle && validTitles.has(pTitle));
    });
  }, [pasesActivos, misServicios]);

  const reservasCompletadasList = useMemo(() => (historialCompleto || []).filter((r: any) => r.estado === 'completada'), [historialCompleto]);

  const operatorStoryOwner = useMemo<IKineticStoryOwner>(() => {
    const lower = String(activeBizName).trim().toLowerCase();
    const isOfficial = lower === 'kinetic oficial' || lower === 'kinetic sports club';
    const disc = businessData?.disciplina || businessData?.categoria || 'Fuerza';
    const matched = (KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES || []).find((a: any) => String(a.nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase() === lower);
    const resolvedId = businessData?.id && businessData?.nombre !== 'Terminal Global Kinetic' ? String(businessData.id) : String(matched?.id || activeBizName);
    return {
      id: resolvedId, name: activeBizName, subtitle: `${td(disc)} • ${tx('Terminal Operativa', 'Operating Terminal')}`,
      avatarUrl: businessData?.avatar_url || businessData?.logo_url || matched?.avatar_url || (matched as any)?.imagen_url || (isOfficial ? profile?.avatar_url : null) || null,
      isOfficialKinetic: isOfficial, isB2B: true, discipline: disc, canUpload: true, availableEvents: serviciosActivos,
    };
  }, [activeBizName, businessData, profile?.avatar_url, serviciosActivos, td, tx]);

  const activarEstadio = async () => {
    const cleanName = nuevoSocio.nombre.trim();
    if (!cleanName || !user?.id || isSaving) {
      if (!cleanName || !user?.id) {
        triggerHaptic('Warning');
        showTacticalAlert(tx('NOMBRE REQUERIDO', 'NAME REQUIRED'), tx('Ingresa el nombre comercial de tu Club o Especialista.', 'Enter the commercial name of your Club or Specialist.'), 'warning');
      }
      return;
    }
    setIsSaving(true);
    try {
      const created = await terminalService.activateOperatorProfile(user.id, user.email || '', {
        nombre: cleanName, ubicacion: nuevoSocio.ubicacion, disciplina: nuevoSocio.disciplina,
      });
      setBusinessData(created);
      setHasBusiness(true);
      await fetchOperacionEstadio(created.nombre, true, isAdminView);
      triggerHaptic('Success');
    } catch (err: any) {
      showTacticalAlert(tx('FRICCIÓN', 'FRICTION'), err?.message || tx('No se pudo activar la terminal.', 'Could not activate the terminal.'), 'error');
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const handleActivarLente = async () => {
    try {
      triggerHaptic('Selection');
      if (!permission?.granted) {
        const res = await requestPermission();
        if (!res.granted) {
          return showTacticalAlert(tx('Permiso de Cámara Requerido', 'Camera Permission Required'), tx('Habilita el acceso a la cámara para escanear llaves Pase QR en recepción.', 'Enable camera access to scan dynamic QR passes at front desk.'), 'warning');
        }
      }
      setScanned(false);
      setIsProcessing(false);
      setShowScanner(true);
    } catch (err: any) {
      showTacticalAlert(tx('FRICCIÓN DE CÁMARA', 'CAMERA ERROR'), err?.message || tx('No se pudo inicializar el lector óptico QR.', 'Could not initialize the optical QR scanner.'), 'error');
    }
  };

  const closeScannerModal = () => {
    triggerHaptic('Light');
    setShowScanner(false);
    setIsProcessing(false);
    setScanned(false);
  };

  const handleBarCodeScanned = (rawQrData: string) => {
    if (!rawQrData) return;
    processScannedData(rawQrData, triggerHaptic, showTacticalAlert, () => triggerArcadeHaptic('checkIn'));
  };

  const handleOpenVisualCreator = useCallback(() => {
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    setIsVisualCreateMode(true);
    setVisualEditorEvent({ id: null, negocio_nombre: activeBizName, ubicacion: '', openInEditMode: true });
  }, [activeBizName]);

  const handleOpenVisualEditor = useCallback((srv: any, isExpired = false) => {
    if (!srv) return;
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    const curIso = extractLocalDatePart(srv.fecha);
    const nextFecha = isExpired && (!curIso || curIso <= getLocalISO(0)) ? getLocalISO(1) : srv.fecha;
    const rawClub = srv.negocio_nombre === 'Terminal Global Kinetic' ? 'Kinetic Oficial' : srv.negocio_nombre;
    setIsVisualCreateMode(false);
    setVisualEditorEvent({
      ...srv, fecha: nextFecha, activo: true, estado: 'activo',
      negocio_nombre: rawClub || activeBizName, openInEditMode: true,
    });
  }, [activeBizName]);

  const handleOpenVisualPreview = useCallback((srv: any, isExpired = false) => {
    if (!srv) return;
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    const curIso = extractLocalDatePart(srv.fecha);
    const nextFecha = isExpired && (!curIso || curIso <= getLocalISO(0)) ? getLocalISO(1) : srv.fecha;
    const rawClub = srv.negocio_nombre === 'Terminal Global Kinetic' ? 'Kinetic Oficial' : srv.negocio_nombre;
    setIsVisualCreateMode(false);
    setVisualEditorEvent({
      ...srv, fecha: nextFecha, activo: isExpired ? true : srv.activo, estado: isExpired ? 'activo' : srv.estado,
      negocio_nombre: rawClub || activeBizName, openInEditMode: isExpired, isTerminalOperational: true,
    });
  }, [activeBizName]);

  const formatDateLabel = useCallback((isoString?: string) => {
    if (!isoString) return tx('HOY', 'TODAY');
    const raw = String(isoString).trim();
    const safeIso = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(raw) ? `${raw}T12:00:00` : raw;
    const out = fmtDate(safeIso, { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short' });
    return out
      ? out.replace(/\s+de\s+/gi, ' ').replace(/[.,]/g, '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().toUpperCase()
      : raw;
  }, [fmtDate, tx]);

  const listaMostrada = useMemo(
    () => (filtroCatalogo === 'activos' ? serviciosActivos : filtroCatalogo === 'expirados' ? serviciosExpirados : [...serviciosActivos, ...serviciosExpirados]),
    [filtroCatalogo, serviciosActivos, serviciosExpirados]
  );

  const listaFiltradaCatalogo = useMemo(() => {
    const q = catalogSearch.trim().toLowerCase();
    const filtered = listaMostrada.filter((srv: any) => {
      const rec = isRecurringService(srv);
      const price = Number(srv?.precio_kc ?? 150) || 0;
      if (catalogFilter === 'once' && rec) return false;
      if (catalogFilter === 'recurring' && !rec) return false;
      if (catalogFilter === 'premium' && price < 180) return false;
      if (!q) return true;
      return `${srv?.nombre_servicio || ''} ${srv?.titulo || ''} ${srv?.categoria || ''} ${srv?.instructor_nombre || ''} ${srv?.ubicacion || ''}`.toLowerCase().includes(q);
    });
    return [...filtered].sort((a: any, b: any) => {
      const pA = Number(a?.precio_kc ?? 150) || 0, pB = Number(b?.precio_kc ?? 150) || 0;
      const cA = Number(a?.cupo_maximo ?? a?.cupo_total ?? 15) || 0;
      const cB = Number(b?.cupo_maximo ?? b?.cupo_total ?? 15) || 0;
      const nA = String(a?.nombre_servicio || a?.titulo || '');
      const nB = String(b?.nombre_servicio || b?.titulo || '');
      const dA = `${extractLocalDatePart(a?.fecha) || '9999-12-31'}T${String(a?.hora_inicio || '08:00').slice(0, 5)}`;
      const dB = `${extractLocalDatePart(b?.fecha) || '9999-12-31'}T${String(b?.hora_inicio || '08:00').slice(0, 5)}`;
      if (catalogSort === 'latest') return dB.localeCompare(dA);
      if (catalogSort === 'price_desc') return pB - pA;
      if (catalogSort === 'price_asc') return pA - pB;
      if (catalogSort === 'capacity_desc') return cB - cA || pB - pA;
      if (catalogSort === 'capacity_asc') return cA - cB || pA - pB;
      if (catalogSort === 'az') return nA.localeCompare(nB);
      if (catalogSort === 'za') return nB.localeCompare(nA);
      return dA.localeCompare(dB);
    });
  }, [listaMostrada, catalogSearch, catalogFilter, catalogSort]);

  const handlePullRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
    setFiltroCatalogo('activos'); setCatalogSearch(''); setCatalogFilter('all'); setCatalogSort('soonest'); setPendingSearchQuery('');
    scrollViewRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: 0, animated: true });
    await onRefresh();
  }, [onRefresh]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const unsubRadar = subscribeKineticRadarUpdated(() => {
      if (isScreenMounted.current && (hasBusiness || isAdminView)) {
        fetchOperacionEstadio(isAdminView ? null : activeBizName, true, isAdminView);
      }
    });
    const unsubRoot = subscribeKineticRootRefresh(() => {
      if (isScreenMounted.current) handlePullRefresh();
    });
    return () => { unsubRadar(); unsubRoot(); };
  }, [activeBizName, fetchOperacionEstadio, handlePullRefresh, hasBusiness, isAdminView, isScreenMounted]);

  if (isAuthLoading || (Boolean(user?.id) && !hasInitialLoaded)) return <TerminalScannerSkeleton topInset={insets.top} />;

  return (
    <View style={[globalStyles.mainContainer, { backgroundColor: themeColors.background }]}>
      <StatusBar barStyle={themeColors.statusBarStyle} backgroundColor={themeColors.background} />
      <View
        style={[
          tm.topBar,
          {
            paddingTop: insets.top + 14,
            backgroundColor: themeColors.background,
            borderBottomWidth: hasScrolled ? 1 : 0,
            borderBottomColor: resolvedTheme === 'light' ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.16)',
          },
        ]}
      >
        <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingRight: 10 }}>
          <Text style={[tm.headerSub, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]} numberOfLines={1} adjustsFontSizeToFit minimumFontScale={0.82}>
            {isAdminView
              ? `${tx('KINETIC OFICIAL', 'KINETIC OFFICIAL')} • ${t('terminal.global_ops')}`
              : !businessData?.nombre || String(businessData.nombre).trim().toUpperCase() === 'SEDE KINETIC'
                ? tx('SEDE KINETIC', 'KINETIC VENUE')
                : activeBizName.toUpperCase()}
          </Text>
          <Text style={[tm.headerTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{t('terminal.title')}</Text>
        </View>
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[tm.storyTopBtn, resolvedTheme === 'light' && tm.storyTopBtnLight, NO_SEL]}
          activeOpacity={0.85}
          onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Light'); setIsOperatorStoryOpen(true); }}
        >
          <PulseBadge color={COLORS.cyan} size={7} />
          <Feather name="camera" size={13} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          <Text selectable={false} style={[tm.storyTopBtnText, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{t('terminal.story_24h')}</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>

      {pendingOfflineCount > 0 && (
        <TouchableOpacity
          activeOpacity={0.85} disabled={isSyncingOffline}
          onPress={async () => {
            triggerHaptic('Warning'); setIsSyncingOffline(true);
            try {
              const count = await syncOfflineQueue((synced) => {
                triggerArcadeHaptic('checkIn');
                showTacticalAlert(tx('Cola Offline Sincronizada', 'Offline Queue Synced'), tx(`Se liquidaron ${synced} pases pendientes en Supabase.`, `${synced} pending passes settled in Supabase.`), 'success');
              });
              if (count === 0) showTacticalAlert(tx('Sin Señal de Red', 'No Network Signal'), tx('Los pases están blindados localmente y se subirán apenas vuelva la red.', 'Passes are shielded locally and will upload once online.'), 'alert');
            } finally { setIsSyncingOffline(false); }
          }}
          style={[NO_SEL, { marginHorizontal: 16, marginBottom: 8, paddingVertical: 9, paddingHorizontal: 12, borderRadius: 999, borderWidth: 1.5, borderColor: '#F59E0B', backgroundColor: resolvedTheme === 'dark' ? '#1F1608' : '#FFFBEB', elevation: 0, flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 6 }]}
        >
          <Text selectable={false} numberOfLines={1} style={{ color: resolvedTheme === 'light' ? '#B45309' : '#F59E0B', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.4, textAlign: 'center' }}>
            {tx(`⚡ MODO OFFLINE: ${pendingOfflineCount} PENDIENTES DE SUBIR (TOCA PARA SINCRONIZAR)`, `⚡ OFFLINE MODE: ${pendingOfflineCount} PENDING SYNC (TAP TO SYNC)`)}
          </Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      )}

      {!hasBusiness ? (
        <TerminalOnboardingForm nuevoSocio={nuevoSocio} setNuevoSocio={setNuevoSocio} isSaving={isSaving} onActivate={activarEstadio} onOpenCatPicker={() => setShowOnbCatPicker(true)} onOpenCityPicker={() => setShowOnbCityPicker(true)} />
      ) : (
        <ScrollView
          ref={scrollViewRef} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
          scrollEventThrottle={16}
          onScrollBeginDrag={() => dismissOpenFilterSortMenus()}
          onScroll={(e) => {
            dismissOpenFilterSortMenus();
            const scrolled = e.nativeEvent.contentOffset.y > 4;
            setHasScrolled((prev) => (prev === scrolled ? prev : scrolled));
          }}
          contentContainerStyle={[tm.scrollContent, { paddingBottom: insets.bottom + 120 }]}
          refreshControl={<RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={handlePullRefresh} tintColor="#00F0FF" colors={['#00F0FF']} progressBackgroundColor={resolvedTheme === 'light' ? '#FFFFFF' : '#080C12'} />}
        >
          {refreshing && (
            <View style={[tm.refreshBanner, resolvedTheme === 'light' && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#06B6D4', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#00B8D4' }]}>
              <Animated.View style={{ transform: [{ rotate: refreshClockwiseSpin }] }}><Feather name="zap" size={15} color="#00F0FF" /></Animated.View>
              <Text selectable={false} style={[tm.refreshBannerTxt, { color: resolvedTheme === 'light' ? '#006B82' : '#00F0FF' }]}>{tx('Sincronizando Terminal en vivo...', 'Syncing Terminal live...')}</Text>
            </View>
          )}

          <TerminalDashboardHeroCards
            ingresosHoy={ingresosHoy} completedCount={reservasCompletadasList.length} pendingCount={validPasesActivos.length}
            onOpenSheet={(sheet) => { if (sheet === 'pases_espera') setPendingSearchQuery(''); setActiveTerminalSheet(sheet); }}
            onActivateScanner={handleActivarLente}
          />

          <TerminalCatalogAndHistorySection
            businessName={activeBizName} serviciosActivos={serviciosActivos} serviciosExpirados={serviciosExpirados}
            listaMostrada={listaMostrada} listaFiltradaCatalogo={listaFiltradaCatalogo}
            filtroCatalogo={filtroCatalogo} setFiltroCatalogo={setFiltroCatalogo}
            catalogSearch={catalogSearch} setCatalogSearch={setCatalogSearch}
            catalogFilter={catalogFilter} setCatalogFilter={setCatalogFilter}
            catalogSort={catalogSort} setCatalogSort={setCatalogSort}
            formatDateLabel={formatDateLabel} onCreateEvent={handleOpenVisualCreator}
            onPreviewEvent={handleOpenVisualPreview} onEditEvent={handleOpenVisualEditor}
            onOpenLiveRoom={(srv) => setLiveRoomService(srv)}
            pasesEnEspera={validPasesActivos}
            historialCompleto={historialCompleto} filtroBitacora={filtroBitacora} setFiltroBitacora={setFiltroBitacora}
            isHistoryExpanded={isHistoryExpanded} setIsHistoryExpanded={setIsHistoryExpanded}
            onSelectAuditItem={(item) => setSelectedAuditItem(item)}
          />
        </ScrollView>
      )}

      <TerminalBottomSheetModal activeSheet={activeTerminalSheet} onClose={() => setActiveTerminalSheet(null)} businessData={businessData} reservasCompletadasList={reservasCompletadasList} pasesActivos={validPasesActivos} onSelectAuditItem={(item) => setSelectedAuditItem(item)} pendingSearchQuery={pendingSearchQuery} setPendingSearchQuery={setPendingSearchQuery} />
      <TerminalLiveStudioRoomModal
        visible={Boolean(liveRoomService)} service={liveRoomService} localActivePasses={validPasesActivos} localCompletedPasses={reservasCompletadasList}
        onClose={() => setLiveRoomService(null)}
        onOpenConfigure3D={(srv) => {
          setLiveRoomService(null);
          if (modalBridgeTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(modalBridgeTimerRef.current);
          modalBridgeTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
            const rawClub = srv?.negocio_nombre === 'Terminal Global Kinetic' ? 'Kinetic Oficial' : srv?.negocio_nombre;
            setIsVisualCreateMode(false);
            setVisualEditorEvent({ ...srv, activo: true, estado: 'activo', negocio_nombre: rawClub || activeBizName, openInEditMode: true, openStudioConfigOnMount: true });
          }, 240);
        }}
      />
      <ScannerModal isVisible={showScanner} onClose={closeScannerModal} isProcessing={isProcessing} onBarcodeScanned={handleBarCodeScanned} />
      <KineticStoriesModal visible={isOperatorStoryOpen} owner={operatorStoryOwner} onClose={() => setIsOperatorStoryOpen(false)} onStoriesUpdated={() => emitKineticStoriesUpdated()} onOpenLinkedEvent={(ev) => { setIsOperatorStoryOpen(false); if (modalBridgeTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(modalBridgeTimerRef.current); modalBridgeTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => handleOpenVisualPreview(ev, false), 240); }} />
      <TerminalAuditModal selectedAuditItem={selectedAuditItem} setSelectedAuditItem={setSelectedAuditItem} isAdminView={isAdminView} />
      <TerminalSuccessModal showSuccessModal={Boolean(showSuccessModal && !showScanner)} setShowSuccessModal={setShowSuccessModal} successDetails={successDetails} pulseAnim={pulseAnim} />
      <EventDetailsModal visible={Boolean(visualEditorEvent)} event={visualEditorEvent} isCreateMode={isVisualCreateMode} defaultClubName={activeBizName} onClose={() => { setVisualEditorEvent(null); setIsVisualCreateMode(false); }} onRefreshEvents={() => { setFiltroCatalogo('activos'); fetchOperacionEstadio(isAdminView || businessData?.nombre === 'Terminal Global Kinetic' ? null : activeBizName, true, isAdminView); }} />
      <TerminalOnboardingSelector showOnbCityPicker={showOnbCityPicker} showOnbCatPicker={showOnbCatPicker} setShowOnbCityPicker={setShowOnbCityPicker} setShowOnbCatPicker={setShowOnbCatPicker} nuevoSocio={nuevoSocio} setNuevoSocio={setNuevoSocio} CIUDADES_PILOTO={CIUDADES_PILOTO} CATEGORY_OPTIONS={CATEGORY_OPTIONS} triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic} />
      <TacticalAlertModal alertConfig={alertConfig} setAlertConfig={setAlertConfig} triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic} />
    </View>
  );
}

    FILE: src/components/ScannerModal.styles.ts

    gap: 8,
    backgroundColor: '#091318',
    borderWidth: 1.2,
    borderColor: '#1E293B',
    borderTopWidth: 1.6,
    borderTopColor: '#334155',
    borderBottomWidth: 3.5,
    borderBottomColor: '#020617',
    paddingHorizontal: 14,

    borderBottomWidth: 4.5,
    borderBottomColor: '#006B78',
    justifyContent: 'center',

    gap: 8,
    shadowColor: COLORS.cyan,
    shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 5 },
    shadowOpacity: 0.3,
    shadowRadius: 10,

export const pressScanner3D = (pressed: boolean, baseLip = 4.5) =>
  pressed ? { borderBottomWidth: 1.5, transform: [{ translateY: Math.max(1.5, baseLip - 2) }] } : null;

export const getTotpSecondsLeft = () =>
  60 - Math.floor((((Date.now() + getLocalServerClockOffsetMs()) % 60_000) + 60_000) % 60_000 / 1000);

export const buildScannerPalette = (isLightMode: boolean, offlineActive: boolean, totpUrgent: boolean) => {
  const cyanInk = isLightMode ? '#0E7490' : COLORS.cyan;
  return {
    cyanInk,
    bgSurface: isLightMode ? '#F8FAFC' : '#040508',
    cardBg: isLightMode ? '#FFFFFF' : '#091318',
    textMain: isLightMode ? '#0F172A' : '#FFFFFF',
    textSub: isLightMode ? '#334155' : '#CBD5E1',
    totpAccent: totpUrgent ? (isLightMode ? '#D97706' : COLORS.yellow) : cyanInk,
    netPillStyle: offlineActive
      ? isLightMode
        ? { backgroundColor: '#FEF3C7', borderColor: '#F59E0B', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#B45309' }
        : { backgroundColor: '#291A04', borderColor: '#F59E0B', borderTopColor: '#FDE68A', borderBottomColor: '#92400E' }
      : isLightMode
        ? { backgroundColor: '#ECFDF5', borderColor: '#10B981', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#047857' }
        : { backgroundColor: '#062415', borderColor: COLORS.green, borderTopColor: '#86EFAC', borderBottomColor: '#00752E' },
    netInk: offlineActive ? (isLightMode ? '#B45309' : '#FDE68A') : (isLightMode ? '#047857' : COLORS.green),
    lightCyanCard: isLightMode ? { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#67E8F9', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0891B2' } : null,
  };
};

    FILE: src/components/ScannerModal.tsx

    // === src/components/ScannerModal.tsx === //
import { Feather, Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { CameraView, useCameraPermissions } from 'expo-camera';
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Animated,
  AppState,
  Easing,
  Modal,
  Platform,
  Pressable,
  ScrollView,
  StatusBar,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { COLORS } from '../styles/globalStyles';
import { buildScannerPalette, getTotpSecondsLeft, pressScanner3D as press3D, styles, VIEWFINDER_SIZE } from './ScannerModal.styles';
import { KineticLoader, triggerHaptic } from './SharedUI';

const getInitialOnline = () =>
  Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && typeof navigator.onLine === 'boolean'
    ? navigator.onLine
    : true;

export const ScannerModal = React.memo(
  ({
    visible,
    isVisible,
    onClose,
    onScanSuccess,
    onBarcodeScanned,
    isProcessing = false,
    isOffline = false,
  }: any) => {
    const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
    const { isLightMode, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
    const modalOpen = Boolean(visible ?? isVisible);
    const [permission, requestPermission] = useCameraPermissions();
    const [torchEnabled, setTorchEnabled] = useState(false);
    const [cameraReady, setCameraReady] = useState(false);
    const [cameraMountFailed, setCameraMountFailed] = useState(false);
    const [mountCamera, setMountCamera] = useState(false);
    const [cameraKey, setCameraKey] = useState(0);
    const [isClosingScanner, setIsClosingScanner] = useState(false);
    const [totpSec, setTotpSec] = useState(getTotpSecondsLeft);
    const [netOnline, setNetOnline] = useState(getInitialOnline);
    const scanLockRef = useRef(false);
    const isProcessingRef = useRef(Boolean(isProcessing));
    isProcessingRef.current = Boolean(isProcessing);
    const permissionGrantedRef = useRef(Boolean(permission?.granted));
    permissionGrantedRef.current = Boolean(permission?.granted);
    const requestPermissionRef = useRef(requestPermission);
    requestPermissionRef.current = requestPermission;
    const wasModalOpenRef = useRef(false);
    const mountRetryCountRef = useRef(0);
    const mountDelayTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
    const mountRetryTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
    const torchResyncTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
    const torchResyncRafRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
    const lastScanRef = useRef<{ code: string; ts: number }>({ code: '', ts: 0 });
    const scanUnlockTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
    const laserAnim = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;

    const clearScannerTimers = useCallback(() => {
      if (scanUnlockTimerRef.current) { clearTimeout(scanUnlockTimerRef.current); scanUnlockTimerRef.current = null; }
      if (mountDelayTimerRef.current) { clearTimeout(mountDelayTimerRef.current); mountDelayTimerRef.current = null; }
      if (mountRetryTimerRef.current) { clearTimeout(mountRetryTimerRef.current); mountRetryTimerRef.current = null; }
      if (torchResyncTimerRef.current) { clearTimeout(torchResyncTimerRef.current); torchResyncTimerRef.current = null; }
      if (torchResyncRafRef.current != null) { cancelAnimationFrame(torchResyncRafRef.current); torchResyncRafRef.current = null; }
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => clearScannerTimers, [clearScannerTimers]);

    useEffect(() => {
      if (!isProcessing && modalOpen && scanLockRef.current) {
        if (scanUnlockTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(scanUnlockTimerRef.current);
        scanUnlockTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
          scanLockRef.current = false;
        }, 900);
      }
    }, [isProcessing, modalOpen]);

    useEffect(() => {
      if (modalOpen) {
        setIsClosingScanner(false);
        if (wasModalOpenRef.current) return;
        wasModalOpenRef.current = true;
        clearScannerTimers();
        mountRetryCountRef.current = 0;
        scanLockRef.current = false;
        lastScanRef.current = { code: '', ts: 0 };
        setTorchEnabled(false);
        setCameraReady(false);
        setCameraMountFailed(false);
        setTotpSec(getTotpSecondsLeft());
        setNetOnline(getInitialOnline());
        if (!permissionGrantedRef.current) {
          requestPermissionRef.current?.().catch(() => {});
        }
        mountDelayTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
          mountDelayTimerRef.current = null;
          setCameraKey((prev) => prev + 1);
          setMountCamera(true);
        }, 80);
        return () => {
          if (mountDelayTimerRef.current) {
            clearTimeout(mountDelayTimerRef.current);
            mountDelayTimerRef.current = null;
          }
        };
      }
      wasModalOpenRef.current = false;
      clearScannerTimers();
      mountRetryCountRef.current = 0;
      scanLockRef.current = false;
      setMountCamera(false);
      setCameraReady(false);
      setCameraMountFailed(false);
      setTorchEnabled(false);
      setIsClosingScanner(false);
    }, [modalOpen, clearScannerTimers]);

    useEffect(() => {
      if (!modalOpen) return;
      const iv = setInterval(() => setTotpSec(getTotpSecondsLeft()), 1000);
      return () => clearInterval(iv);
    }, [modalOpen]);

    useEffect(() => {
      if (!modalOpen || Platform.OS !== 'web' || typeof window === 'undefined') return;
      const syncNet = () => setNetOnline(navigator.onLine);
      syncNet();
      window.addEventListener('online', syncNet);
      window.addEventListener('offline', syncNet);
      return () => {
        window.removeEventListener('online', syncNet);
        window.removeEventListener('offline', syncNet);
      };
    }, [modalOpen]);

    useEffect(() => {
      if (!modalOpen || Platform.OS === 'web') return;
      const sub = AppState.addEventListener('change', (nextState) => {
        if (nextState !== 'active') {
          clearScannerTimers();
          setTorchEnabled(false);
          setCameraReady(false);
          setMountCamera(false);
        } else if (wasModalOpenRef.current) {
          clearScannerTimers();
          setCameraMountFailed(false);
          mountDelayTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
            mountDelayTimerRef.current = null;
            setCameraKey((prev) => prev + 1);
            setMountCamera(true);
          }, 80);
        }
      });
      return () => sub.remove();
    }, [modalOpen, clearScannerTimers]);

    useEffect(() => {
      if (!modalOpen) return;
      laserAnim.setValue(0);
      const useNat = Platform.OS !== 'web';
      const loop = Animated.loop(
        Animated.sequence([
          Animated.timing(laserAnim, { toValue: 1, duration: 1850, easing: Easing.inOut(Easing.quad), useNativeDriver: useNat }),
          Animated.timing(laserAnim, { toValue: 0, duration: 1850, easing: Easing.inOut(Easing.quad), useNativeDriver: useNat }),
        ])
      );
      loop.start();
      return () => loop.stop();
    }, [modalOpen, laserAnim]);

    const handleBarcodeRead = useCallback((result: any) => {
      if (scanLockRef.current || isProcessingRef.current) return;
      const rawCandidate =
        typeof result === 'string'
          ? result
          : typeof result?.data === 'string'
            ? result.data
            : '';
      const rawData = rawCandidate.trim();
      if (!rawData) return;
      const now = Date.now();
      if (lastScanRef.current.code === rawData && now - lastScanRef.current.ts < 3200) return;
      scanLockRef.current = true;
      lastScanRef.current = { code: rawData, ts: now };
      triggerHaptic('Success');
      const callback = onScanSuccess || onBarcodeScanned;
      try {
        if (callback) {
          callback(rawData);
        }
      } catch {
        scanLockRef.current = false;
      } finally {
        if (scanUnlockTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(scanUnlockTimerRef.current);
        scanUnlockTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
          if (!isProcessingRef.current) {
            scanLockRef.current = false;
          }
        }, 2600);
      }
    }, [onScanSuccess, onBarcodeScanned]);

    useEffect(() => {
      if (Platform.OS !== 'web' || !modalOpen || typeof window === 'undefined') return;
      const onOpticalFrame = (ev: any) => {
        const payload = ev?.detail?.data;
        if (payload) handleBarcodeRead({ data: String(payload) });
      };
      window.addEventListener('kinetic:optical-qr-frame', onOpticalFrame);
      return () => window.removeEventListener('kinetic:optical-qr-frame', onOpticalFrame);
    }, [modalOpen, handleBarcodeRead]);

    const handleSafeCloseScanner = useCallback(() => {
      clearScannerTimers();
      setIsClosingScanner(true);
      setMountCamera(false);
      setCameraReady(false);
      setTorchEnabled(false);
      triggerHaptic('Light');
      onClose?.();
    }, [onClose, clearScannerTimers]);

    const handleToggleTorch = useCallback(() => {
      triggerHaptic('Selection');
      if (torchResyncRafRef.current != null) { cancelAnimationFrame(torchResyncRafRef.current); torchResyncRafRef.current = null; }
      if (torchResyncTimerRef.current) { clearTimeout(torchResyncTimerRef.current); torchResyncTimerRef.current = null; }
      if (torchEnabled && Platform.OS === 'android') {
        setTorchEnabled(false);
        setCameraReady(false);
        torchResyncRafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
          torchResyncRafRef.current = null;
          torchResyncTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
            torchResyncTimerRef.current = null;
            if (wasModalOpenRef.current) setCameraKey((prev) => prev + 1);
          }, 40);
        });
        return;
      }
      setTorchEnabled((prev) => !prev);
    }, [torchEnabled]);

    const laserTranslateY = laserAnim.interpolate({
      inputRange: [0, 1],
      outputRange: [12, VIEWFINDER_SIZE - 16],
    });

    if (!modalOpen || isClosingScanner) return null;

    const isOpticalVideoLive = Boolean(permission?.granted && cameraReady && mountCamera && !cameraMountFailed && !isClosingScanner);

    const offlineActive = Boolean(isOffline) || !netOnline;
    const totpUrgent = totpSec <= 10;
    const {
      cyanInk, bgSurface, cardBg, textMain, textSub, totpAccent, netPillStyle, netInk, lightCyanCard,
    } = buildScannerPalette(isLightMode, offlineActive, totpUrgent);

    return (
      <Modal
        visible={modalOpen && !isClosingScanner}
        transparent={false}
        animationType="fade"
        statusBarTranslucent
        onRequestClose={handleSafeCloseScanner}
      >
        <View style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: bgSurface }]}>
          <StatusBar barStyle={isLightMode ? 'dark-content' : 'light-content'} backgroundColor={bgSurface} />
          <ScrollView
            contentContainerStyle={[
              styles.scrollContent,
              { paddingTop: Math.max(insets.top, 20) + 12, paddingBottom: insets.bottom > 0 ? insets.bottom + 18 : 26 },
            ]}
            showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
            bounces={false}
          >
            <View style={styles.headerBlock}>
              <View style={styles.topBadgesRow}>
                <View style={[styles.topPillBadge, isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#06B6D4', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0891B2' }]}>
                  <Ionicons name="flash" size={12} color={cyanInk} />
                  <Text style={[styles.topPillText, { color: cyanInk }]}>{tx('TERMINAL ÓPTICA KINETIC', 'KINETIC OPTICAL TERMINAL')}</Text>
                </View>
                {offlineActive && (
                  <View style={[styles.topPillBadge, netPillStyle]}>
                    <Feather name="wifi-off" size={11} color={netInk} />
                    <Text style={[styles.topPillText, { color: netInk }]}>{tx('RESPALDO OFFLINE ACTIVO', 'OFFLINE BACKUP ACTIVE')}</Text>
                  </View>
                )}
                <View style={[styles.topPillBadge, isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#F0FDF4', borderColor: '#10B981', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#047857' }]}>
                  <Feather name="lock" size={11} color={isLightMode ? '#047857' : COLORS.green} />
                  <Text style={[styles.topPillText, { color: isLightMode ? '#047857' : COLORS.green }]}>{tx('NTP TOTP 60s • Anti-Captura Activo', '60s NTP TOTP • Anti-Screenshot Active')}</Text>
                </View>
              </View>

              <Text style={[styles.mainTitle, { color: textMain }]}>{tx('VALIDACIÓN DE LLAVE QR', 'DYNAMIC QR PASS VALIDATION')}</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.subDescription, { color: textSub }]}>
                {tx('Encuadra el código QR dinámico vivo del atleta dentro del visor óptico para validar su acceso.', "Frame the athlete's live dynamic QR code inside the optical viewfinder to validate access.")}
              </Text>

              {offlineActive && (
                <View style={[styles.offlineBanner, isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#FEF3C7', borderColor: '#F59E0B', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#B45309' }]}>
                  <Feather name="shield" size={14} color={isLightMode ? '#B45309' : '#FDE68A'} />
                  <Text style={[styles.offlineBannerText, isLightMode && { color: '#92400E' }]}>
                    {tx(
                      'Sin red detectada: Respaldo Offline Activo para validación criptográfica TOTP y sincronización automática.',
                      'No network detected: Offline Backup Active for cryptographic TOTP validation and auto-sync.'
                    )}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              )}

              <View style={[styles.totpCycleCard, lightCyanCard]}>
                <View style={styles.totpHeaderRow}>
                  <Text style={[styles.totpLabel, { color: totpAccent }]}>{tx('CICLO DINÁMICO DE SEGURIDAD', 'DYNAMIC SECURITY CYCLE')}</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.totpTimerText, { color: textMain }]}>{tx(`Rotación en ${totpSec}s`, `Rotates in ${totpSec}s`)}</Text>
                </View>
                <View style={[styles.totpTrack, isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#E2E8F0' }]}>
                  <View style={[styles.totpFill, { width: `${Math.max(4, Math.round((totpSec / 60) * 100))}%`, backgroundColor: totpUrgent ? COLORS.yellow : COLORS.cyan }]} />
                </View>
              </View>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.centerSection}>
              {!isClosingScanner && (
                <View
                  {...(Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ dataSet: { kineticOnMedia: 'true' } } as any) : {})}
                  collapsable={false}
                  style={[styles.viewfinderWrapper, { width: VIEWFINDER_SIZE, height: VIEWFINDER_SIZE, backgroundColor: '#0A0D14' }, isLightMode && { borderColor: '#0891B2' }]}
                >
                  {permission?.granted ? (
                    <>
                      {mountCamera && !cameraMountFailed && (
                        <CameraView
                          key={`kinetic_native_cam_${cameraKey}`}
                          style={styles.cameraSurface}
                          facing="back"
                          zoom={0}
                          autofocus="on"
                          enableTorch={torchEnabled}
                          barcodeScannerSettings={{ barcodeTypes: ['qr'] }}
                          onCameraReady={() => {
                            mountRetryCountRef.current = 0;
                            setCameraMountFailed(false);
                            setCameraReady(true);
                          }}
                          onMountError={() => {
                            setCameraReady(false);
                            if (mountRetryTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(mountRetryTimerRef.current);
                            if (mountRetryCountRef.current < 2) {
                              mountRetryCountRef.current += 1;
                              mountRetryTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
                                if (wasModalOpenRef.current) {
                                  setCameraKey((prev) => prev + 1);
                                }
                              }, 350);
                            } else {
                              setCameraMountFailed(true);
                            }
                          }}
                          onBarcodeScanned={handleBarcodeRead}
                        />
                      )}
                      {(!cameraReady || !mountCamera || cameraMountFailed) && (
                        <View style={[styles.cameraWarmupBox, StyleSheet.absoluteFill, { paddingHorizontal: 18 }]}>
                          {cameraMountFailed ? (
                            <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
                              <Feather name="alert-circle" size={26} color={COLORS.yellow} />
                              <Text style={{ color: '#E2E8F0', fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '700', textAlign: 'center' }}>
                                {tx('No se pudo iniciar la cámara. Verifica permisos o reintenta.', 'Could not start camera. Check permissions or retry.')}
                              </Text>
                              <Pressable
                                style={({ pressed }) => [styles.grantPermBtn, press3D(pressed, 4)]}
                                onPress={() => {
                                  mountRetryCountRef.current = 0;
                                  setCameraMountFailed(false);
                                  setCameraReady(false);
                                  setCameraKey((p) => p + 1);
                                }}
                              >
                                <Text style={styles.grantPermBtnText}>{tx('REINTENTAR CÁMARA', 'RETRY CAMERA')}</Text>
                              </Pressable>
                            </View>
                          ) : (
                            <>
                              <KineticLoader size={26} color={COLORS.cyan} />
                              <Text style={styles.warmupHintText}>
                                {tx('Inicializando lente óptico...', 'Initializing optical lens...')}
                              </Text>
                            </>
                          )}
                        </View>
                      )}
                      <View pointerEvents="none" style={styles.opticalAfChip}>
                        <Text style={styles.opticalAfChipText}>
                          {tx('1.0x ÓPTICO • AF', '1.0x OPTICAL • AF')}
                        </Text>
                      </View>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <View style={styles.permissionFallbackBox}>
                      <Feather name="camera-off" size={30} color={COLORS.cyan} style={{ marginBottom: 8 }} />
                      <Text style={styles.permissionTitle}>{tx('ACCESO A CÁMARA REQUERIDO', 'CAMERA ACCESS REQUIRED')}</Text>
                      <Text style={styles.permissionSub}>
                        {tx('Habilita la cámara para escanear llaves Pase QR en puerta.', 'Enable camera access to scan dynamic QR passes at front desk.')}
                      </Text>
                      <Pressable style={({ pressed }) => [styles.grantPermBtn, press3D(pressed, 4.5)]} onPress={() => requestPermission()}>
                        <Text style={styles.grantPermBtnText}>{tx('PERMITIR CÁMARA', 'ALLOW CAMERA')}</Text>
                      </Pressable>
                    </View>
                  )}

                  <View pointerEvents="none" style={[styles.corner, styles.cornerTL]} />
                  <View pointerEvents="none" style={[styles.corner, styles.cornerTR]} />
                  <View pointerEvents="none" style={[styles.corner, styles.cornerBL]} />
                  <View pointerEvents="none" style={[styles.corner, styles.cornerBR]} />

                  {isOpticalVideoLive && (
                    <Animated.View pointerEvents="none" style={[styles.laserLine, { transform: [{ translateY: laserTranslateY }] }]} />
                  )}

                  {isProcessing && (
                    <View style={styles.processingOverlay}>
                      <KineticLoader size={30} color={COLORS.cyan} />
                      <Text style={styles.processingText}>{tx('VALIDANDO PASE QR DE ACCESO...', 'VALIDATING QR ACCESS PASS...')}</Text>
                    </View>
                  )}
                </View>
              )}

              <View style={[styles.sensorStatusPill, { backgroundColor: cardBg }, isLightMode && { borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' }]}>
                <View style={[styles.sensorDot, { backgroundColor: isOpticalVideoLive ? COLORS.green : COLORS.yellow }]} />
                <Text style={[styles.sensorStatusText, { color: textMain }]}>
                  {isOpticalVideoLive
                    ? tx('SENSOR ÓPTICO ACTIVO • ANTI-DOBLE LECTURA', 'OPTICAL SENSOR ACTIVE • ANTI-DUPLICATE LOCK')
                    : tx('INICIALIZANDO SENSOR ÓPTICO...', 'INITIALIZING OPTICAL SENSOR...')}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>

            <View style={[styles.footerControls, { paddingBottom: Math.max(insets.bottom + 28, Platform.OS === 'android' ? 48 : 24) }]}>
              <View style={[styles.securityNoteBox, { backgroundColor: cardBg }, isLightMode && { borderColor: '#BAE6FD', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7' }]}>
                <Feather name="shield" size={14} color={cyanInk} />
                <Text style={[styles.securityNoteText, { color: textSub }]}>
                  {tx(
                    'Protocolo Anti-Fraude: Liquidación protegida en recepción exclusivamente mediante lectura óptica de llave QR dinámica.',
                    'Anti-Fraud Protocol: Protected check-in at reception exclusively via optical scan of dynamic QR key.'
                  )}
                </Text>
              </View>
              <View style={styles.bottomButtonsRow}>
                <Pressable
                  style={({ pressed }) => [
                    styles.controlBtn,
                    { backgroundColor: torchEnabled ? COLORS.cyan : (isLightMode ? '#ECFEFF' : '#091318') },
                    isLightMode && !torchEnabled && { borderColor: '#06B6D4', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0891B2' },
                    torchEnabled && styles.controlBtnTorchActive,
                    press3D(pressed, 4.5),
                  ]}
                  onPress={handleToggleTorch}
                >
                  <Ionicons name={torchEnabled ? 'flash' : 'flash-outline'} size={16} color={torchEnabled ? '#050505' : cyanInk} />
                  <Text style={[styles.controlBtnText, { color: torchEnabled ? '#050505' : cyanInk }]}>
                    {torchEnabled ? tx('LINTERNA ACTIVA', 'TORCH ON') : tx('LINTERNA', 'TORCH')}
                  </Text>
                </Pressable>
                <Pressable
                  style={({ pressed }) => [
                    styles.closeScannerRedBtn,
                    { backgroundColor: '#B91C1C', borderTopColor: '#FCA5A5', borderBottomColor: '#7F1D1D' },
                    press3D(pressed, 4.5),
                  ]}
                  onPress={handleSafeCloseScanner}
                >
                  <Feather name="power" size={15} color="#FFFFFF" />
                  <Text style={[styles.closeScannerRedText, { color: '#FFFFFF' }]}>{tx('APAGAR ESCÁNER', 'CLOSE SCANNER')}</Text>
                </Pressable>
              </View>
            </View>
          </ScrollView>
        </View>
      </Modal>
    );
  }
);

export default ScannerModal;

    FILE: src/components/terminal/TerminalBottomSheetModal.tsx

    // === src/components/terminal/TerminalBottomSheetModal.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform, Pressable, ScrollView, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { baseTerminalStyles } from '../../styles/terminal.styles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { KineticListFilterSortBar } from '../shared/KineticListFilterSortBar';
import { KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from '../shared/KineticModalHeader3D';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { TerminalLiquidityAnalyticsView } from '../TerminalActionModals';

export {
  TerminalDashboardHeroCards,
  TerminalOnboardingForm,
  TerminalScannerSkeleton,
} from './TerminalRosterAndCards';

export type TerminalSheetType = null | 'liquidez' | 'completados' | 'pases_espera';

const NO_SEL = Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as any) : null;
const press3D = (pressed: boolean, baseLip = 4.5) =>
  pressed ? { borderBottomWidth: 1.5, transform: [{ translateY: Math.max(1.5, baseLip - 2) }] } : null;

const isMasterPassRow = (item: any) => {
  const costo = Number(item?.costo_total ?? item?.precio_kc ?? 0) || 0;
  const puerta = item?.monto_puerta != null && item?.monto_puerta !== '' ? Number(item.monto_puerta) || 0 : Math.floor(costo * 0.6);
  const escrow = item?.monto_escrow != null && item?.monto_escrow !== '' ? Number(item.monto_escrow) || 0 : Math.max(0, costo - puerta);
  return Boolean(item?.isMasterPass || item?.es_pase_maestro) || (costo === 0 && puerta === 0 && escrow === 0);
};

const getTodayIsoLocal = () => {
  const n = new Date();
  return `${n.getFullYear()}-${String(n.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(n.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
};

interface TerminalBottomSheetModalProps {
  activeSheet: TerminalSheetType;
  onClose: () => void;
  businessData?: any;
  reservasCompletadasList: any[];
  pasesActivos: any[];
  onSelectAuditItem: (item: any) => void;
  pendingSearchQuery?: string;
  setPendingSearchQuery?: (q: string) => void;
}

export const TerminalBottomSheetModal = memo(({
  activeSheet, onClose, businessData, reservasCompletadasList = [], pasesActivos = [],
  onSelectAuditItem, pendingSearchQuery, setPendingSearchQuery,
}: TerminalBottomSheetModalProps) => {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, tx, td, fmtDate, fmtDateTime } = useKineticPreferences();
  const tm = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseTerminalStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);

  const lastSheetRef = useRef<TerminalSheetType>(null);
  if (activeSheet) lastSheetRef.current = activeSheet;
  const displaySheet = activeSheet ?? lastSheetRef.current;
  const auditTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => () => {
    if (auditTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(auditTimerRef.current);
  }, []);

  const [completedSearch, setCompletedSearch] = useState('');
  const [completedFilter, setCompletedFilter] = useState<'all' | 'today' | '7d' | '30d'>('all');
  const [completedSort, setCompletedSort] = useState<'recent' | 'oldest' | 'kc_desc' | 'kc_asc' | 'az' | 'za'>('recent');

  const [localPendingSearch, setLocalPendingSearch] = useState('');
  const [pendingFilter, setPendingFilter] = useState<'all' | 'today' | '7d' | '30d'>('all');
  const [pendingSort, setPendingSort] = useState<'soonest' | 'latest' | 'kc_desc' | 'kc_asc' | 'az' | 'za'>('soonest');

  const effectivePendingSearch = typeof pendingSearchQuery === 'string' ? pendingSearchQuery : localPendingSearch;
  const handleChangePendingSearch = (val: string) => { setLocalPendingSearch(val); setPendingSearchQuery?.(val); };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeSheet) {
      setCompletedSearch('');
      setCompletedFilter('all');
      setCompletedSort('recent');
      setPendingFilter('all');
      setPendingSort('soonest');
    }
  }, [activeSheet]);

  const todayIso = useMemo(() => getTodayIsoLocal(), []);
  const weekAheadIso = useMemo(() => {
    const d = new Date();
    d.setDate(d.getDate() + 7);
    return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  }, []);
  const monthAheadIso = useMemo(() => {
    const d = new Date();
    d.setDate(d.getDate() + 30);
    return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  }, []);

  const resolveAthleteName = (item: any) =>
    (Array.isArray(item?.perfiles) ? item?.perfiles[0]?.nombre : item?.perfiles?.nombre) ||
    item?.atleta_nombre ||
    item?.nombre_atleta ||
    '';

  const completedCounts = useMemo(() => {
    const sevenDaysAgo = Date.now() - 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
    const thirtyDaysAgo = Date.now() - 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
    let today = 0, week = 0, month = 0;
    (reservasCompletadasList || []).forEach((item: any) => {
      const raw = String(item?.updated_at || item?.fecha_clase || item?.created_at || '');
      const ts = raw ? new Date(raw).getTime() : 0;
      if (raw.startsWith(todayIso)) today += 1;
      if (ts >= sevenDaysAgo) week += 1;
      if (ts >= thirtyDaysAgo) month += 1;
    });
    return { all: reservasCompletadasList.length, today, week, month };
  }, [reservasCompletadasList, todayIso]);

  const filteredCompleted = useMemo(() => {
    const q = completedSearch.trim().toLowerCase();
    const sevenDaysAgo = Date.now() - 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
    const thirtyDaysAgo = Date.now() - 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
    const list = (reservasCompletadasList || []).filter((item: any) => {
      const raw = String(item?.updated_at || item?.fecha_clase || item?.created_at || '');
      const ts = raw ? new Date(raw).getTime() : 0;
      if (completedFilter === 'today' && !raw.startsWith(todayIso)) return false;
      if (completedFilter === '7d' && ts < sevenDaysAgo) return false;
      if (completedFilter === '30d' && ts < thirtyDaysAgo) return false;
      if (!q) return true;
      const athlete = resolveAthleteName(item);
      const folio = String(item?.id || '').replace(/-/g, '').substring(0, 8);
      return `${item?.servicio || ''} ${athlete} ${folio}`.toLowerCase().includes(q);
    });
    return [...list].sort((a: any, b: any) => {
      const tsA = new Date(a?.updated_at || a?.created_at || 0).getTime() || 0;
      const tsB = new Date(b?.updated_at || b?.created_at || 0).getTime() || 0;
      const kcA = Number(a?.monto_puerta) || 0;
      const kcB = Number(b?.monto_puerta) || 0;
      const nA = resolveAthleteName(a);
      const nB = resolveAthleteName(b);
      if (completedSort === 'oldest') return tsA - tsB;
      if (completedSort === 'kc_desc') return kcB - kcA;
      if (completedSort === 'kc_asc') return kcA - kcB;
      if (completedSort === 'az') return nA.localeCompare(nB);
      if (completedSort === 'za') return nB.localeCompare(nA);
      return tsB - tsA;
    });
  }, [reservasCompletadasList, completedSearch, completedFilter, completedSort, todayIso]);

  const pendingCounts = useMemo(() => {
    let today = 0, week = 0, month = 0;
    (pasesActivos || []).forEach((item: any) => {
      const raw = String(item?.fecha_clase || item?.created_at || '').split('T')[0];
      if (raw === todayIso) today += 1;
      if (raw >= todayIso && raw <= weekAheadIso) week += 1;
      if (raw >= todayIso && raw <= monthAheadIso) month += 1;
    });
    return { all: pasesActivos.length, today, week, month };
  }, [pasesActivos, todayIso, weekAheadIso, monthAheadIso]);

  const filteredPending = useMemo(() => {
    const q = effectivePendingSearch.trim().toLowerCase();
    const list = (pasesActivos || []).filter((item: any) => {
      const raw = String(item?.fecha_clase || item?.created_at || '').split('T')[0];
      if (pendingFilter === 'today' && raw !== todayIso) return false;
      if (pendingFilter === '7d' && !(raw >= todayIso && raw <= weekAheadIso)) return false;
      if (pendingFilter === '30d' && !(raw >= todayIso && raw <= monthAheadIso)) return false;
      if (!q) return true;
      const athlete = resolveAthleteName(item);
      const folio = String(item?.id || '').replace(/-/g, '').substring(0, 8);
      return `${item?.servicio || ''} ${athlete} ${folio} ${item?.id || ''}`.toLowerCase().includes(q);
    });
    return [...list].sort((a: any, b: any) => {
      const schedA = `${String(a?.fecha_clase || a?.created_at || '').split('T')[0]}T${String(a?.hora_inicio || a?.hora || '08:00').slice(0, 5)}`;
      const schedB = `${String(b?.fecha_clase || b?.created_at || '').split('T')[0]}T${String(b?.hora_inicio || b?.hora || '08:00').slice(0, 5)}`;
      const kcA = Number(a?.monto_puerta) || 0;
      const kcB = Number(b?.monto_puerta) || 0;
      const nA = resolveAthleteName(a);
      const nB = resolveAthleteName(b);
      if (pendingSort === 'latest') return schedB.localeCompare(schedA);
      if (pendingSort === 'kc_desc') return kcB - kcA;
      if (pendingSort === 'kc_asc') return kcA - kcB;
      if (pendingSort === 'az') return nA.localeCompare(nB);
      if (pendingSort === 'za') return nB.localeCompare(nA);
      return schedA.localeCompare(schedB);
    });
  }, [pasesActivos, effectivePendingSearch, pendingFilter, pendingSort, todayIso, weekAheadIso, monthAheadIso]);

  return (
    <ReactNativeModal
      isVisible={Boolean(activeSheet)}
      onBackdropPress={onClose}
      onBackButtonPress={onClose}
      avoidKeyboard
      style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
      backdropColor={isLightMode ? '#0F172A' : '#030407'}
      backdropOpacity={isLightMode ? 0.5 : 0.92}
      backdropTransitionOutTiming={0} backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
      hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
      animationInTiming={200}
      animationOutTiming={180}
      useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
      useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
    >
      <KeyboardAvoidingView behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined} style={{ width: '100%', flex: 1, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
        <View
          style={[
            tm.terminalSheetModal,
            KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS,
            { paddingBottom: Math.max(insets.bottom, 20) + 12, backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#FFFFFF' : '#0A0C12', elevation: 0 },
            isLightMode && { borderColor: '#38BDF8', borderTopColor: '#0284C7' },
          ]}
        >
          <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={onClose} />

          {displaySheet === 'liquidez' && (
            <ScrollView style={{ flex: 1 }} contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 10 }} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} bounces={false} overScrollMode="never" nestedScrollEnabled keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
              <TerminalLiquidityAnalyticsView businessData={businessData} completedList={reservasCompletadasList} pendingList={pasesActivos} />
            </ScrollView>
          )}

          {displaySheet === 'completados' && (
            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <Text style={[tm.sheetModalTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx('Check-ins Completados', 'Completed Check-ins')} ({reservasCompletadasList.length})</Text>
              <KineticListFilterSortBar
                searchQuery={completedSearch}
                onChangeSearch={setCompletedSearch}
                searchPlaceholder={tx('Buscar atleta, clase o #folio...', 'Search athlete, class or #folio...')}
                filterChips={[
                  { key: 'all', label: tx('Todos', 'All'), count: completedCounts.all },
                  { key: 'today', label: tx('Hoy', 'Today'), count: completedCounts.today },
                  { key: '7d', label: tx('7 Días', '7 Days'), count: completedCounts.week },
                  { key: '30d', label: tx('30 Días', '30 Days'), count: completedCounts.month },
                ]}
                activeFilter={completedFilter}
                onSelectFilter={(k) => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setCompletedFilter(k as any); }}
                sortOptions={[
                  { key: 'recent', label: tx('Más Recientes', 'Most Recent') },
                  { key: 'oldest', label: tx('Más Antiguos', 'Oldest') },
                  { key: 'kc_desc', label: tx('Mayor a Menor KC', 'Highest to Lowest KC') },
                  { key: 'kc_asc', label: tx('Menor a Mayor KC', 'Lowest to Highest KC') },
                  { key: 'az', label: tx('Atleta A–Z', 'Athlete A–Z') },
                  { key: 'za', label: tx('Atleta Z–A', 'Athlete Z–A') },
                ]}
                activeSort={completedSort}
                onSelectSort={(k) => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setCompletedSort(k as any); }}
              />
              <ScrollView style={{ flex: 1 }} contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 10 }} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} bounces={false} overScrollMode="never" nestedScrollEnabled keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
                {filteredCompleted.length === 0 ? (
                  <View style={[tm.emptyBox, { elevation: 0, backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#F8FAFC' : '#080B12' }, NO_SEL]}>
                    <Feather name="inbox" size={22} color={themeColors.textMuted} />
                    <Text style={tm.emptyDesc}>{tx('No se encontraron check-ins completados con este filtro.', 'No completed check-ins match this filter.')}</Text>
                  </View>
                ) : (
                  filteredCompleted.map((item: any) => {
                    const athlete = resolveAthleteName(item) || tx('Atleta', 'Athlete');
                    const folio = String(item?.id || '').replace(/-/g, '').substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();
                    const stamp = fmtDateTime(item?.updated_at || item?.created_at, { dateStyle: 'short', timeStyle: 'short' });
                    const master = isMasterPassRow(item);
                    const masterBadgeStyle = isLightMode
                      ? { backgroundColor: '#ECFDF5', borderColor: '#10B981', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#047857' }
                      : { backgroundColor: '#06241B', borderColor: '#10B981', borderTopColor: '#A7F3D0', borderBottomColor: '#065F46' };
                    return (
                      <Pressable
                        key={item.id}
                        style={({ pressed }) => [
                          tm.historyCard,
                          { elevation: 0, backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#F8FAFC' : '#090D16' },
                          isLightMode && { borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' },
                          press3D(pressed, 4.2),
                          NO_SEL,
                        ]}
                        onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); onClose(); if (auditTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(auditTimerRef.current); auditTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => onSelectAuditItem(item), 220); }}
                      >
                        <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingRight: 10 }}>
                          <Text selectable={false} style={tm.serviceTitle} numberOfLines={1}>{td(item.servicio || tx('Sesión Kinetic', 'Kinetic Session'))}</Text>
                          <Text selectable={false} style={tm.serviceMeta} numberOfLines={1}>👤 {athlete} {folio ? `• #${folio}` : ''} {stamp ? `• ${stamp}` : ''}</Text>
                        </View>
                        {master ? (
                          <View style={[{ paddingHorizontal: 8, paddingVertical: 4, borderRadius: 8, borderWidth: 1, borderTopWidth: 1.5, borderBottomWidth: 3, elevation: 0 }, masterBadgeStyle]}>
                            <Text selectable={false} style={{ color: isLightMode ? '#047857' : '#34D399', fontSize: 9.5, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.5 }}>{tx('0 KC • PASE MAESTRO', '0 KC • MASTER PASS')}</Text>
                          </View>
                        ) : (
                          <Text selectable={false} style={tm.servicePrice}>+{item.monto_puerta ?? 0} KC</Text>
                        )}
                      </Pressable>
                    );
                  })
                )}
              </ScrollView>
            </View>
          )}

          {displaySheet === 'pases_espera' && (
            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <Text style={[tm.sheetModalTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx('Pases en Espera', 'Pending Passes')} ({pasesActivos.length} {tx('programados', 'scheduled')})</Text>
              <KineticListFilterSortBar
                searchQuery={effectivePendingSearch}
                onChangeSearch={handleChangePendingSearch}
                searchPlaceholder={tx('Buscar por atleta, clase o #folio...', 'Search by athlete, class or #folio...')}
                filterChips={[
                  { key: 'all', label: tx('Todos', 'All'), count: pendingCounts.all },
                  { key: 'today', label: tx('Hoy', 'Today'), count: pendingCounts.today },
