                  { key: '7d', label: tx('7 Días', '7 Days'), count: pendingCounts.week },
                  { key: '30d', label: tx('30 Días', '30 Days'), count: pendingCounts.month },
                ]}
                activeFilter={pendingFilter}
                onSelectFilter={(k) => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setPendingFilter(k as any); }}
                sortOptions={[
                  { key: 'soonest', label: tx('Próximos', 'Soonest') },
                  { key: 'latest', label: tx('Más Lejanos', 'Furthest') },
                  { key: 'kc_desc', label: tx('Mayor a Menor KC', 'Highest to Lowest KC') },
                  { key: 'kc_asc', label: tx('Menor a Mayor KC', 'Lowest to Highest KC') },
                  { key: 'az', label: tx('Atleta A–Z', 'Athlete A–Z') },
                  { key: 'za', label: tx('Atleta Z–A', 'Athlete Z–A') },
                ]}
                activeSort={pendingSort}
                onSelectSort={(k) => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setPendingSort(k as any); }}
              />
              <ScrollView style={{ flex: 1 }} contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 10 }} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} bounces={false} overScrollMode="never" nestedScrollEnabled keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
                {filteredPending.length === 0 ? (
                  <View style={[tm.emptyBox, { elevation: 0, backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#F8FAFC' : '#080B12' }, NO_SEL]}>
                    <Feather name="clock" size={22} color={themeColors.textMuted} />
                    <Text style={tm.emptyDesc}>{tx('No hay pases en espera para esta búsqueda o filtro.', 'No pending passes match your search or filter.')}</Text>
                  </View>
                ) : (
                  filteredPending.map((item: any) => {
                    const athlete = resolveAthleteName(item) || tx('Atleta', 'Athlete');
                    const folio = String(item?.id || '').replace(/-/g, '').substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();
                    const schedDate = String(item?.fecha_clase || '').trim();
                    const dateFormatted = fmtDate(schedDate || item?.created_at, { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' });
                    const schedTime = String(item?.hora_inicio || item?.hora || '').trim().substring(0, 5);
                    const dateTimeStr = [dateFormatted, schedTime ? `${schedTime} hrs` : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' • ');
                    const master = isMasterPassRow(item);
                    const masterBadgeStyle = isLightMode
                      ? { backgroundColor: '#ECFDF5', borderColor: '#10B981', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#047857' }
                      : { backgroundColor: '#06241B', borderColor: '#10B981', borderTopColor: '#A7F3D0', borderBottomColor: '#065F46' };
                    return (
                      <View key={item.id} style={[tm.historyCard, { elevation: 0, backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#F8FAFC' : '#090D16' }, isLightMode && { borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' }, NO_SEL]}>
                        <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingRight: 10 }}>
                          <Text selectable={false} style={tm.serviceTitle} numberOfLines={1}>{td(item.servicio || tx('Sesión Kinetic', 'Kinetic Session'))}</Text>
                          <Text selectable={false} style={tm.serviceMeta} numberOfLines={1}>👤 {athlete} {folio ? `• #${folio}` : ''} {dateTimeStr ? `• ${dateTimeStr}` : ''}</Text>
                        </View>
                        {master ? (
                          <View style={[{ paddingHorizontal: 8, paddingVertical: 4, borderRadius: 8, borderWidth: 1, borderTopWidth: 1.5, borderBottomWidth: 3, elevation: 0 }, masterBadgeStyle]}>
                            <Text selectable={false} style={{ color: isLightMode ? '#047857' : '#34D399', fontSize: 9.5, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.5 }}>{tx('0 KC • PASE MAESTRO', '0 KC • MASTER PASS')}</Text>
                          </View>
                        ) : (
                          <Text selectable={false} style={[tm.servicePrice, { color: themeColors.green }]}>+{item.monto_puerta ?? 0} KC</Text>
                        )}
                      </View>
                    );
                  })
                )}
              </ScrollView>
            </View>
          )}

          <Pressable
            style={({ pressed }) => [
              tm.sheetCloseBtn,
              { elevation: 0, backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#F1F5F9' : '#111827' },
              isLightMode && { borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' },
              press3D(pressed, 4.5),
              NO_SEL,
            ]}
            onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); onClose(); }}
          >
            <Feather name="arrow-left" size={15} color={themeColors.textPrimary} style={{ marginRight: 6 }} />
            <Text selectable={false} style={[tm.sheetCloseBtnText, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx('VOLVER A LA TERMINAL', 'BACK TO TERMINAL')}</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>
      </KeyboardAvoidingView>
    </ReactNativeModal>
  );
});

    FILE: src/components/terminal/TerminalCatalogAndHistorySection.tsx

    // === src/components/terminal/TerminalCatalogAndHistorySection.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { Image } from 'expo-image';
import React, { memo, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { LayoutAnimation, Platform, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { resolveCategoryImage } from '../../constants/exploreData';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { baseTerminalStyles } from '../../styles/terminal.styles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { KineticPhysicsPressable } from '../../utils/kineticPhysicsEngine';
import { KineticListFilterSortBar } from '../shared/KineticListFilterSortBar';
import { SmartImage, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';

const KINETIC_LOGO = require('../../../assets/logo.png');
const NO_SEL = Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as any) : null;

const isRecurringSrv = (srv: any) => {
  const rawDesc = String(srv?.descripcion || '');
  const freqMatch = rawDesc.match(/\[Frecuencia Programada:\s*(.*?)\]/i);
  const freq = String(srv?.frecuencia || freqMatch?.[1] || 'Única Vez').toLowerCase();
  return /diario|semanal|mensual|daily|weekly|monthly/.test(freq);
};

export type TerminalCatalogStatusFilter = 'activos' | 'expirados' | 'todos';
export type TerminalCatalogSortOption = 'soonest' | 'latest' | 'price_desc' | 'price_asc' | 'capacity_desc' | 'capacity_asc' | 'az' | 'za';

interface TerminalCatalogAndHistorySectionProps {
  businessName?: string | null;
  serviciosActivos: any[];
  serviciosExpirados: any[];
  listaMostrada: any[];
  listaFiltradaCatalogo: any[];
  filtroCatalogo: TerminalCatalogStatusFilter;
  setFiltroCatalogo: (val: TerminalCatalogStatusFilter) => void;
  catalogSearch: string;
  setCatalogSearch: (val: string) => void;
  catalogFilter: 'all' | 'once' | 'recurring' | 'premium';
  setCatalogFilter: (val: 'all' | 'once' | 'recurring' | 'premium') => void;
  catalogSort: TerminalCatalogSortOption;
  setCatalogSort: (val: TerminalCatalogSortOption) => void;
  formatDateLabel: (isoString?: string) => string;
  onCreateEvent: () => void;
  onPreviewEvent: (srv: any, isExpired: boolean) => void;
  onEditEvent: (srv: any, isExpired: boolean) => void;
  onOpenLiveRoom?: (srv: any) => void;
  pasesEnEspera?: any[];
  historialCompleto: any[];
  filtroBitacora: string;
  setFiltroBitacora: (val: string) => void;
  isHistoryExpanded: boolean;
  setIsHistoryExpanded: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<boolean>>;
  onSelectAuditItem: (item: any) => void;
}

export const TerminalCatalogAndHistorySection = memo(({
  businessName, serviciosActivos, serviciosExpirados, listaMostrada, listaFiltradaCatalogo,
  filtroCatalogo, setFiltroCatalogo, catalogSearch, setCatalogSearch, catalogFilter, setCatalogFilter,
  catalogSort, setCatalogSort, formatDateLabel, onCreateEvent, onPreviewEvent, onEditEvent,
  onOpenLiveRoom, pasesEnEspera = [], historialCompleto, filtroBitacora, setFiltroBitacora, isHistoryExpanded,
  setIsHistoryExpanded, onSelectAuditItem,
}: TerminalCatalogAndHistorySectionProps) => {
  const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, t, tx, td, fmtDateTime } = useKineticPreferences();
  const tm = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseTerminalStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);

  const [historySearch, setHistorySearch] = useState('');
  const [historySort, setHistorySort] = useState<'recent' | 'oldest' | 'kc_desc' | 'kc_asc' | 'az' | 'za'>('recent');

  const resolveAthleteName = (item: any) =>
    (Array.isArray(item?.perfiles) ? item?.perfiles[0]?.nombre : item?.perfiles?.nombre) ||
    item?.atleta_nombre ||
    item?.nombre_atleta ||
    '';

  const expiredIdSet = useMemo(
    () => new Set((serviciosExpirados || []).map((s: any) => String(s?.id || ''))),
    [serviciosExpirados]
  );

  const activeCounts = useMemo(() => {
    let once = 0, recurring = 0, premium = 0;
    (serviciosActivos || []).forEach((srv: any) => {
      if (isRecurringSrv(srv)) recurring += 1; else once += 1;
      if ((Number(srv?.precio_kc ?? 150) || 0) >= 180) premium += 1;
    });
    return { all: (serviciosActivos || []).length, once, recurring, premium };
  }, [serviciosActivos]);

  const totalCatalogCount = (serviciosActivos || []).length + (serviciosExpirados || []).length;
  const unifiedCatalogActiveFilter =
    filtroCatalogo === 'expirados'
      ? 'expirados:all'
      : filtroCatalogo === 'todos'
        ? `todos:${catalogFilter}`
        : `activos:${catalogFilter}`;

  const handleSelectUnifiedCatalogFilter = (compoundKey: string) => {
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    const [nextStatus, nextType] = String(compoundKey).split(':');
    if (nextStatus === 'expirados') {
      setFiltroCatalogo('expirados');
      setCatalogFilter('all');
    } else if (nextStatus === 'todos') {
      setFiltroCatalogo('todos');
      setCatalogFilter(((nextType as any) || 'all') as 'all' | 'once' | 'recurring' | 'premium');
    } else {
      setFiltroCatalogo('activos');
      setCatalogFilter(((nextType as any) || 'all') as 'all' | 'once' | 'recurring' | 'premium');
    }
  };

  const historyCounts = useMemo(() => {
    let espera = (pasesEnEspera || []).length;
    let completada = 0, expirada = 0;
    (historialCompleto || []).forEach((item: any) => {
      const st = String(item?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
      if (st === 'completada') completada += 1;
      else if (st === 'no_show' || st === 'expirada' || st === 'penalizada') expirada += 1;
    });
    return { espera, completada, expirada, all: espera + (historialCompleto || []).length };
  }, [pasesEnEspera, historialCompleto]);

  const bitacoraFiltradaYSorteada = useMemo(() => {
    const q = historySearch.trim().toLowerCase();
    const activeIds = new Set((pasesEnEspera || []).map((p: any) => String(p?.id || '')));
    const combinedAll = [
      ...(pasesEnEspera || []),
      ...(historialCompleto || []).filter((h: any) => !activeIds.has(String(h?.id || ''))),
    ];
    const sourceList =
      filtroBitacora === 'espera'
        ? pasesEnEspera || []
        : filtroBitacora === 'all'
        ? combinedAll
        : (historialCompleto || []).filter((item: any) => {
            const st = String(item?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
            if (filtroBitacora === 'expirada') {
              return st === 'no_show' || st === 'expirada' || st === 'penalizada';
            }
            return st === filtroBitacora;
          });
    const base = sourceList.filter((item: any) => {
      if (!q) return true;
      const athlete = resolveAthleteName(item);
      const folio = String(item?.id || '').replace(/-/g, '').substring(0, 8);
      return `${item?.servicio || ''} ${athlete} ${folio} ${item?.id || ''}`.toLowerCase().includes(q);
    });
    return [...base].sort((a: any, b: any) => {
      const tsA = new Date(a?.updated_at || a?.created_at || 0).getTime() || 0;
      const tsB = new Date(b?.updated_at || b?.created_at || 0).getTime() || 0;
      const kcA = Number(a?.monto_puerta) || 0;
      const kcB = Number(b?.monto_puerta) || 0;
      const nA = resolveAthleteName(a);
      const nB = resolveAthleteName(b);
      if (historySort === 'oldest') return tsA - tsB;
      if (historySort === 'kc_desc') return kcB - kcA;
      if (historySort === 'kc_asc') return kcA - kcB;
      if (historySort === 'az') return nA.localeCompare(nB);
      if (historySort === 'za') return nB.localeCompare(nA);
      return tsB - tsA;
    });
  }, [pasesEnEspera, historialCompleto, filtroBitacora, historySearch, historySort]);

  return (
    <>
      <View style={[tm.rowBetween, { marginBottom: 10 }]}>
        <Text style={[tm.sectionTitle, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{t('terminal.event_catalog')}</Text>
        <KineticPhysicsPressable preset="tactileChip" style={[tm.newEventBtn, isLightMode && tm.newEventBtnLight, NO_SEL]} onPress={onCreateEvent}>
          <Feather name="plus" size={13} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          <Text selectable={false} style={[tm.newEventBtnText, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{t('terminal.new_event')}</Text>
        </KineticPhysicsPressable>
      </View>

      <KineticListFilterSortBar
        searchQuery={catalogSearch}
        onChangeSearch={setCatalogSearch}
        searchPlaceholder={tx('Buscar clase, coach o disciplina...', 'Search class, coach, or discipline...')}
        filterChips={[
          { key: 'activos:all', label: tx('⚡ Activos', '⚡ Active'), count: activeCounts.all },
          { key: 'expirados:all', label: tx('🟣 Expirados / Inactivos', '🟣 Expired / Inactive'), count: serviciosExpirados.length },
          { key: 'todos:all', label: tx('📋 Todos', '📋 All'), count: totalCatalogCount },
          { key: 'activos:once', label: tx('📅 Única Vez', '📅 One-Time'), count: activeCounts.once },
          { key: 'activos:recurring', label: tx('🔄 Recurrentes', '🔄 Recurring'), count: activeCounts.recurring },
          { key: 'activos:premium', label: tx('💎 Premium', '💎 Premium'), count: activeCounts.premium },
        ]}
        activeFilter={unifiedCatalogActiveFilter}
        onSelectFilter={handleSelectUnifiedCatalogFilter}
        sortOptions={[
          { key: 'soonest', label: tx('Próximos', 'Soonest') },
          { key: 'latest', label: tx('Más Lejanos', 'Furthest') },
          { key: 'price_desc', label: tx('Mayor Precio', 'Highest Price') },
          { key: 'price_asc', label: tx('Menor Precio', 'Lowest Price') },
          { key: 'capacity_desc', label: tx('Mayor Cupo', 'Highest Capacity') },
          { key: 'capacity_asc', label: tx('Menor Cupo', 'Lowest Capacity') },
          { key: 'az', label: 'A–Z' },
          { key: 'za', label: 'Z–A' },
        ]}
        activeSort={catalogSort}
        onSelectSort={(k) => {
          triggerHaptic('Selection');
          setCatalogSort(k as TerminalCatalogSortOption);
        }}
      />

      {listaFiltradaCatalogo.length === 0 ? (
        <View style={[tm.emptyBox, NO_SEL]}>
          <Feather name="calendar" size={24} color={themeColors.textMuted} />
          <Text style={tm.emptyDesc}>
            {listaMostrada.length === 0
              ? filtroCatalogo === 'activos'
                ? tx('No tienes eventos activos próximos. Crea uno nuevo o reactiva uno expirado.', 'You have no upcoming active events. Create a new one or reactivate an expired event.')
                : filtroCatalogo === 'expirados'
                  ? tx('No hay eventos expirados ni inactivos en el archivo.', 'No expired or inactive events in the archive.')
                  : tx('Aún no hay eventos registrados en tu catálogo.', 'No events registered in your catalog yet.')
              : tx('Sin coincidencias para tu búsqueda o filtro.', 'No events match your search or filter.')}
          </Text>
        </View>
      ) : (
        <View style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
          {listaFiltradaCatalogo.map((srv: any) => {
            const isExpCard = expiredIdSet.has(String(srv?.id || ''));
            const isInactiveState = srv?.activo === false || ['inactivo', 'pausado', 'cancelado', 'inactive', 'paused', 'canceled'].includes(String(srv?.estado || '').trim().toLowerCase());
            const dateStr = formatDateLabel(srv.fecha);
            const timeStr = String(srv.hora_inicio || '08:00').substring(0, 5);
            const durMin = srv.duracion_minutos || 60;
            const cupoVal = srv.cupo_maximo ?? srv.cupo_total ?? 15;
            const rawClub = srv.negocio_nombre === 'Terminal Global Kinetic' ? 'Kinetic Oficial' : (srv.negocio_nombre || businessName || 'Kinetic Oficial');
            const clubStr = String(rawClub).toLowerCase();
            const isOfficial = clubStr === 'kinetic oficial' || clubStr === 'kinetic sports club';
            return (
              <View key={srv.id} style={[tm.serviceCard, { flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'stretch', gap: 10 }, isExpCard && tm.serviceCardExpired, NO_SEL]}>
                <TouchableOpacity style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12 }} activeOpacity={0.85} onPress={() => onPreviewEvent(srv, isExpCard)}>
                  <View style={[tm.serviceLogoBox, { flexShrink: 0 }, isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC' }, isExpCard && { borderColor: themeColors.purpleText }]}>
                    {isOfficial ? (
                      <Image source={KINETIC_LOGO} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }} contentFit="cover" />
                    ) : (
                      <SmartImage source={{ uri: resolveCategoryImage(srv.categoria, srv.nombre_servicio) }} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }} contentFit="cover" />
                    )}
                  </View>
                  <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                    <Text selectable={false} style={tm.serviceTitle} numberOfLines={2}>{td(srv.nombre_servicio || srv.titulo || tx('Experiencia Kinetic', 'Kinetic Experience'))}</Text>
                  </View>
                  <Text selectable={false} style={[tm.servicePrice, { flexShrink: 0, color: isExpCard ? themeColors.purpleText : themeColors.cyanText }]}>{srv.precio_kc ?? 150} KC</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', gap: 8 }}>
                  <View style={{ flex: 1, flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
                    <View style={{ flexShrink: 0, paddingHorizontal: 8, paddingVertical: 4, borderRadius: 8, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: isLightMode ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.22)', backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#F8FAFC' : '#0B111E' }}>
                      <Text selectable={false} style={[tm.serviceMeta, { marginTop: 0, fontSize: 10.5 }]}>
                        🕒 {dateStr} • {timeStr} ({durMin}m) • 👥 {cupoVal}{isExpCard ? ` • ${isInactiveState ? tx('INACTIVO', 'INACTIVE') : tx('EXPIRADO', 'EXPIRED')}` : ''}
                      </Text>
                    </View>
                  </View>
                  <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, flexShrink: 0 }}>
                    {onOpenLiveRoom && (
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        style={[
                          {
                            flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4, paddingHorizontal: 8, paddingVertical: 6,
                            borderRadius: 10, borderWidth: 1, borderBottomWidth: 3, elevation: 0,
                            backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#ECFEFF' : 'rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.1)',
                            borderColor: isLightMode ? '#06B6D4' : 'rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.45)',
                            borderBottomColor: isLightMode ? '#0891B2' : 'rgba(0, 184, 212, 0.85)',
                          },
                          NO_SEL,
                        ]}
                        hitSlop={{ top: 6, bottom: 6, left: 6, right: 6 }}
                        activeOpacity={0.78}
                        onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); onOpenLiveRoom(srv); }}
                      >
                        <Feather name="grid" size={12} color={isLightMode ? '#0E7490' : COLORS.cyan} />
                        <Text selectable={false} style={{ fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '900', color: isLightMode ? '#0E7490' : COLORS.cyan }}>
                          {tx('Sala 3D', '3D Room')}
                        </Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    )}
                    <TouchableOpacity
                      style={[
                        tm.cleanEditIconBtn,
                        isLightMode && tm.cleanEditIconBtnLight,
                        isExpCard && tm.cleanEditIconBtnExpired,
                        isExpCard && isLightMode && tm.cleanEditIconBtnExpiredLight,
                        NO_SEL,
                      ]}
                      hitSlop={{ top: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8, right: 8 }}
                      activeOpacity={0.78}
                      onPress={() => onEditEvent(srv, isExpCard)}
                    >
                      <Feather name={isExpCard ? 'refresh-cw' : 'edit-2'} size={15} color={isExpCard ? themeColors.purpleText : themeColors.cyanText} />
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  </View>
                </View>
              </View>
            );
          })}
        </View>
      )}

      <TouchableOpacity
        style={[tm.accordionHeader, NO_SEL]}
        activeOpacity={0.8}
        onPress={() => {
          triggerHaptic('Selection');
          if (Platform.OS !== 'web') LayoutAnimation.configureNext(LayoutAnimation.Presets.easeInEaseOut);
          setIsHistoryExpanded((p) => !p);
        }}
      >
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
          <Feather name="list" size={16} color={themeColors.textPrimary} />
          <Text selectable={false} style={[tm.accordionTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx('Bitácora y Auditoría', 'Audit & Check-in Log')} ({historialCompleto.length})</Text>
        </View>
        <Feather name={isHistoryExpanded ? 'chevron-up' : 'chevron-down'} size={18} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
      </TouchableOpacity>

      {isHistoryExpanded && (
        <View style={{ marginTop: 10 }}>
          <KineticListFilterSortBar
            searchQuery={historySearch}
            onChangeSearch={setHistorySearch}
            searchPlaceholder={tx('Buscar atleta, sesión o #folio...', 'Search athlete, session or #folio...')}
            filterChips={[
              { key: 'all', label: tx('Todos', 'All'), count: historyCounts.all },
              { key: 'espera', label: tx('En Espera', 'Waiting'), count: historyCounts.espera },
              { key: 'completada', label: tx('Completados', 'Completed'), count: historyCounts.completada },
              { key: 'expirada', label: tx('Expirados', 'Expired'), count: historyCounts.expirada },
            ]}
            activeFilter={filtroBitacora}
            onSelectFilter={(k) => {
              triggerHaptic('Selection');
              setFiltroBitacora(k);
            }}
            sortOptions={[
              { key: 'recent', label: tx('Más Recientes', 'Most Recent') },
              { key: 'oldest', label: tx('Más Antiguos', 'Oldest') },
              { key: 'kc_desc', label: tx('Mayor a Menor KC', 'Highest to Lowest KC') },
              { key: 'kc_asc', label: tx('Menor a Mayor KC', 'Lowest to Highest KC') },
              { key: 'az', label: tx('Atleta A–Z', 'Athlete A–Z') },
              { key: 'za', label: tx('Atleta Z–A', 'Athlete Z–A') },
            ]}
            activeSort={historySort}
            onSelectSort={(k) => {
              triggerHaptic('Selection');
              setHistorySort(k as 'recent' | 'oldest' | 'kc_desc' | 'kc_asc' | 'az' | 'za');
            }}
          />
          {bitacoraFiltradaYSorteada.length === 0 ? (
            <View style={[tm.emptyBox, NO_SEL]}><Text style={tm.emptyDesc}>{tx('Sin registros en esta categoría.', 'No records in this category.')}</Text></View>
          ) : (
            bitacoraFiltradaYSorteada.map((item: any) => {
              const stamp = fmtDateTime(item?.updated_at || item?.created_at, { dateStyle: 'short', timeStyle: 'short' });
              const stLow = String(item?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
              const isCanceledRow = stLow === 'cancelada' || stLow === 'reembolsada';
              const isExpiredRow = stLow === 'no_show' || stLow === 'expirada' || stLow === 'penalizada';
              const isWaitingRow = stLow === 'activa' || stLow === 'pendiente' || stLow === 'confirmada';
              const athleteLabel = resolveAthleteName(item) || tx('Atleta', 'Athlete');
              const isZeroKcMaster =
                (Number(item?.costo_total ?? item?.precio_kc ?? 0) || 0) === 0 &&
                (Number(item?.monto_puerta ?? 0) || 0) === 0 &&
                (Number(item?.monto_escrow ?? 0) || 0) === 0;
              return (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={item.id}
                  style={[tm.historyCard, NO_SEL]}
                  activeOpacity={0.85}
                  onPress={() => {
                    triggerHaptic('Selection');
                    onSelectAuditItem(item);
                  }}
                >
                  <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0, paddingRight: 8 }}>
                    <Text selectable={false} style={tm.serviceTitle} numberOfLines={2}>{td(item.servicio || tx('Sesión Kinetic', 'Kinetic Session'))}</Text>
                    <Text selectable={false} style={tm.serviceMeta} numberOfLines={1}>
                      👤 {athleteLabel} • #{String(item.id || '').substring(0, 8).toUpperCase()}{stamp ? ` • ${stamp}` : ''}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                  <Text
                    selectable={false}
                    style={[
                      tm.servicePrice,
                      { flexShrink: 0 },
                      isZeroKcMaster && !isCanceledRow && !isExpiredRow && { color: isLightMode ? '#047857' : '#34D399', fontSize: 10.5 },
                      isWaitingRow && { color: themeColors.cyanText, fontSize: 10.5 },
                      isExpiredRow && { color: isLightMode ? '#B45309' : '#FBBF24', fontSize: 10 },
                      isCanceledRow && { color: isLightMode ? '#DC2626' : COLORS.red, fontSize: isZeroKcMaster ? 10 : undefined },
                    ]}
                  >
                    {isExpiredRow
                      ? (isZeroKcMaster ? tx('EXPIRADO (0 KC)', 'EXPIRED (0 KC)') : tx(`NO-SHOW • +${item.monto_escrow ?? 0} KC`, `NO-SHOW • +${item.monto_escrow ?? 0} KC`))
                      : isWaitingRow
                      ? (isZeroKcMaster ? tx('EN ESPERA (0 KC)', 'WAITING (0 KC)') : tx(`EN ESPERA • ${item.monto_puerta ?? 0} KC`, `WAITING • ${item.monto_puerta ?? 0} KC`))
                      : isCanceledRow
                      ? (isZeroKcMaster ? tx('PASE MAESTRO CANCELADO (0 KC)', 'CANCELED MASTER PASS (0 KC)') : `${item.monto_puerta ?? 0} KC`)
                      : (isZeroKcMaster ? tx('0 KC • PASE MAESTRO', '0 KC • MASTER PASS') : `+${item.monto_puerta ?? 0} KC`)}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              );
            })
          )}
        </View>
      )}
    </>
  );
});

    FILE: src/components/terminal/TerminalLiquidityAnalyticsView.tsx

    // === src/components/terminal/TerminalLiquidityAnalyticsView.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Platform, ScrollView, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import {
  fetchMasterPassesAuditSummary,
  IMasterPassAuditSummary,
} from '../../services/bovedaB2BDataHelper';
import {
  buildPartnerPeriodOptions,
  computePartnerSpeiStatement,
  exportPartnerSpeiStatementPdf,
  IPartnerPeriodOption,
} from '../../services/terminalSpeiAndStudioService';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { GpuProgressBar, KineticLoader, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { styles as baseStyles } from '../TerminalActionModals.styles';

const NO_SEL = Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as any) : null;
const getTodayLocalISO = () => {
  const n = new Date();
  return `${n.getFullYear()}-${String(n.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(n.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
};

export const TerminalLiquidityAnalyticsView = memo(
  ({ businessData, completedList = [], pendingList = [] }: { businessData?: any; completedList: any[]; pendingList: any[] }) => {
    const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, language, tx, td } = useKineticPreferences();
    const styles = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);
    const [period, setPeriod] = useState<'hoy' | '7dias' | 'mes' | 'historico'>('hoy');
    const [speiKey, setSpeiKey] = useState<IPartnerPeriodOption['key']>('week_0');
    const [isExportingPdf, setIsExportingPdf] = useState(false);
    const [speiMsg, setSpeiMsg] = useState<{ text: string; isError: boolean } | null>(null);
    const [masterPassSummary, setMasterPassSummary] = useState<IMasterPassAuditSummary>({
      total_emitidos: 0, activos: 0, liquidados: 0, cancelados: 0, hoy: 0, semana: 0, mes: 0,
    });
    const mountedRef = useRef(true);
    useEffect(() => () => { mountedRef.current = false; }, []);

    useEffect(() => {
      let alive = true;
      const isOfficial = Boolean(businessData?.isOfficialKinetic) || String(businessData?.nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase() === 'kinetic oficial';
      const hasActiveBiz = isOfficial || Boolean(businessData?.id && String(businessData?.estado || '').trim().toLowerCase() === 'activo');
      const fallbackRows = [...(completedList || []), ...(pendingList || [])];
      fetchMasterPassesAuditSummary(isOfficial ? null : businessData?.nombre || null, isOfficial, fallbackRows, hasActiveBiz)
        .then((res) => { if (alive && mountedRef.current) setMasterPassSummary(res); })
        .catch(() => {});
      return () => { alive = false; };
    }, [businessData?.isOfficialKinetic, businessData?.nombre, completedList, pendingList]);

    const speiPeriods = useMemo(() => buildPartnerPeriodOptions(Date.now(), language === 'en'), [language]);
    const activeSpeiOpt = useMemo(() => speiPeriods.find((o) => o.key === speiKey) || speiPeriods[0], [speiPeriods, speiKey]);
    const speiCut = useMemo(
      () => computePartnerSpeiStatement(businessData, completedList, pendingList, activeSpeiOpt?.override),
      [businessData, completedList, pendingList, activeSpeiOpt]
    );

    const analytics = useMemo(() => {
      const now = new Date();
      const todayIso = getTodayLocalISO();
      const sevenDaysAgo = new Date(now.getTime() - 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
      const startOfMonth = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 1);

      const filtered = (completedList || []).filter((item: any) => {
        const rawDate = String(item?.updated_at || item?.fecha_clase || item?.created_at || '').trim();
        if (!rawDate) return period === 'historico' || period === 'mes';
        const safeRaw = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(rawDate) ? `${rawDate}T12:00:00` : rawDate;
        const d = new Date(safeRaw);
        if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return true;
        const localItemIso = `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
        if (period === 'hoy') return localItemIso === todayIso || rawDate.startsWith(todayIso);
        if (period === '7dias') return d >= sevenDaysAgo;
        if (period === 'mes') return d >= startOfMonth;
        return true;
      });

      const effectiveItems = period === 'hoy' && filtered.length === 0 ? completedList : filtered;
      let puerta60Total = 0;
      let bruto100Total = 0;
      let escrowLiberado40Total = 0;
      const byClassMap = new Map<string, { count: number; kcPuerta: number; kcTotal: number }>();

      effectiveItems.forEach((r: any) => {
        const base = parseFloat(r?.costo_total) || parseFloat(r?.precio_kc) || 0;
        const puerta = !Number.isNaN(parseFloat(r?.monto_puerta))
          ? parseFloat(r.monto_puerta)
          : Math.round(base * 0.6);
        const total100 = base > 0 ? base : Math.round(puerta / 0.6);
        const escrow40 = Math.max(0, total100 - puerta);
        puerta60Total += puerta;
        bruto100Total += total100;
        escrowLiberado40Total += escrow40;
        const className =
          r?.servicio || r?.titulo_clase || r?.nombre_servicio || tx('Sesión Kinetic', 'Kinetic Session');
        const prev = byClassMap.get(className) || { count: 0, kcPuerta: 0, kcTotal: 0 };
        prev.count += 1;
        prev.kcPuerta += puerta;
        prev.kcTotal += total100;
        byClassMap.set(className, prev);
      });

      const escrowCustodiaActiva = (pendingList || []).reduce(
        (acc: number, p: any) => acc + (Number(p?.monto_escrow) || 0),
        0
      );
      const sessionsCount = effectiveItems.length;
      const ticketPromedio = sessionsCount > 0 ? Math.round(bruto100Total / sessionsCount) : 0;
      const totalOperaciones = sessionsCount + pendingList.length;
      const tasaConversionPct =
        totalOperaciones > 0 ? Math.round((sessionsCount / totalOperaciones) * 100) : 100;

      const topClasses = Array.from(byClassMap.entries())
        .map(([name, info]) => ({
          name,
          count: info.count,
          kcPuerta: info.kcPuerta,
          kcTotal: info.kcTotal,
          sharePct:
            puerta60Total > 0
              ? Math.round((info.kcPuerta / puerta60Total) * 100)
              : Math.round((1 / Math.max(1, byClassMap.size)) * 100),
        }))
        .sort((a, b) => b.kcPuerta - a.kcPuerta || b.count - a.count)
        .slice(0, 6);

      const maxClassKc =
        topClasses.length > 0 ? Math.max(1, ...topClasses.map((c) => c.kcPuerta || c.count)) : 1;

      return {
        puerta60Total,
        escrowLiberado40Total,
        bruto100Total,
        socioNeto80: Math.round(bruto100Total * 0.8),
        sessionsCount,
        ticketPromedio,
        tasaConversionPct,
        escrowCustodiaActiva,
        pendingCount: pendingList.length,
        topClasses,
        maxClassKc,
      };
    }, [completedList, pendingList, period, tx]);

    const periodLabels: Record<string, string> = {
      hoy: tx('Corte del Día (Hoy)', 'Daily Cut (Today)'),
      '7dias': tx('Últimos 7 Días', 'Last 7 Days'),
      mes: tx('Acumulado de Este Mes', 'Month to Date'),
      historico: tx('Histórico Global', 'All-Time History'),
    };

    return (
      <View style={NO_SEL}>
        <Text style={styles.sheetTitle}>
          {tx('Liquidez y Estadísticas de Recepción', 'Front Desk Liquidity & Analytics')}
        </Text>
        <Text style={styles.sheetSub}>
          {tx(
            'Consulta tus ingresos capturados en puerta QR por día, semana o mes y audita el rendimiento de tus experiencias (1 KC = $1.00 MXN).',
            'Track your QR front-desk revenue by day, week, or month and audit your experiences performance (1 KC = $1.00 MXN).'
          )}
        </Text>

        <View style={styles.periodPillsRow}>
          {[
            { key: 'hoy', label: tx('Hoy', 'Today') },
            { key: '7dias', label: tx('7 Días', '7 Days') },
            { key: 'mes', label: tx('Este Mes', 'This Month') },
            { key: 'historico', label: tx('Histórico', 'All-Time') },
          ].map((p: any) => {
            const active = period === p.key;
            const darkInkProps: any =
              Platform.OS === 'web' && active ? { dataSet: { kineticInk: 'dark' } } : {};
            return (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={p.key} {...darkInkProps}
                style={[styles.periodPillBtn, !active && { backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#F1F5F9' : '#0D131F', elevation: 0 }, active && styles.periodPillBtnActive, NO_SEL]}
                onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setPeriod(p.key); }}
              >
                <Text selectable={false} style={[styles.periodPillText, active && { color: '#050505' }]}>{p.label}</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            );
          })}
        </View>

        <View style={styles.miniKpiStripRow}>
          <View style={[styles.miniKpiBox, { backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#FFFFFF' : '#090D16', elevation: 0 }, NO_SEL]}>
            <Text style={styles.miniKpiLabel}>{tx('TICKET PROM.', 'AVG. TICKET')}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.miniKpiValue}>{analytics.ticketPromedio} KC</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.miniKpiBox, { backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#FFFFFF' : '#090D16', elevation: 0 }, NO_SEL]}>
            <Text style={styles.miniKpiLabel}>{tx('CUSTODIA 40%', '40% ESCROW')}</Text>
            <Text style={[styles.miniKpiValue, { color: themeColors.purpleText }]}>
              {analytics.escrowLiberado40Total} KC
            </Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.miniKpiBox, { backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#FFFFFF' : '#090D16', elevation: 0 }, NO_SEL]}>
            <Text style={styles.miniKpiLabel}>{tx('ASISTENCIA QR', 'QR ATTENDANCE')}</Text>
            <Text style={[styles.miniKpiValue, { color: themeColors.green }]}>{analytics.tasaConversionPct}%</Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={[styles.analyticsSummaryBox, { backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#FFFFFF' : '#090D16', elevation: 0 }, NO_SEL]}>
          <Text style={[styles.periodHeaderTag, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
            ⚡ {periodLabels[period].toUpperCase()}
          </Text>
          <View style={styles.summaryRowItem}>
            <Text style={styles.summaryLabel}>
              {tx('60% Capturado en Puerta QR:', '60% Captured at QR Front Desk:')}
            </Text>
            <Text style={[styles.summaryVal, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
              +{analytics.puerta60Total} KC (${analytics.puerta60Total}.00 MXN)
            </Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.summaryRowItem}>
            <Text style={styles.summaryLabel}>
              {tx('Volumen Total Devengado (100%):', 'Total Accrued Volume (100%):')}
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.summaryVal}>{analytics.bruto100Total} KC</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.summaryRowItem}>
            <Text style={styles.summaryLabel}>
              {tx('Participación Bruta Socio (80%):', 'Partner Gross Share (80%):')}
            </Text>
            <Text style={[styles.summaryVal, { color: themeColors.green }]}>${analytics.socioNeto80}.00 MXN</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.summaryRowItem}>
            <Text style={styles.summaryLabel}>
              {tx('Check-ins Validados QR:', 'Verified QR Check-ins:')}
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.summaryVal}>
              {analytics.sessionsCount} {tx('sesiones', 'sessions')}
            </Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.summaryRowItem, { borderBottomWidth: 0 }]}>
            <Text style={styles.summaryLabel}>
              {tx('Próximas Llegadas en Espera:', 'Upcoming Waiting Arrivals:')}
            </Text>
            <Text style={[styles.summaryVal, { color: themeColors.purpleText }]}>
              {analytics.pendingCount} {tx('pases', 'passes')} ({analytics.escrowCustodiaActiva}{' '}
              {tx('KC Apartados', 'KC in Escrow')})
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={[styles.chartCardBox, { backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#FFFFFF' : '#090D16', elevation: 0 }, NO_SEL]}>
          <Text style={styles.chartCardTitle}>
            {tx(
              'GRÁFICA DE RENDIMIENTO POR EXPERIENCIA (60% PUERTA + TOTAL 100%)',
              'PERFORMANCE BY EXPERIENCE (60% FRONT DESK + 100% TOTAL)'
            )}
          </Text>
          {analytics.topClasses.length === 0 ? (
            <Text style={styles.emptyChartText}>
              {tx('Sin check-ins registrados en este periodo.', 'No check-ins recorded in this period.')}
            </Text>
          ) : (
            <View>
              {analytics.topClasses.map((cl) => {
                const metricVal = cl.kcPuerta > 0 ? cl.kcPuerta : cl.count;
                const pct = Math.round((metricVal / analytics.maxClassKc) * 100);
                return (
                  <View key={cl.name} style={{ marginBottom: 12 }}>
                    <View style={styles.chartRowTop}>
                      <Text style={styles.chartClassName} numberOfLines={1}>
                        {td(cl.name)}
                      </Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.chartClassVal, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
                        +{cl.kcPuerta} KC • {cl.count} QR ({cl.sharePct}%)
                      </Text>
                    </View>
                    <GpuProgressBar progress={Math.max(10, pct)} color={COLORS.cyan} height={6} />
                    <Text style={styles.chartMicroSub}>
                      {tx('Volumen 100%', '100% Volume')}: {cl.kcTotal} KC • {tx('Socio 80%', 'Partner 80%')}: $
                      {Math.round(cl.kcTotal * 0.8)}.00 MXN
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                );
              })}
            </View>
          )}
        </View>

        <View style={[styles.analyticsSummaryBox, { backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#FFFFFF' : '#090D16', elevation: 0, marginTop: 14 }, NO_SEL]}>
          <Text style={[styles.periodHeaderTag, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
            {tx('📄 CORTE SEMANAL SPEI (80/20) & ESTADO DE CUENTA PDF', '📄 WEEKLY SPEI CUT (80/20) & PDF STATEMENT')}
          </Text>
          <Text style={{ fontSize: 11, color: themeColors.textSecondary, marginBottom: 10 }}>
            {activeSpeiOpt?.rangeLabel} • {tx('Corte Dominical 23:59:59', 'Sunday 23:59:59 Cut')}
          </Text>

          <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={{ gap: 8, paddingBottom: 10 }}>
            {speiPeriods.map((opt) => {
              const isAct = opt.key === speiKey;
              return (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={opt.key}
                  onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setSpeiKey(opt.key); setSpeiMsg(null); }}
                  style={{
                    paddingHorizontal: 11, paddingVertical: 7, borderRadius: 10, borderWidth: 1, borderBottomWidth: 3, elevation: 0,
                    backgroundColor: isAct ? COLORS.cyan : isLightMode ? '#F1F5F9' : '#0D131F',
                    borderColor: isAct ? '#0284C7' : isLightMode ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)',
                  }}
                >
                  <Text selectable={false} style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900', color: isAct ? '#050505' : themeColors.textPrimary }}>
                    {opt.label}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              );
            })}
          </ScrollView>

          <View style={styles.summaryRowItem}>
            <Text style={styles.summaryLabel}>{tx('Bruto 100% Devengado:', '100% Gross Accrued:')}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.summaryVal}>${speiCut.corteSocio.volumen_bruto_mxn.toFixed(2)} MXN</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.summaryRowItem}>
            <Text style={styles.summaryLabel}>{tx('Comisión Kinetic (20%):', 'Kinetic Fee (20%):')}</Text>
            <Text style={[styles.summaryVal, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>-${speiCut.corteSocio.comision_kinetic_total_mxn.toFixed(2)} MXN</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.summaryRowItem}>
            <Text style={styles.summaryLabel}>
              {tx('Retención SAT', 'SAT Withholding')} ({speiCut.corteSocio.regimen_fiscal === 'persona_moral' ? '0%' : speiCut.corteSocio.regimen_fiscal === 'persona_fisica_con_rfc' ? '10.1%' : '36%'}):
            </Text>
            <Text style={[styles.summaryVal, { color: '#F59E0B' }]}>-${speiCut.corteSocio.total_retenciones_sat_mxn.toFixed(2)} MXN</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.summaryRowItem, { borderBottomWidth: 0 }]}>
            <Text style={[styles.summaryLabel, { fontWeight: '900', color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx('Neto SPEI a Dispersar:', 'Net SPEI Payout:')}</Text>
            <Text style={[styles.summaryVal, { color: themeColors.green, fontSize: 15 }]}>${speiCut.corteSocio.neto_dispersar_spei_mxn.toFixed(2)} MXN</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.summaryRowItem, { borderBottomWidth: 0, marginTop: 4, paddingTop: 8, borderTopWidth: 1, borderTopColor: isLightMode ? '#E2E8F0' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)' }]}>
            <Text style={styles.summaryLabel}>{tx('Pases Maestros (0 KC):', 'Master Passes (0 KC):')}</Text>
            <Text style={[styles.summaryVal, { color: themeColors.green, fontSize: 11.5 }]}>
              {tx(
                `Hoy ${masterPassSummary.hoy} • 7D ${masterPassSummary.semana} • Mes ${masterPassSummary.mes}`,
                `Today ${masterPassSummary.hoy} • 7D ${masterPassSummary.semana} • Mo ${masterPassSummary.mes}`
              )}
            </Text>
          </View>

          {speiMsg && (
            <Text style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '800', marginTop: 6, color: speiMsg.isError ? '#EF4444' : themeColors.green }}>
              {speiMsg.text}
            </Text>
          )}

          <TouchableOpacity
            disabled={isExportingPdf}
            onPress={async () => {
              triggerHaptic('Medium'); setIsExportingPdf(true); setSpeiMsg(null);
              try {
                await exportPartnerSpeiStatementPdf(businessData, completedList, pendingList, activeSpeiOpt?.override, masterPassSummary);
                if (mountedRef.current) setSpeiMsg({ text: tx('✅ Estado de cuenta PDF generado.', '✅ PDF statement generated.'), isError: false });
              } catch (err: any) {
                if (mountedRef.current) setSpeiMsg({ text: err?.message || tx('No se pudo exportar el PDF.', 'Could not export PDF.'), isError: true });
              } finally { if (mountedRef.current) setIsExportingPdf(false); }
            }}
            style={{
              marginTop: 12, flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 8,
              backgroundColor: COLORS.cyan, borderRadius: 13, paddingVertical: 12, borderBottomWidth: 4, borderBottomColor: '#0284C7', elevation: 0,
            }}
          >
            {isExportingPdf ? <KineticLoader size={15} color="#050505" /> : <Feather name="download" size={15} color="#050505" />}
            <Text selectable={false} style={{ color: '#050505', fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.5 }}>
              {tx('DESCARGAR ESTADO DE CUENTA (.PDF)', 'DOWNLOAD STATEMENT (.PDF)')}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>
    );
  }
);

    FILE: src/components/terminal/TerminalLiveStudioRoomModal.tsx

    // === src/components/terminal/TerminalLiveStudioRoomModal.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { ScrollView, StyleSheet, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import {
  fetchLiveStudioRoomRosterB2B,
  ILiveStudioRosterResult,
  ILiveStudioSpotItem,
  mergeLiveStudioSpotsRoster,
} from '../../services/terminalSpeiAndStudioService';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { resolveStudioFrontStageLabel } from '../../services/studioLayoutGridHelper';
import { KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from '../shared/KineticModalHeader3D';
import { KineticLoader, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';

export interface TerminalLiveStudioRoomModalProps {
  visible: boolean;
  service: any;
  localActivePasses?: any[];
  localCompletedPasses?: any[];
  onClose: () => void;
  onOpenConfigure3D: (service: any) => void;
}

export const TerminalLiveStudioRoomModal: React.FC<TerminalLiveStudioRoomModalProps> = ({
  visible,
  service,
  localActivePasses = [],
  localCompletedPasses = [],
  onClose,
  onOpenConfigure3D,
}) => {
  const { tx, tDisc, isLightMode, language } = useKineticPreferences();
  const mountedRef = useRef(true);
  const reqIdRef = useRef(0);
  const lastSrvRef = useRef<any>(null);
  if (service) lastSrvRef.current = service;
  const activeSrv = service || lastSrvRef.current;

  const [cloudRoster, setCloudRoster] = useState<ILiveStudioRosterResult | null>(null);
  const [isRefreshing, setIsRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [selectedSpotNum, setSelectedSpotNum] = useState<number | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    mountedRef.current = true;
    return () => {
      mountedRef.current = false;
    };
  }, []);

  const syncPreview = useMemo<ILiveStudioRosterResult>(() => {
    const sid = String(activeSrv?.id || '').trim();
    const sName = String(activeSrv?.nombre_servicio || activeSrv?.titulo || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const match = (r: any) => {
      const rSid = String(r?.servicio_id || '').trim();
      if (sid && rSid) return rSid === sid;
      const rName = String(r?.servicio || r?.nombre_servicio || r?.titulo_clase || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      return Boolean(sName && rName === sName);
    };
    const rawDisc = String(activeSrv?.disciplina || '').trim();
    const resolvedDisc = rawDisc && rawDisc.toLowerCase() !== 'club' ? rawDisc : String(activeSrv?.categoria || 'Fuerza');
    return mergeLiveStudioSpotsRoster({
      layoutRaw: activeSrv?.studio_layout_json || activeSrv?.atributos?.studio_layout,
      discipline: resolvedDisc,
      eventTitle: activeSrv?.nombre_servicio || activeSrv?.titulo,
      maxCupo: Number(activeSrv?.cupo_maximo ?? activeSrv?.cupo_total ?? 12),
      localActivePasses: (localActivePasses || []).filter(match),
      localCompletedPasses: (localCompletedPasses || []).filter(match),
    });
  }, [activeSrv, localActivePasses, localCompletedPasses]);

  const loadLiveRoster = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!activeSrv) return;
    const reqId = ++reqIdRef.current;
    setIsRefreshing(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetchLiveStudioRoomRosterB2B(
        activeSrv,
        activeSrv?.fecha || null,
        localActivePasses,
        localCompletedPasses
      );
      if (mountedRef.current && reqIdRef.current === reqId) {
        setCloudRoster(res);
      }
    } finally {
      if (mountedRef.current && reqIdRef.current === reqId) {
        setIsRefreshing(false);
      }
    }
  }, [activeSrv, localActivePasses, localCompletedPasses]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!visible || !activeSrv) {
      setCloudRoster(null);
      setSelectedSpotNum(null);
      return;
    }
    void loadLiveRoster();
  }, [visible, activeSrv, loadLiveRoster]);

  const roster = cloudRoster || syncPreview;
  const spotMap = useMemo(() => {
    const m = new Map<string, ILiveStudioSpotItem>();
    roster.spots.forEach((sp) => m.set(`${sp.row}-${sp.col}`, sp));
    return m;
  }, [roster.spots]);

  const selectedSpot = useMemo(
    () => (selectedSpotNum != null ? roster.spots.find((s) => s.spotNumber === selectedSpotNum && s.status !== 'free') || null : null),
    [roster.spots, selectedSpotNum]
  );

  const disabledSet = useMemo(() => new Set(roster.layout.disabledCells || []), [roster.layout.disabledCells]);
  const bgSheet = isLightMode ? '#F8FAFC' : '#0B0F17';
  const cardBg = isLightMode ? '#FFFFFF' : '#121824';
  const borderCol = isLightMode ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.1)';
  const textPrimary = isLightMode ? '#0F172A' : '#FFFFFF';
  const textSub = isLightMode ? '#475569' : '#94A3B8';
  const titleStr = String(activeSrv?.nombre_servicio || activeSrv?.titulo || tx('Sala 3D en Vivo', 'Live 3D Room'));
  const rawDisplayDisc = String(activeSrv?.disciplina || '').trim();
  const discStr = tDisc(rawDisplayDisc && rawDisplayDisc.toLowerCase() !== 'club' ? rawDisplayDisc : String(activeSrv?.categoria || 'Fuerza'));
  const equipmentSingular = useMemo(() => {
    const eq = roster.layout?.equipmentType || 'spot';
    if (eq === 'reformer') return 'Reformer';
    if (eq === 'bike') return tx('Bici', 'Bike');
    if (eq === 'barre') return tx('Barra', 'Barre');
    if (eq === 'bag') return tx('Costal', 'Heavy Bag');
    if (eq === 'court') return tx('Cancha', 'Court');
    return tx('Estación', 'Station');
  }, [roster.layout?.equipmentType, tx]);

  return (
    <ReactNativeModal
      isVisible={visible}
      onBackdropPress={onClose}
      onBackButtonPress={onClose}
      backdropOpacity={0.78}
      backdropTransitionOutTiming={0}
      backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
      hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
      useNativeDriverForBackdrop
      style={st.modalWrap}
    >
      <View style={[st.sheetBox, KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, { backgroundColor: bgSheet, borderColor: borderCol }]}>
        <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={onClose} accentColor={COLORS.cyan} />

        <View style={st.headerRow}>
          <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingRight: 10 }}>
            <Text style={[st.kickerTxt, { color: COLORS.cyan }]}>
              {tx('SUPERVISIÓN DE SALA 3D EN VIVO', 'LIVE 3D STUDIO SUPERVISION')} • {discStr.toUpperCase()}
            </Text>
            <Text style={[st.titleTxt, { color: textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>{titleStr}</Text>
          </View>
          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Light'); void loadLiveRoster(); }}
            style={[st.iconBtn3D, { backgroundColor: cardBg, borderColor: borderCol }]}
            disabled={isRefreshing}
          >
            {isRefreshing ? <KineticLoader size={15} color={COLORS.cyan} /> : <Feather name="refresh-cw" size={15} color={COLORS.cyan} />}
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        <View style={st.kpiRow}>
          <View style={[st.kpiCard, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.12)', borderColor: 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.42)' }]}>
            <Text style={[st.kpiVal, { color: COLORS.green }]}>{roster.checkedInCount}</Text>
            <Text style={[st.kpiLbl, { color: textSub }]}>{tx('Presentes QR', 'QR Present')}</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[st.kpiCard, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.12)', borderColor: 'rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.42)' }]}>
            <Text style={[st.kpiVal, { color: '#A855F7' }]}>{roster.waitingCount}</Text>
            <Text style={[st.kpiLbl, { color: textSub }]}>{tx('En Espera', 'Waiting')}</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[st.kpiCard, { backgroundColor: cardBg, borderColor: borderCol }]}>
            <Text style={[st.kpiVal, { color: textPrimary }]}>{roster.freeCount}</Text>
            <Text style={[st.kpiLbl, { color: textSub }]}>{tx('Libres', 'Free')}</Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        <ScrollView style={{ flex: 1 }} contentContainerStyle={st.scrollBody} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
          <View style={[st.stageBanner, { borderColor: isLightMode ? '#0284C7' : 'rgba(0,229,255,0.45)', backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#E0F2FE' : 'rgba(0,229,255,0.09)' }]}>
            <Feather name="radio" size={13} color={isLightMode ? '#0369A1' : COLORS.cyan} />
            <Text style={[st.stageTxt, { color: isLightMode ? '#0369A1' : COLORS.cyan }]}>
              {resolveStudioFrontStageLabel(activeSrv?.categoria || activeSrv?.disciplina, roster.layout.equipmentType, language === 'en')}
            </Text>
          </View>

          <View style={st.gridContainer}>
            {Array.from({ length: roster.layout.rows }).map((_, rIdx) => (
              <View key={`r_${rIdx}`} style={st.gridRow}>
                {Array.from({ length: roster.layout.cols }).map((__, cIdx) => {
                  const cellKey = `${rIdx}-${cIdx}`;
                  if (disabledSet.has(cellKey)) {
                    return <View key={cellKey} style={st.aisleCell} />;
                  }
                  const sp = spotMap.get(cellKey);
                  if (!sp) return <View key={cellKey} style={st.aisleCell} />;
                  const isPresent = sp.status === 'checked_in';
                  const isReserved = sp.status === 'reserved';
                  const isSel = selectedSpotNum === sp.spotNumber;
                  const cellBg = isPresent
                    ? 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.16)'
                    : isReserved
                      ? 'rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.16)'
                      : cardBg;
                  const cellBorder = isPresent ? COLORS.green : isReserved ? '#A855F7' : borderCol;
                  const accent = isPresent ? COLORS.green : isReserved ? '#A855F7' : textSub;

                  return (
                    <TouchableOpacity
                      key={cellKey}
                      activeOpacity={sp.status === 'free' ? 0.9 : 0.75}
                      onPress={() => {
                        if (sp.status === 'free') return;
                        triggerHaptic('Selection');
                        setSelectedSpotNum((prev) => (prev === sp.spotNumber ? null : sp.spotNumber));
                      }}
                      style={[
                        st.spotCell3D,
                        { backgroundColor: cellBg, borderColor: isSel ? COLORS.cyan : cellBorder, borderBottomColor: cellBorder },
                      ]}
                    >
                      <Text style={[st.spotNumTxt, { color: accent }]}>#{sp.spotNumber}</Text>
                      <Text style={[st.spotNameTxt, { color: sp.status === 'free' ? textSub : textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                        {sp.status === 'free' ? tx('LIBRE', 'FREE') : sp.athleteShortName}
                      </Text>
                      <Text style={[st.spotStateMicro, { color: accent }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                        {isPresent ? 'CHECK-IN QR' : isReserved ? tx('APARTADO 40%', 'BOOKED 40%') : '•'}
                      </Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  );
                })}
              </View>
            ))}
          </View>

          <View style={st.legendRow}>
            <View style={st.legendItem}><View style={[st.dot, { backgroundColor: COLORS.green }]} /><Text style={[st.legendTxt, { color: textSub }]}>CHECK-IN QR</Text></View>
            <View style={st.legendItem}><View style={[st.dot, { backgroundColor: '#A855F7' }]} /><Text style={[st.legendTxt, { color: textSub }]}>{tx('APARTADO 40%', 'BOOKED 40%')}</Text></View>
            <View style={st.legendItem}><View style={[st.dot, { backgroundColor: '#64748B' }]} /><Text style={[st.legendTxt, { color: textSub }]}>{tx('LIBRE', 'FREE')}</Text></View>
          </View>

          {selectedSpot && (
            <View style={[st.detailCard, { backgroundColor: cardBg, borderColor: selectedSpot.checkedIn ? COLORS.green : '#A855F7' }]}>
              <View style={st.detailTop}>
                <Text style={[st.detailSpotBadge, { color: selectedSpot.checkedIn ? COLORS.green : '#A855F7' }]}>
                  {equipmentSingular.toUpperCase()} #{selectedSpot.spotNumber} • {selectedSpot.checkedIn ? 'CHECK-IN QR' : tx('APARTADO 40%', 'BOOKED 40%')}
                </Text>
                <Text style={[st.detailFolio, { color: textSub }]}>#{selectedSpot.folio || 'KNT'}</Text>
              </View>
              <Text style={[st.detailName, { color: textPrimary }]}>{selectedSpot.athleteShortName}</Text>
              <Text style={[st.detailTier, { color: textSub }]}>{tx('Membresía:', 'Tier:')} {selectedSpot.vipTier}</Text>
            </View>
          )}
        </ScrollView>

        <View style={[st.footerBar, { borderTopColor: borderCol, backgroundColor: bgSheet }]}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={st.config3DBtn}
            onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Medium'); onOpenConfigure3D(activeSrv); }}
          >
            <Feather name="sliders" size={15} color="#05070A" />
            <Text style={st.config3DBtnTxt}>{tx('CONFIGURAR SALA 3D', 'CONFIGURE 3D ROOM')}</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>
    </ReactNativeModal>
  );
};

    // === src/components/terminal/TerminalServiceDetailsModal.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  Animated,
  Platform,
  ScrollView,
  Text,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { resolveCategoryImage } from '../../constants/exploreData';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from '../shared/KineticModalHeader3D';
import { SmartImage } from '../SharedUI';
import { styles as baseStyles } from '../TerminalSecondaryModals.styles';

const NO_SEL =
  Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as any) : null;
const DARK_INK_DATA: any =
  Platform.OS === 'web' ? { dataSet: { kineticInk: 'dark' } } : {};

export const ServiceDetailsModal = memo(
  ({
    selectedServiceDetail,
    onClose,
    formatDateLabel,
    openEditModal,
    onDeleteService,
    triggerHaptic,
  }: any) => {
    const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
    const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, tx, td, fmtDate } = useKineticPreferences();
    const styles = useMemo(
      () =>
        adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme, [
          'dragPillBox',
          'premiumDetailCategoryBadge',
          'premiumDetailCategoryText',
          'frequencyCleanBadge',
          'frequencyCleanBadgeText',
          'premiumDetailBtn',
          'premiumDetailBtnText',
        ]),
      [resolvedTheme]
    );
    const onMediaProps: any = Platform.OS === 'web' ? { dataSet: { kineticOnMedia: 'true' } } : {};

    const cachedServiceRef = useRef<any>(null);
    const actionTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
    if (selectedServiceDetail) {
      cachedServiceRef.current = selectedServiceDetail;
    }
    const activeItem = selectedServiceDetail || cachedServiceRef.current;
    const swipeHintAnim = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;

    useEffect(() => () => {
      if (actionTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(actionTimerRef.current);
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => {
      if (selectedServiceDetail) {
        swipeHintAnim.setValue(0);
        Animated.sequence([
          Animated.delay(220),
          Animated.timing(swipeHintAnim, {
            toValue: 10,
            duration: 180,
            useNativeDriver: Platform.OS !== 'web',
          }),
          Animated.spring(swipeHintAnim, {
            toValue: 0,
            friction: 5,
            useNativeDriver: Platform.OS !== 'web',
          }),
        ]).start();
      }
    }, [selectedServiceDetail, swipeHintAnim]);

    const formattedDate = useMemo(() => {
      if (!activeItem?.fecha) return tx('Fecha por definir', 'Date TBD');
      if (formatDateLabel) return formatDateLabel(activeItem.fecha);
      const raw = String(activeItem.fecha).trim();
      const safeIso = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(raw) ? `${raw}T12:00:00` : raw;
      return (
        fmtDate(safeIso, { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short' }) ||
        raw
      );
    }, [activeItem?.fecha, formatDateLabel, fmtDate, tx]);

    const formattedTime = String(activeItem?.hora_inicio || '').substring(0, 5);
    const duracionMin = Number(activeItem?.duracion_minutos ?? activeItem?.duracion_min ?? 60);
    const coachName =
      activeItem?.instructor_nombre || tx('Coach Oficial Kinetic', 'Official Kinetic Coach');
    const requisitosText = td(
      activeItem?.requisitos_traer ||
        tx(
          'Agua / Hidratación personal, Toalla de entrenamiento',
          'Personal hydration / water, Training towel'
        )
    );
    const edadText = td(activeItem?.edad_minima || tx('18+ Adultos', '18+ Adults'));

    const { cleanDesc, frequencyLabel } = useMemo(() => {
      const raw = String(activeItem?.descripcion || '');
      const match = raw.match(/\[Frecuencia Programada:\s*([^\]]+)\]/i);
      const rawFreq = (match?.[1] || activeItem?.frecuencia || tx('Única Vez', 'One-Time')).trim();
      const stripped = raw.replace(/\[Frecuencia Programada:[^\]]*\]/gi, '').trim();
      return {
        cleanDesc: stripped
          ? td(stripped)
          : tx(
              'Experiencia exclusiva diseñada para la comunidad Kinetic.',
              'Exclusive experience designed for the Kinetic community.'
            ),
        frequencyLabel: td(rawFreq),
      };
    }, [activeItem?.descripcion, activeItem?.frecuencia, td, tx]);

    const coverUri = useMemo(
      () => resolveCategoryImage(activeItem?.categoria, activeItem?.nombre_servicio),
      [activeItem?.categoria, activeItem?.nombre_servicio]
    );

    const categoryBadgeLabel = td(
      activeItem?.categoria || tx('Experiencia', 'Experience')
    ).toUpperCase();

    return (
      <ReactNativeModal
        isVisible={Boolean(selectedServiceDetail)}
        style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
        backdropColor={isLightMode ? '#0F172A' : '#050505'}
        backdropOpacity={isLightMode ? 0.5 : 0.92}
        backdropTransitionOutTiming={0} backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
        hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
        animationInTiming={200}
        animationOutTiming={180}
        onBackdropPress={onClose}
        onBackButtonPress={onClose}
        useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
        useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
      >
        <Animated.View
          style={[
            styles.detailsSheet,
            KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS,
            {
              paddingTop: 8,
              backgroundColor: themeColors.background,
              transform: [{ translateY: swipeHintAnim }],
            },
            isLightMode && { borderColor: '#38BDF8', borderTopColor: '#0284C7' },
            NO_SEL,
          ]}
        >
          <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={onClose} style={{ marginBottom: 8 }} />

          <ScrollView
            style={{ flex: 1 }}
            showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
            bounces={false}
            overScrollMode="never"
            nestedScrollEnabled
            keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
            directionalLockEnabled
            contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 36 }}
          >
            <View
              style={{
                position: 'relative',
                width: '100%',
                height: 224,
                backgroundColor: themeColors.background,
              }}
              {...onMediaProps}
            >
              <SmartImage uri={coverUri} style={styles.detailsCoverImage} />
              <View style={styles.coverBadgesRow} {...onMediaProps}>
                <View style={[styles.premiumDetailCategoryBadge, { elevation: 0 }]}>
                  <Text selectable={false} style={styles.premiumDetailCategoryText}>{categoryBadgeLabel}</Text>
                </View>
                {Boolean(frequencyLabel) && (
                  <View style={[styles.frequencyCleanBadge, { backgroundColor: '#05080F', elevation: 0 }]}>
                    <Feather name="repeat" size={10} color={COLORS.cyan} />
                    <Text selectable={false} style={[styles.frequencyCleanBadgeText, { color: '#FFFFFF' }]}>
                      {frequencyLabel.toUpperCase()}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                )}
              </View>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.premiumDetailContent}>
              <Text style={[styles.premiumDetailTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                {td(activeItem?.nombre_servicio || tx('Experiencia Kinetic', 'Kinetic Experience'))}
              </Text>
              <View style={[styles.premiumDetailMetaCard, { backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#F8FAFC' : '#090C12', elevation: 0 }, NO_SEL]}>
                <View style={styles.premiumDetailRow}>
                  <Feather name="calendar" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                  <Text style={styles.premiumDetailRowText}>
                    {formattedDate}
                    {formattedTime ? tx(` • ${formattedTime} HRS`, ` • ${formattedTime}`) : ''} ({duracionMin} min)
                  </Text>
                </View>
                <View style={styles.premiumDetailRow}>
                  <Feather name="user-check" size={14} color={themeColors.purpleText} />
                  <Text style={styles.premiumDetailRowText}>
                    {coachName} • {edadText}
                  </Text>
                </View>
                <View style={styles.premiumDetailRow}>
                  <Feather name="shopping-bag" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                  <Text style={styles.premiumDetailRowText} numberOfLines={2}>
                    {requisitosText}
                  </Text>
                </View>
                <View style={styles.premiumDetailRow}>
                  <Feather name="map-pin" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                  <Text style={styles.premiumDetailRowText} numberOfLines={2}>
                    {activeItem?.ubicacion?.startsWith('http') ||
                    activeItem?.ubicacion?.includes('api=1&query=')
                      ? tx('Ubicación sincronizada en Google Maps', 'Location synced in Google Maps')
                      : activeItem?.ubicacion || tx('Sede Oficial Kinetic', 'Official Kinetic Venue')}
                  </Text>
                </View>
                <View style={[styles.premiumDetailRow, { marginBottom: 0 }]}>
                  <Feather name="users" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                  <Text style={styles.premiumDetailRowText}>
                    {tx('Cupo Máximo:', 'Max Capacity:')} {activeItem?.cupo_maximo ?? activeItem?.cupo_total ?? 15}{' '}
                    {tx('Atletas', 'Athletes')}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              </View>

              <Text style={styles.premiumDetailDescriptionLabel}>
                {tx('SOBRE ESTA EXPERIENCIA', 'ABOUT THIS EXPERIENCE')}
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.premiumDetailDescription}>{cleanDesc}</Text>

              {onDeleteService && activeItem?.id && (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={[styles.deleteServiceInlineBtn, { backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#FEF2F2' : '#1F0A10', elevation: 0 }, NO_SEL]}
                  activeOpacity={0.8}
                  onPress={() => {
                    triggerHaptic?.('Warning');
                    const idToDelete = activeItem.id;
                    onClose();
                    if (actionTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(actionTimerRef.current);
                    actionTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => onDeleteService(idToDelete), 320);
                  }}
                >
                  <Feather name="trash-2" size={15} color={isLightMode ? '#DC2626' : COLORS.red} style={{ marginRight: 8 }} />
                  <Text selectable={false} style={styles.deleteServiceInlineText}>
                    {tx('ELIMINAR EXPERIENCIA', 'DELETE EXPERIENCE')}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              )}
            </View>
          </ScrollView>

          <View
            style={[
              styles.premiumDetailFooter,
              { paddingBottom: insets.bottom > 0 ? insets.bottom + 15 : 22 },
            ]}
          >
            <View>
              <Text style={styles.premiumDetailPriceLabel}>{tx('Acceso', 'Access')}</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.premiumDetailPrice, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
                {activeItem?.precio_kc ?? 0} KC
              </Text>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.footerActionsRow}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[styles.backThumbBtn, { backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#F1F5F9' : '#111622', elevation: 0 }, NO_SEL]}
                activeOpacity={0.8}
                onPress={() => {
                  triggerHaptic?.('Light');
                  onClose();
                }}
              >
                <Feather name="arrow-left" size={15} color={themeColors.textPrimary} />
                <Text selectable={false} style={[styles.backThumbBtnText, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                  {tx('ATRÁS', 'BACK')}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
              <TouchableOpacity
                {...DARK_INK_DATA}
                style={[styles.premiumDetailBtn, NO_SEL]}
                activeOpacity={0.8}
                onPress={() => {
                  triggerHaptic?.('Light');
                  const srv = activeItem;
                  onClose();
                  if (actionTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(actionTimerRef.current);
                  actionTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => openEditModal(srv), 320);
                }}
              >
                <Feather name="edit-2" size={15} color="#050505" style={{ marginRight: 6 }} />
                <Text selectable={false} style={styles.premiumDetailBtnText}>{tx('MODIFICAR', 'EDIT')}</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </View>
        </Animated.View>
      </ReactNativeModal>
    );
  }
);
