    FILE: src/components/TerminalActionModals.tsx

    // === src/components/TerminalActionModals.tsx === //
import { Feather, Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import { Animated, Platform, Pressable, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { COLORS } from '../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { triggerHaptic } from './SharedUI';
import { styles as baseStyles } from './TerminalActionModals.styles';

export { TerminalLiquidityAnalyticsView } from './terminal/TerminalLiquidityAnalyticsView';

const NO_SEL =
  Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as any) : null;
const DARK_INK_DATA: any =
  Platform.OS === 'web' ? { dataSet: { kineticInk: 'dark' } } : {};
const press3D = (pressed: boolean, baseLip = 4.8) =>
  pressed ? { borderBottomWidth: 1.5, transform: [{ translateY: Math.max(1.5, baseLip - 2) }] } : null;

export const TerminalAuditModal = React.memo(
  ({ selectedAuditItem, setSelectedAuditItem, isAdminView }: any) => {
    const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, tx, td, fmtDateTime } = useKineticPreferences();
    const styles = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);
    const frozenAuditRef = useRef<any>(null);
    if (selectedAuditItem) {
      frozenAuditRef.current = selectedAuditItem;
    }
    const effectiveItem = selectedAuditItem || frozenAuditRef.current;
    const estadoNormalizado = String(effectiveItem?.estado || 'completada').toLowerCase().trim();
    const isCanceled = estadoNormalizado === 'cancelada' || estadoNormalizado === 'reembolsada';
    const isCompleted = !isCanceled;
    const athleteName = Array.isArray(effectiveItem?.perfiles)
      ? effectiveItem?.perfiles[0]?.nombre
      : effectiveItem?.perfiles?.nombre ||
        effectiveItem?.atleta_nombre ||
        tx('Atleta Kinetic', 'Kinetic Athlete');
    const costoBase = Number(effectiveItem?.costo_total ?? effectiveItem?.precio_kc ?? 0) || 0;
    const montoLiquidado =
      effectiveItem?.monto_puerta != null && effectiveItem?.monto_puerta !== '' && !Number.isNaN(Number(effectiveItem.monto_puerta))
        ? Number(effectiveItem.monto_puerta)
        : Math.floor(costoBase * 0.6);
    const montoEscrow =
      effectiveItem?.monto_escrow != null && effectiveItem?.monto_escrow !== '' && !Number.isNaN(Number(effectiveItem.monto_escrow))
        ? Number(effectiveItem.monto_escrow)
        : Math.max(0, costoBase - montoLiquidado);
    const isMasterPass =
      isCompleted &&
      (Boolean(effectiveItem?.isMasterPass || effectiveItem?.es_pase_maestro) ||
        (montoLiquidado === 0 && montoEscrow === 0));
    const folioStr =
      String(effectiveItem?.id || '')
        .replace(/-/g, '')
        .substring(0, 8)
        .toUpperCase() || 'KNT-PASS';
    const rawAuditDate = effectiveItem?.updated_at || effectiveItem?.created_at;
    const auditDateFormatted = rawAuditDate
      ? fmtDateTime(rawAuditDate, { dateStyle: 'medium', timeStyle: 'short' })
      : tx('No registrada', 'Not recorded');

    const redInk = isLightMode ? '#DC2626' : COLORS.red;
    const greenInk = isLightMode ? '#047857' : '#34D399';

    return (
      <ReactNativeModal
        isVisible={Boolean(selectedAuditItem)}
        animationIn="fadeInUp"
        animationOut="fadeOutDown"
        animationInTiming={200}
        animationOutTiming={180}
        backdropOpacity={isLightMode ? 0.5 : 0.9}
        backdropColor={isLightMode ? '#0F172A' : '#030407'}
        backdropTransitionOutTiming={0} backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
        hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
        useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
        useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
        onBackdropPress={() => setSelectedAuditItem(null)}
        onBackButtonPress={() => setSelectedAuditItem(null)}
        style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'center', padding: 20 }}
      >
        <View
          style={[
            styles.auditCardClean,
            isLightMode && {
              backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
              borderColor: '#06B6D4',
              borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
              borderBottomColor: '#00B8D4',
              shadowColor: '#00B8D4',
              shadowOpacity: 0.22,
            },
            isCanceled &&
              (isLightMode
                ? {
                    borderColor: '#F87171',
                    borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                    borderBottomColor: '#DC2626',
                    shadowColor: '#DC2626',
                    shadowOpacity: 0.24,
                  }
                : {
                    borderColor: '#EF4444',
                    borderTopColor: '#FCA5A5',
                    borderBottomColor: '#991B1B',
                    shadowColor: COLORS.red,
                  }),
            NO_SEL,
          ]}
        >
          <View style={styles.auditHeader}>
            <View
              style={[
                styles.auditIconCircle,
                isLightMode && !isCanceled && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#06B6D4', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0891B2' },
                isCanceled && (isLightMode
                  ? { backgroundColor: '#FEF2F2', borderColor: '#F87171', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#DC2626' }
                  : { backgroundColor: '#2A0A0D', borderColor: COLORS.red, borderTopColor: '#FCA5A5', borderBottomColor: '#991B1B' }),
              ]}
            >
              <Feather
                name={isCanceled ? 'x-octagon' : isMasterPass ? 'shield' : 'file-text'}
                size={22}
                color={isCanceled ? redInk : isMasterPass ? greenInk : themeColors.cyanText}
              />
            </View>
            <Text style={[styles.auditTitle, isCanceled && { color: redInk }]}>
              {isCanceled ? tx('AUDITORÍA DE CANCELACIÓN', 'CANCELLATION AUDIT') : tx('AUDITORÍA DE ACCESO', 'ACCESS CHECK-IN AUDIT')}
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.auditSubtitle}>
              {td(effectiveItem?.servicio || effectiveItem?.titulo_clase || effectiveItem?.nombre_servicio || tx('Experiencia Kinetic', 'Kinetic Experience'))}
            </Text>
          </View>

          {isMasterPass && (
            <View style={[styles.masterPassBadge, isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#ECFDF5', borderColor: '#10B981', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#047857' }, NO_SEL]}>
              <View style={styles.masterPassRow}>
                <Ionicons name="shield-checkmark" size={14} color={greenInk} />
                <Text style={[styles.masterPassBadgeTitle, { color: greenInk }]}>
                  {tx('PASE MAESTRO OFICIAL (0 KC)', 'OFFICIAL MASTER PASS (0 KC)')}
                </Text>
              </View>
              <Text style={[styles.masterPassBadgeSub, isLightMode && { color: '#065F46' }]}>
                {tx('Check-in oficial validado sin cargo en puerta', 'Official check-in validated with no front-desk charge')}
              </Text>
            </View>
          )}

          <View style={[styles.auditBody, isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC', borderColor: '#BAE6FD', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7' }, NO_SEL]}>
            <View style={styles.auditRow}>
              <Text style={styles.auditLabel}>{tx('Folio de Operación:', 'Transaction Folio:')}</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.auditValue, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>#KNT-{folioStr}</Text>
            </View>
            {isAdminView && (
              <View style={styles.auditRow}>
                <Text style={styles.auditLabel}>{tx('Club Operador:', 'Operating Venue:')}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.auditValue} numberOfLines={1}>
                  {effectiveItem?.negocio_nombre || tx('Sede Kinetic', 'Kinetic Venue')}
                </Text>
              </View>
            )}
            <View style={styles.auditRow}>
              <Text style={styles.auditLabel}>{tx('Atleta:', 'Athlete:')}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.auditValue} numberOfLines={1}>
                {athleteName}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.auditRow}>
              <Text style={styles.auditLabel}>{tx('Fecha Movimiento:', 'Transaction Date:')}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.auditValue}>{auditDateFormatted}</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={[styles.dividerInner, isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#CBD5E1' }]} />
            {isCompleted ? (
              <>
                <View style={styles.auditRow}>
                  <Text style={styles.auditLabel}>{tx('Precio Base (100%):', 'Base Price (100%):')}</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.auditValue}>{costoBase} KC</Text>
                </View>
                <View style={styles.auditRow}>
                  <Text style={styles.auditLabel}>
                    {tx('Reserva Protegida 40/60 (40%):', 'Protected 40/60 Escrow (40%):')}
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.auditValue, { color: themeColors.purpleText }]}>
                    {montoEscrow} KC
                  </Text>
                </View>
                <View style={styles.auditRow}>
                  <Text style={styles.auditLabel}>
                    {isMasterPass
                      ? tx('Cargo en Puerta (Pase Maestro):', 'Front-Desk Charge (Master Pass):')
                      : tx('Liquidado en Puerta (60%):', 'Settled at Front Desk (60%):')}
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.auditValueDoor, isMasterPass && { color: greenInk }]}>
                    {isMasterPass ? '0 KC' : `+${montoLiquidado} KC`}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              </>
            ) : (
              <View style={styles.auditRow}>
                <Text style={styles.auditLabel}>{tx('Estado Financiero:', 'Financial Status:')}</Text>
                <Text style={{ color: redInk, fontWeight: '900', fontSize: 12 }}>
                  {costoBase === 0 && montoLiquidado === 0 && montoEscrow === 0
                    ? tx('PASE MAESTRO CANCELADO (0 KC)', 'CANCELED MASTER PASS (0 KC)')
                    : tx('REEMBOLSADO AL ATLETA', 'REFUNDED TO ATHLETE')}
                </Text>
              </View>
            )}
          </View>
          <Pressable
            {...(!isCanceled ? DARK_INK_DATA : {})}
            style={({ pressed }) => [
              styles.auditCloseBtnClean,
              isCanceled && styles.auditCloseBtnCanceled,
              isCanceled && isLightMode && {
                backgroundColor: '#FEF2F2',
                borderColor: '#F87171',
                borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                borderBottomColor: '#DC2626',
              },
              press3D(pressed, isCanceled ? 4.5 : 4.8),
              NO_SEL,
            ]}
            onPress={() => {
              triggerHaptic('Selection');
              setSelectedAuditItem(null);
            }}
          >
            <Text selectable={false} style={[styles.auditCloseBtnText, isCanceled && { color: redInk }]}>
              {tx('CERRAR AUDITORÍA', 'CLOSE AUDIT')}
            </Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>
      </ReactNativeModal>
    );
  }
);

export const TerminalSuccessModal = React.memo(
  ({ showSuccessModal, setShowSuccessModal, successDetails, pulseAnim }: any) => {
    const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
    const styles = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);
    const safePulse = pulseAnim ?? 1;
    const frozenSuccessRef = useRef<any>(null);
    if (successDetails) {
      frozenSuccessRef.current = successDetails;
    }
    const effectiveSuccess = successDetails || frozenSuccessRef.current;
    const athleteStr = effectiveSuccess?.nombre || tx('Atleta Kinetic', 'Kinetic Athlete');
    const amountStr = effectiveSuccess?.monto_puerta ?? effectiveSuccess?.monto ?? 0;
    const doorNumeric = Number(amountStr) || 0;
    const escrowNumeric = Number(effectiveSuccess?.monto_escrow ?? effectiveSuccess?.costo_total ?? 0) || 0;
    const isMasterPass =
      Boolean(effectiveSuccess?.isMasterPass || effectiveSuccess?.es_pase_maestro) ||
      (Boolean(effectiveSuccess) && doorNumeric === 0 && escrowNumeric === 0);
    const spotNum = Number(effectiveSuccess?.spotNumber) >= 1 ? Math.round(Number(effectiveSuccess.spotNumber)) : null;
    const greenInk = isLightMode ? '#047857' : '#34D399';

    return (
      <ReactNativeModal
        isVisible={Boolean(showSuccessModal)}
        animationIn="fadeInUp"
        animationOut="fadeOutDown"
        animationInTiming={200}
        animationOutTiming={180}
        backdropOpacity={isLightMode ? 0.5 : 0.9}
        backdropColor={isLightMode ? '#0F172A' : '#030407'}
        backdropTransitionOutTiming={0} backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
        hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
        useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
        useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
        onBackdropPress={() => setShowSuccessModal(false)}
        onBackButtonPress={() => setShowSuccessModal(false)}
        style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'center', padding: 20 }}
      >
        <View
          style={[
            styles.successCardClean,
            isLightMode && {
              backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
              borderColor: '#06B6D4',
              borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
              borderBottomColor: '#00B8D4',
              shadowColor: '#00B8D4',
              shadowOpacity: 0.22,
            },
            NO_SEL,
          ]}
        >
          <Animated.View style={[styles.lightningContainer, { transform: [{ scale: safePulse }] }]}>
            <View
              style={[
                styles.lightningGlow,
                isLightMode && {
                  backgroundColor: isMasterPass ? '#ECFDF5' : '#ECFEFF',
                  borderColor: isMasterPass ? '#10B981' : '#06B6D4',
                  borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                  borderBottomColor: isMasterPass ? '#047857' : '#0891B2',
                },
                !isLightMode && isMasterPass && {
                  backgroundColor: '#06241B',
                  borderColor: '#10B981',
                  borderTopColor: '#A7F3D0',
                  borderBottomColor: '#065F46',
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Ionicons
                name={isMasterPass ? 'shield-checkmark' : 'flash'}
                size={34}
                color={isMasterPass ? greenInk : themeColors.cyanText}
              />
            </View>
          </Animated.View>
          <View style={styles.successHeader}>
            <Text style={styles.successTitle}>{tx('¡CHECK-IN QR EXITOSO!', 'QR CHECK-IN VERIFIED!')}</Text>
            <Text style={[styles.successSubtitle, isLightMode && { color: '#047857' }]}>
              {isMasterPass
                ? tx('CHECK-IN OFICIAL SIN CARGO EN PUERTA', 'OFFICIAL CHECK-IN • NO FRONT-DESK CHARGE')
                : tx('LIQUIDACIÓN 60% EN PUERTA ACREDITADA', '60% FRONT-DESK SETTLEMENT CREDITED')}
            </Text>
            {spotNum !== null && (
              <View
                style={[
                  {
                    marginTop: 8, alignSelf: 'center', flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6,
                    paddingHorizontal: 12, paddingVertical: 5, borderRadius: 999, borderWidth: 1.2,
                    borderTopWidth: 1.8, borderBottomWidth: 3.5, elevation: 0,
                    backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#ECFEFF' : '#082635',
                    borderColor: isLightMode ? '#06B6D4' : COLORS.cyan,
                    borderTopColor: isLightMode ? '#FFFFFF' : '#B8FFFF',
                    borderBottomColor: isLightMode ? '#0891B2' : '#006B78',
                  },
                  NO_SEL,
                ]}
              >
                <Feather name="grid" size={12} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                <Text selectable={false} style={{ color: themeColors.cyanText, fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.8 }}>
                  {`SPOT #${spotNum}`}
                </Text>
              </View>
            )}
          </View>

          {isMasterPass && (
            <View
              style={[
                styles.masterPassBadge,
                isLightMode && {
                  backgroundColor: '#ECFDF5',
                  borderColor: '#10B981',
                  borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                  borderBottomColor: '#047857',
                },
                NO_SEL,
              ]}
            >
              <View style={styles.masterPassRow}>
                <Ionicons name="ribbon" size={14} color={greenInk} />
                <Text style={[styles.masterPassBadgeTitle, { color: greenInk }]}>
                  {tx('PASE MAESTRO OFICIAL (0 KC)', 'OFFICIAL MASTER PASS (0 KC)')}
                </Text>
              </View>
              <Text style={[styles.masterPassBadgeSub, isLightMode && { color: '#065F46' }]}>
                {tx(
                  'Check-in oficial validado sin cargo en puerta',
                  'Official check-in validated with no front-desk charge'
                )}
              </Text>
            </View>
          )}

          <View
            style={[
              styles.successMessageBox,
              isLightMode && {
                backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC',
                borderColor: '#BAE6FD',
                borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                borderBottomColor: '#0284C7',
              },
              NO_SEL,
            ]}
          >
            {isMasterPass ? (
              <Text style={[styles.successMainText, isLightMode && { color: '#1E293B' }]}>
                {tx(
                  'Acceso oficial validado con llave dinámica TOTP para ',
                  'Official access verified via dynamic TOTP key for '
                )}
                <Text style={[styles.highlightText, { color: greenInk }]}>{athleteStr}</Text>
                {tx(
                  '. Check-in oficial validado sin cargo en puerta (0 KC).',
                  '. Official check-in validated with no front-desk charge (0 KC).'
                )}
              </Text>
            ) : (
              <Text style={[styles.successMainText, isLightMode && { color: '#1E293B' }]}>
                {tx(
                  'Acceso validado con llave dinámica TOTP para ',
                  'Access verified via dynamic TOTP key for '
                )}
                <Text style={styles.highlightText}>{athleteStr}</Text>
                {tx('. Se han acreditado ', '. Credited ')}
                <Text style={styles.highlightText}>+{amountStr} KC</Text>
                {tx(
                  ' a la liquidez de recepción y liberado el 40% de reserva protegida 40/60.',
                  ' to front-desk liquidity and released the 40% protected 40/60 escrow.'
                )}
              </Text>
            )}
          </View>
          <Pressable
            {...DARK_INK_DATA}
            style={({ pressed }) => [styles.auditCloseBtnClean, press3D(pressed, 4.8), NO_SEL]}
            onPress={() => {
              triggerHaptic('Selection');
              setShowSuccessModal(false);

          >
            <Text selectable={false} style={styles.auditCloseBtnText}>

    FILE: src/components/TerminalOnboardingSelector.tsx

    // === src/components/TerminalOnboardingSelector.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  KeyboardAvoidingView,
  Platform,
  Pressable,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { isWellnessCategory } from '../constants/exploreData';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { COLORS, globalStyles } from '../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { translateDiscipline } from '../utils/kineticSmartTranslator';
import { KineticListFilterSortBar } from './shared/KineticListFilterSortBar';
import { KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from './shared/KineticModalHeader3D';

const NO_SEL =
  Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as any) : null;
const press3D = (pressed: boolean, baseLip = 4.2) =>
  pressed ? { borderBottomWidth: 1.5, transform: [{ translateY: Math.max(1.5, baseLip - 2) }] } : null;

export const TerminalOnboardingSelector = ({
  showOnbCityPicker,
  showOnbCatPicker,
  setShowOnbCityPicker,
  setShowOnbCatPicker,
  nuevoSocio,
  setNuevoSocio,
  CIUDADES_PILOTO = [],
  CATEGORY_OPTIONS = [],
  triggerHaptic,
}: any) => {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, language, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
  const styles = useMemo(
    () => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme),
    [resolvedTheme]
  );
  const isVisible = Boolean(showOnbCityPicker || showOnbCatPicker);
  const lastCityModeRef = useRef<boolean>(true);
  if (showOnbCityPicker) lastCityModeRef.current = true;
  else if (showOnbCatPicker) lastCityModeRef.current = false;
  const isCityMode = showOnbCityPicker ? true : showOnbCatPicker ? false : lastCityModeRef.current;
  const isCatMode = !isCityMode;

  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [divFilter, setDivFilter] = useState<'all' | 'fitness' | 'wellness'>('all');
  const [sortOrder, setSortOrder] = useState<'az' | 'za'>('az');

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isVisible) {
      setSearchQuery('');
      setDivFilter('all');
      setSortOrder('az');
    }
  }, [isVisible]);

  const onClose = () => {
    triggerHaptic?.('Light');
    setShowOnbCityPicker(false);
    setShowOnbCatPicker(false);
  };

  const { fitnessCount, wellnessCount } = useMemo(() => {
    let fit = 0;
    let well = 0;
    (CATEGORY_OPTIONS || []).forEach((c: string) => {
      if (isWellnessCategory(c)) well += 1;
      else fit += 1;
    });
    return { fitnessCount: fit, wellnessCount: well };
  }, [CATEGORY_OPTIONS]);

  const filteredOptions = useMemo(() => {
    const rawList: string[] = isCityMode ? CIUDADES_PILOTO : CATEGORY_OPTIONS;
    const q = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
    const filtered = (rawList || []).filter((item: string) => {
      if (isCatMode) {
        const isWell = isWellnessCategory(item);
        if (divFilter === 'fitness' && isWell) return false;
        if (divFilter === 'wellness' && !isWell) return false;
      }
      if (!q) return true;
      const displayLabel = isCatMode ? translateDiscipline(item, language) : item;
      return item.toLowerCase().includes(q) || displayLabel.toLowerCase().includes(q);
    });
    return [...filtered].sort((a, b) => {
      const labelA = isCatMode ? translateDiscipline(a, language) : a;
      const labelB = isCatMode ? translateDiscipline(b, language) : b;
      const cmp = labelA.localeCompare(labelB);
      return sortOrder === 'za' ? -cmp : cmp;
    });
  }, [
    isCityMode,
    isCatMode,
    CIUDADES_PILOTO,
    CATEGORY_OPTIONS,
    searchQuery,
    divFilter,
    sortOrder,
    language,
  ]);

  return (
    <ReactNativeModal
      isVisible={isVisible}
      style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
      backdropColor={isLightMode ? '#0F172A' : COLORS.background}
      backdropOpacity={isLightMode ? 0.5 : 0.92}
      backdropTransitionOutTiming={0} backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
      hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
      animationInTiming={200}
      animationOutTiming={180}
      onBackdropPress={onClose}
      onBackButtonPress={onClose}
      useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
      useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
      avoidKeyboard
    >
      <KeyboardAvoidingView
        behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
        style={{ width: '100%', flex: 1, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
      >
        <View
          style={[
            globalStyles.bottomSheet,
            KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS,
            styles.sheetSurface,
            isLightMode && {
              backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
              borderColor: '#38BDF8',
              borderTopColor: '#0284C7',
            },
            { paddingBottom: insets.bottom > 0 ? insets.bottom + 20 : 28 },
          ]}
        >
          <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={onClose} />

          <View style={styles.sheetHeader}>
            <Text style={[styles.sheetTag, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
              {isCityMode
                ? tx('DIRECTORIO DE CIUDADES', 'CITIES DIRECTORY')
                : tx(
                    `CATÁLOGO OFICIAL • ${(CATEGORY_OPTIONS || []).length} DISCIPLINAS`,
                    `OFFICIAL CATALOG • ${(CATEGORY_OPTIONS || []).length} DISCIPLINES`
                  )}
            </Text>
            <Text style={[styles.sheetTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
              {isCityMode
                ? tx('Ciudad Base', 'Base City')
                : tx('Disciplina Principal', 'Primary Discipline')}
            </Text>
          </View>

          <KineticListFilterSortBar
            searchQuery={searchQuery}
            onChangeSearch={setSearchQuery}
            searchPlaceholder={isCityMode ? tx('Buscar ciudad...', 'Search city...') : tx('Buscar disciplina...', 'Search discipline...')}
            filterChips={
              isCatMode
                ? [
                    { key: 'all', label: tx('Todas', 'All'), count: (CATEGORY_OPTIONS || []).length },
                    { key: 'fitness', label: tx('Fitness & Deporte', 'Fitness & Sports'), count: fitnessCount },
                    { key: 'wellness', label: tx('Bienestar & Spa', 'Wellness & Spa'), count: wellnessCount },
                  ]
                : undefined
            }
            activeFilter={divFilter}
            onSelectFilter={(k) => setDivFilter(k as 'all' | 'fitness' | 'wellness')}
            sortOptions={[
              { key: 'az', label: 'A-Z' },
              { key: 'za', label: 'Z-A' },
            ]}
            activeSort={sortOrder}
            onSelectSort={(k) => setSortOrder(k as 'az' | 'za')}
          />

          <ScrollView
            style={{ flex: 1 }}
            bounces={false}
            directionalLockEnabled
            keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
            showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
            contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 20 }}
          >
            {filteredOptions.map((item: string) => {
              const isActive = isCityMode
                ? nuevoSocio?.ubicacion === item
                : nuevoSocio?.disciplina === item;
              const isWell = isCatMode && isWellnessCategory(item);

              return (
                <Pressable
                  key={item}
                  style={({ pressed }) => [
                    styles.sheetOption,
                    isLightMode && {
                      backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC',
                      borderColor: '#CBD5E1',
                      borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                      borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8',
                    },
                    isActive &&
                      (isLightMode
                        ? {
                            backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF',
                            borderColor: '#06B6D4',
                            borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                            borderBottomColor: '#0891B2',
                          }
                        : styles.sheetOptionActive),
                    press3D(pressed, 4.2),
                    NO_SEL,
                  ]}
                  onPress={() => {
                    triggerHaptic?.('Light');
                    if (isCityMode) setNuevoSocio({ ...nuevoSocio, ubicacion: item });
                    else setNuevoSocio({ ...nuevoSocio, disciplina: item });
                    setShowOnbCityPicker(false);
                    setShowOnbCatPicker(false);
                  }}
                >
                  <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingRight: 8 }}>
                    <Text
                      selectable={false}
                      style={[
                        styles.sheetOptionText,
                        isActive && { color: themeColors.cyanText, fontWeight: '900' },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {isCatMode ? translateDiscipline(item, language) : item}
                    </Text>
                    {isCatMode && (
                      <Text
                        selectable={false}
                        style={[
                          styles.divisionSubTag,
                          { color: isWell ? themeColors.purpleText : themeColors.cyanText },
                        ]}
                      >
                        {isWell
                          ? tx('BIENESTAR & SPA', 'WELLNESS & SPA')
                          : tx('FITNESS & DEPORTE', 'FITNESS & SPORTS')}
                      </Text>
                    )}
                  </View>
                  <Feather
                    name={isActive ? 'check-circle' : 'chevron-right'}
                    size={17}
                    color={isActive ? themeColors.cyanText : themeColors.textMuted}
                  />
                </Pressable>
              );
            })}
            {filteredOptions.length === 0 && (
              <Text style={[styles.emptyHint, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                {tx('Sin resultados para tu búsqueda.', 'No matches found for your search.')}
              </Text>
            )}
          </ScrollView>
        </View>
      </KeyboardAvoidingView>
    </ReactNativeModal>
  );
};

export default TerminalOnboardingSelector;

const baseStyles = StyleSheet.create({
  sheetSurface: {
    backgroundColor: '#090D16',
    borderTopWidth: 2,
    borderColor: '#0E4F5C',
    borderTopColor: '#B8FFFF',
    paddingHorizontal: 18,
    elevation: 0,
  },
  sheetHeader: {
    paddingBottom: 10,
    marginBottom: 8,
  },
  sheetTag: {
    color: COLORS.cyan,
    fontSize: 9.5,
    fontWeight: '900',
    letterSpacing: 1.2,
    textAlign: 'center',
    marginBottom: 2,
  },
  sheetTitle: {
    color: COLORS.textPrimary,
    fontSize: 18,
    fontWeight: '900',
    letterSpacing: 0.4,
    textAlign: 'center',
  },
  sheetOption: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    alignItems: 'center',
    backgroundColor: '#0E131F',
    borderRadius: 12,
    paddingHorizontal: 14,

    borderWidth: 1.2,
    borderColor: '#1E293B',

    borderTopColor: '#334155',

    borderBottomColor: '#03060B',

    shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 4 },
    shadowOpacity: 0.28,

    elevation: 0,
  },

    FILE: src/components/TerminalSecondaryModals.styles.ts

    // === src/components/TerminalSecondaryModals.styles.ts === //
import { Platform, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import { COLORS, RADIUS } from '../styles/globalStyles';

const WEB_NO_SEL: any =
  Platform.OS === 'web' ? { userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } : {};

export const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  phantomCard: {
    ...WEB_NO_SEL,
    padding: 14, borderRadius: 14, backgroundColor: '#0E131F', marginBottom: 8,
    borderWidth: 1.2, borderColor: '#1E293B',
    borderTopWidth: 1.8, borderTopColor: '#334155',
    borderBottomWidth: 4.5, borderBottomColor: '#03060B',
    shadowColor: '#000000', shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 4 }, shadowOpacity: 0.28, shadowRadius: 8, elevation: 0,
  },

    backgroundColor: '#1B0E2E', borderColor: COLORS.purple,

  inputLabelModal: {

  },
  inputModal: {

  approveBtnModal: {

    borderWidth: 1.2, borderColor: '#C77DFF', borderTopWidth: 1.8, borderTopColor: '#E0AAFF', borderBottomWidth: 4.8, borderBottomColor: '#4C1D95',
    shadowColor: COLORS.purple, shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 5 }, shadowOpacity: 0.34, shadowRadius: 10, elevation: 0,
  },
  approveBtnTextModal: { color: '#FFFFFF', fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 1.4 },

  cancelBtnModalText: { color: COLORS.textPrimary, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 1.1 },

    FILE: src/components/TerminalSecondaryModals.tsx

    // === src/components/TerminalSecondaryModals.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Keyboard,
  KeyboardAvoidingView,
  Platform,
  Pressable,
  ScrollView,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { CATEGORY_IMAGES } from '../constants/exploreData';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { COLORS, globalStyles } from '../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { KineticListFilterSortBar } from './shared/KineticListFilterSortBar';
import { KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from './shared/KineticModalHeader3D';
import { KineticLoader, SmartImage } from './SharedUI';
import { styles as baseStyles } from './TerminalSecondaryModals.styles';

export { CATEGORY_IMAGES, KineticLoader, SmartImage };
export { ServiceDetailsModal } from './terminal/TerminalServiceDetailsModal';

const NO_SEL =
  Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as any) : null;
const LIGHT_INK_DATA: any =
  Platform.OS === 'web' ? { dataSet: { kineticInk: 'light' } } : {};
const press3D = (pressed: boolean, baseLip = 4.5) =>
  pressed ? { borderBottomWidth: 1.5, transform: [{ translateY: Math.max(1.5, baseLip - 2) }] } : null;

export const PhantomProfileModal = ({
  isVisible,
  onClose,
  phantomList = [],
  selectedPhantom,
  setSelectedPhantom,
  phantomEmail,
  setPhantomEmail,
  isLinking,
  handleVincularSocio,
  triggerHaptic,
}: any) => {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, tx, td } = useKineticPreferences();
  const isLight = isLightMode;
  const styles = useMemo(
    () => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme),
    [resolvedTheme]
  );

  const [keyboardHeight, setKeyboardHeight] = useState(0);
  const [prospectSearch, setProspectSearch] = useState('');
  const [prospectSort, setProspectSort] = useState<'az' | 'za' | 'disc' | 'disc_desc'>('az');
  const lastPhantomRef = useRef<any>(null);
  if (selectedPhantom) lastPhantomRef.current = selectedPhantom;

  useEffect(() => {
    const showEv = Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'keyboardWillShow' : 'keyboardDidShow';
    const hideEv = Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'keyboardWillHide' : 'keyboardDidHide';
    const s1 = Keyboard.addListener(showEv, (e) => {
      setKeyboardHeight(e.endCoordinates?.height || 260);
    });
    const s2 = Keyboard.addListener(hideEv, () => {
      setKeyboardHeight(0);
    });
    return () => {
      s1.remove();
      s2.remove();
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isVisible) {
      setProspectSearch('');
      if (!selectedPhantom) lastPhantomRef.current = null;
    }
  }, [isVisible, selectedPhantom]);

  const displayPhantom = isVisible ? selectedPhantom : (selectedPhantom ?? lastPhantomRef.current);

  const safePhantomList = useMemo(
    () => (Array.isArray(phantomList) ? phantomList : []),
    [phantomList]
  );

  const formatCleanLocation = (ubicacion?: string, disciplina?: string) => {
    const cleanDisc = disciplina ? td(disciplina) : '';
    if (!ubicacion) {
      return cleanDisc
        ? `${cleanDisc} • ${tx('Ubicación Verificada', 'Verified Location')}`
        : tx('Ubicación Verificada', 'Verified Location');
    }
    if (ubicacion.startsWith('http') || ubicacion.includes('api=1&query=')) {
      return cleanDisc
        ? `${cleanDisc} • ${tx('Pin GPS sincronizado en Maps', 'GPS Pin synced in Maps')}`
        : tx('Pin GPS sincronizado en Google Maps', 'GPS Pin synced in Google Maps');
    }
    return cleanDisc ? `${cleanDisc} • ${ubicacion}` : ubicacion;
  };

  const filteredPhantoms = useMemo(() => {
    const q = prospectSearch.trim().toLowerCase();
    const list = safePhantomList.filter((p: any) => {
      if (!q) return true;
      return `${p?.nombre || ''} ${p?.ubicacion || ''} ${p?.disciplina || ''}`
        .toLowerCase()
        .includes(q);
    });
    return [...list].sort((a: any, b: any) => {
      if (prospectSort === 'za') return String(b?.nombre || '').localeCompare(String(a?.nombre || ''));
      if (prospectSort === 'disc') {
        return String(a?.disciplina || '').localeCompare(String(b?.disciplina || ''));
      }
      if (prospectSort === 'disc_desc') {
        return String(b?.disciplina || '').localeCompare(String(a?.disciplina || ''));
      }
      return String(a?.nombre || '').localeCompare(String(b?.nombre || ''));
    });
  }, [safePhantomList, prospectSearch, prospectSort]);

  return (
    <ReactNativeModal
      isVisible={Boolean(isVisible)}
      style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
      backdropColor={isLight ? '#0F172A' : '#050505'}
      backdropOpacity={isLight ? 0.5 : 0.92}
      backdropTransitionOutTiming={0} backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
      hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
      animationInTiming={200}
      animationOutTiming={180}
      onBackdropPress={onClose}
      onBackButtonPress={onClose}
      avoidKeyboard
      useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
      useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
    >
      <KeyboardAvoidingView
        behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
        style={{ width: '100%', flex: 1, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
      >
        <View
          style={[
            globalStyles.bottomSheet,
            KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS,
            {
              backgroundColor: isLight ? '#FFFFFF' : '#0A0D14',
              borderColor: isLight ? '#C4B5FD' : '#6B21A8',
              borderTopWidth: 1.8,
              borderTopColor: isLight ? '#7C3AED' : '#C084FC',
              borderBottomWidth: 4.5,
              borderBottomColor: '#7C3AED',
              elevation: 0,
              paddingBottom:
                keyboardHeight > 0 ? keyboardHeight + 14 : insets.bottom > 0 ? insets.bottom + 12 : 20,
            },
          ]}
        >
          <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={onClose} accentColor={themeColors.purpleText} />

          <Text style={[styles.serviceModalTitle, { color: themeColors.purpleText }]}>
            {tx('VINCULAR PROSPECTO', 'LINK PROSPECT')}
          </Text>
          <Text style={styles.serviceModalSub}>
            {tx(
              'Activa un Perfil Fantasma en la red asignándole un correo oficial de operador.',
              'Activate a Phantom Profile on the network by assigning an official operator email.'
            )}
          </Text>

          <Text style={styles.inputLabelModal}>
            {tx('Prospectos Inactivos', 'Inactive Prospects')} ({safePhantomList.length})
          </Text>

          {safePhantomList.length > 0 && (
            <KineticListFilterSortBar
              searchQuery={prospectSearch}
              onChangeSearch={setProspectSearch}
              searchPlaceholder={tx(
                'Buscar prospecto, ciudad o disciplina...',
                'Search prospect, city or discipline...'
              )}
              sortOptions={[
                { key: 'az', label: 'A–Z' },
                { key: 'za', label: 'Z–A' },
                { key: 'disc', label: tx('Disciplina A–Z', 'Discipline A–Z') },
                { key: 'disc_desc', label: tx('Disciplina Z–A', 'Discipline Z–A') },
              ]}
              activeSort={prospectSort}
              onSelectSort={(k) => setProspectSort(k as any)}
            />
          )}

          <ScrollView
            style={{ flex: 1, marginBottom: 12 }}
            showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
            bounces={false}
            overScrollMode="never"
            nestedScrollEnabled
            keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
            keyboardDismissMode="on-drag"
          >
            {filteredPhantoms.map((p: any) => {
              const isSelected = displayPhantom?.id === p.id;
              return (
                <Pressable
                  key={p.id}
                  style={({ pressed }) => [
                    styles.phantomCard,
                    isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' },
                    isSelected && (isLight
                      ? { backgroundColor: '#F5F3FF', borderColor: '#A855F7', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#7C3AED' }
                      : styles.phantomCardActive),
                    press3D(pressed, 4.5),
                    NO_SEL,
                  ]}
                  onPress={() => { triggerHaptic?.('Light'); setSelectedPhantom(p); }}
                >
                  <Text selectable={false} style={[styles.phantomCardText, isSelected && styles.phantomCardTextActive]} numberOfLines={1}>
                    {p.nombre}
                  </Text>
                  <Text selectable={false} style={{ color: themeColors.textMuted, fontSize: 11, marginTop: 3 }} numberOfLines={1}>
                    {formatCleanLocation(p.ubicacion, p.disciplina)}
                  </Text>
                </Pressable>
              );
            })}
            {filteredPhantoms.length === 0 && (
              <Text style={{ color: themeColors.textMuted, fontSize: 12, textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 20 }}>
                {safePhantomList.length === 0
                  ? tx('No hay prospectos inactivos en el radar.', 'No inactive prospects on the radar.')
                  : tx('Sin coincidencias para tu búsqueda.', 'No prospects match your search.')}
              </Text>
            )}
          </ScrollView>

          {displayPhantom && (
            <>
              <Text style={styles.inputLabelModal}>
                {tx('Correo Oficial del Socio *', 'Partner Official Email *')}
              </Text>
              <TextInput
                style={[
                  styles.inputModal,
                  { fontSize: 16, color: themeColors.textPrimary },
                  isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#C4B5FD', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#7C3AED' },
                ]}
                placeholder={tx('correo@club.com', 'partner@club.com')}
                placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
                value={phantomEmail}
                onChangeText={setPhantomEmail}
                keyboardType="email-address"
                autoCapitalize="none"
              />
              <Pressable
                {...LIGHT_INK_DATA}
                style={({ pressed }) => [
                  styles.approveBtnModal,
                  { marginTop: 14, marginBottom: 4 },
                  isLight && { backgroundColor: '#7C3AED', borderColor: '#6D28D9', borderTopColor: '#A78BFA', borderBottomColor: '#4C1D95' },
                  press3D(pressed, 4.8),
                  NO_SEL,
                ]}
                onPress={() => { Keyboard.dismiss(); handleVincularSocio(); }}
                disabled={isLinking}
              >
                {isLinking ? (
                  <KineticLoader size={18} color="#FFFFFF" />
                ) : (
                  <Text selectable={false} style={[styles.approveBtnTextModal, { color: '#FFFFFF' }]}>
                    {tx('ENCENDER PERFIL', 'ACTIVATE PROFILE')}
                  </Text>
                )}
              </Pressable>
            </>
          )}

          <Pressable
            style={({ pressed }) => [
              styles.cancelBtnModal,
              isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F1F5F9', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' },
              press3D(pressed, 4.5),
              NO_SEL,
            ]}
            onPress={onClose}
          >
            <Feather name="arrow-left" size={14} color={themeColors.textPrimary} style={{ marginRight: 6 }} />
            <Text selectable={false} style={[styles.cancelBtnModalText, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
              {tx('VOLVER AL DASHBOARD', 'BACK TO DASHBOARD')}
            </Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>
      </KeyboardAvoidingView>
    </ReactNativeModal>
  );
};

export const TacticalAlertModal = ({ alertConfig, setAlertConfig, triggerHaptic }: any) => {
  const { isLightMode, themeColors, td, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
  const lastAlertRef = useRef<any>(null);
  const confirmTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  if (alertConfig?.visible && (alertConfig.title || alertConfig.message)) {
    lastAlertRef.current = alertConfig;
  } else if (alertConfig && !lastAlertRef.current) {
    lastAlertRef.current = alertConfig;
  }
  useEffect(() => () => {
    if (confirmTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(confirmTimerRef.current);
  }, []);

  const displayAlert = alertConfig?.visible ? alertConfig : (lastAlertRef.current || alertConfig);
  if (!displayAlert) return null;

  const neonDataSet: any = Platform.OS === 'web' ? { dataSet: { kineticNeon: 'true' } } : {};
  const aType = String(displayAlert.type || 'alert');
  const isErr = aType === 'error' || aType === 'confirm';
  const isWarn = aType === 'warning';
  const isSucc = aType === 'success';
  const neonLip = isErr ? '#FF3B30' : isWarn ? '#FFAB00' : isSucc ? '#00C853' : '#00B8D4';
  const lightSideBorder = isErr ? '#FCA5A5' : isWarn ? '#FCD34D' : isSucc ? '#6EE7B7' : '#67E8F9';
  const titleInk = isErr
    ? isLightMode ? '#DC2626' : COLORS.red
    : isWarn
      ? isLightMode ? '#B45309' : COLORS.yellow
      : isSucc
        ? isLightMode ? '#047857' : COLORS.green
        : themeColors.cyanText;
  const closeAlertModal = () => {
    triggerHaptic?.('Light');
    setAlertConfig((prev: any) => (prev ? { ...prev, visible: false } : { visible: false }));
  };
  return (
    <ReactNativeModal
      isVisible={Boolean(alertConfig?.visible)}
      onBackdropPress={closeAlertModal}
      onBackButtonPress={closeAlertModal}
      animationIn="fadeIn"
      animationOut="fadeOut"
      animationInTiming={200}
      animationOutTiming={180}
      hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
      backdropColor={isLightMode ? '#0F172A' : '#050505'}
      backdropOpacity={isLightMode ? 0.55 : 0.9}
      backdropTransitionOutTiming={0} backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
      useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
      useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
      style={{ margin: 0, padding: 20, justifyContent: 'center' }}
    >
      <View
        style={[
          globalStyles.tacticalAlertBox,
          { borderTopWidth: 1.8, borderBottomWidth: 4.5, elevation: 0 },
          isLightMode
            ? { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: lightSideBorder, borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: neonLip, shadowColor: neonLip, shadowOpacity: 0.28 }
            : {
                backgroundColor: '#090C12',
                borderColor: isErr ? COLORS.red : isWarn ? COLORS.yellow : isSucc ? COLORS.green : COLORS.cyan,
                borderTopColor: isErr ? '#FCA5A5' : isWarn ? '#FDE68A' : isSucc ? '#86EFAC' : '#B8FFFF',
                borderBottomColor: isErr ? '#8B1A14' : isWarn ? '#854D0E' : isSucc ? '#00752E' : '#006B78',
                shadowColor: isErr ? COLORS.red : isWarn ? COLORS.yellow : isSucc ? COLORS.green : COLORS.cyan,
              },
          NO_SEL,
        ]}
      >
        <View style={globalStyles.tacticalAlertHeader}>
          <Feather
            name={aType === 'error' ? 'alert-octagon' : isWarn ? 'alert-triangle' : isSucc ? 'check-circle' : 'activity'}
            size={20}
            color={titleInk}
          />
          <Text style={[globalStyles.tacticalAlertTitle, { color: titleInk }]}>
            {td(displayAlert.title)}
          </Text>
        </View>
        <Text style={[globalStyles.tacticalAlertDesc, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>
          {td(displayAlert.message)}
        </Text>
        {aType === 'confirm' ? (
          <View style={globalStyles.tacticalAlertActions}>
            <Pressable
              {...neonDataSet}
              style={({ pressed }) => [
                globalStyles.tacticalCancelBtn,
                { borderTopWidth: 1.8, borderBottomWidth: 4.5, elevation: 0 },
                isLightMode
                  ? { backgroundColor: '#F1F5F9', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8', shadowColor: '#64748B', shadowOpacity: 0.16 }
                  : { backgroundColor: '#111827', borderColor: '#334155', borderTopColor: '#475569', borderBottomColor: '#020617' },
                press3D(pressed, 4.5),
                NO_SEL,
              ]}
              onPress={closeAlertModal}
            >
              <Text selectable={false} style={[globalStyles.tacticalCancelBtnText, { color: isLightMode ? '#0F172A' : '#E2E8F0' }]}>
                {td(displayAlert.cancelText || tx('CANCELAR', 'CANCEL'))}
              </Text>
            </Pressable>
            <Pressable
              {...neonDataSet}
              style={({ pressed }) => [
                globalStyles.tacticalConfirmBtn,
                { borderTopWidth: 1.8, borderBottomWidth: 4.5, elevation: 0 },
                isLightMode
                  ? { backgroundColor: '#FEF2F2', borderColor: '#F87171', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#FF3B30', shadowColor: '#FF3B30', shadowOpacity: 0.25 }
                  : { backgroundColor: '#260A0D', borderColor: COLORS.red, borderTopColor: '#FCA5A5', borderBottomColor: '#8B1A14' },
                press3D(pressed, 4.5),
                NO_SEL,
              ]}
              onPress={() => {
                const confirmFn = displayAlert.onConfirm;
                setAlertConfig((prev: any) => (prev ? { ...prev, visible: false } : { visible: false }));
                if (confirmFn) confirmFn();
              }}
            >
              <Text selectable={false} style={[globalStyles.tacticalConfirmBtnText, isLightMode && { color: '#DC2626' }]}>
                {td(displayAlert.confirmText || tx('CONFIRMAR', 'CONFIRM'))}
              </Text>
            </Pressable>
          </View>
        ) : (
          <Pressable
            {...neonDataSet}
            style={({ pressed }) => [
              globalStyles.tacticalAlertBtn,
              { borderTopWidth: 1.8, borderBottomWidth: 4.5, elevation: 0 },
              isLightMode
                ? {
                    backgroundColor: isErr ? '#FEF2F2' : isWarn ? '#FEF3C7' : isSucc ? '#ECFDF5' : '#ECFEFF',
                    borderColor: isErr ? '#F87171' : isWarn ? '#F59E0B' : isSucc ? '#10B981' : '#06B6D4',
                    borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                    borderBottomColor: neonLip,
                    shadowColor: neonLip,
                    shadowOpacity: 0.25,
                  }
                : {
                    backgroundColor: aType === 'error' ? '#260A0D' : isWarn ? '#261C05' : isSucc ? '#062415' : '#071E26',
                    borderColor: aType === 'error' ? COLORS.red : isWarn ? COLORS.yellow : isSucc ? COLORS.green : COLORS.cyan,
                    borderTopColor: aType === 'error' ? '#FCA5A5' : isWarn ? '#FDE68A' : isSucc ? '#86EFAC' : '#B8FFFF',
                    borderBottomColor: aType === 'error' ? '#8B1A14' : isWarn ? '#854D0E' : isSucc ? '#00752E' : '#006B78',
                    shadowColor: aType === 'error' ? COLORS.red : isWarn ? COLORS.yellow : isSucc ? COLORS.green : COLORS.cyan,
                  },
              press3D(pressed, 4.5),
              NO_SEL,
            ]}
            onPress={() => {
              const confirmFn = displayAlert.onConfirm;
              closeAlertModal();
              if (confirmFn) {
                if (confirmTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(confirmTimerRef.current);
                confirmTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => confirmFn(), 160);
              }
            }}
          >
            <Text selectable={false} style={[globalStyles.tacticalAlertBtnText, { color: titleInk }]}>
              {td(displayAlert.confirmText || tx('ENTENDIDO', 'GOT IT'))}
            </Text>
          </Pressable>
        )}
      </View>
    </ReactNativeModal>
  );
};

    FILE: src/hooks/terminal/terminalHookHelpers.ts

    // === src/hooks/terminal/terminalHookHelpers.ts === //
import { isPassExpiredWithoutCheckIn } from '../../services/kineticNoShowEngine';
import { isRealSupabaseJwt } from '../../services/kineticSessionPersistence';

export const KINETIC_OFFICIAL_NAME = 'Kinetic Oficial';

const OFFICIAL_ADMIN_EMAILS = new Set([
  'administrador@kinetic.com.mx', 'administrador@kinetic.com', 'kinetic@kinetic.com', 'admin@kinetic.com',
]);

const DELETED_SERVICE_STATES_SET = new Set([
  'eliminado', 'eliminada', 'borrado', 'borrada', 'deleted',
  'cancelado', 'cancelada', 'inactivo', 'inactiva', 'archivado', 'archivada',
]);

export const UUID_REGEX = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-5][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;
const FREQUENCY_REGEX = /\s*\[Frecuencia Programada:\s*(Diario|Semanal|Mensual|Única Vez)\]\s*$/i;

export interface ITerminalSuccessDetails {
  nombre: string;
  monto: number;
  monto_escrow: number;
  costo_total: number;
  isMasterPass: boolean;
  spotNumber?: number | null;
}

export const INITIAL_SUCCESS_DETAILS: ITerminalSuccessDetails = {
  nombre: '', monto: 0, monto_escrow: 0, costo_total: 0, isMasterPass: false, spotNumber: null,
};

export const extractReservaSpotNumber = (reserva: any): number | null => {
  const direct = Number(reserva?.spot_number ?? reserva?.estacion ?? reserva?.spot ?? NaN);
  if (Number.isInteger(direct) && direct >= 1 && direct <= 50) return direct;
  const m = String(reserva?.notas || '').match(/(?:#|spot\s*|estaci[oó]n\s*)(\d+)\b/i);
  const parsed = m ? Number(m[1]) : NaN;
  return Number.isInteger(parsed) && parsed >= 1 && parsed <= 50 ? parsed : null;
};

export const extractFrequencyMeta = (rawDesc?: string) => {
  const text = String(rawDesc || '');
  const match = text.match(FREQUENCY_REGEX);
  return { cleanDescripcion: text.replace(FREQUENCY_REGEX, '').trim(), frecuencia: match ? match[1] : 'Única Vez' };
};

export const injectFrequencyMeta = (cleanDesc: string, frecuencia?: string) => {
  const base = String(cleanDesc || '').replace(FREQUENCY_REGEX, '').trim();
  return !frecuencia || frecuencia === 'Única Vez' ? base : `${base}\n\n[Frecuencia Programada: ${frecuencia}]`;
};

export const isKineticAdminView = (user: any, profile: any, accessToken?: string | null): boolean => {
  if (!user?.id || !isRealSupabaseJwt(accessToken)) return false;
  const email = String(user?.email || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  const rol = String(profile?.rol || profile?.tipo_cuenta || user?.app_metadata?.rol || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  return OFFICIAL_ADMIN_EMAILS.has(email) || rol === 'admin' || rol === 'fundador';
};

export const isLegacyGlobalTerminalName = (name?: string | null): boolean =>
  String(name || '').trim().toLowerCase() === 'terminal global kinetic';

export const buildOfficialKineticBusiness = (fallbackAvatarUrl?: string | null) => ({
  id: 'admin-global-terminal', nombre: KINETIC_OFFICIAL_NAME, categoria: 'Club',
  disciplina: 'Multidisciplina', ubicacion: '', estado: 'activo',
  estrellas_exclusividad: 5, avatar_url: fallbackAvatarUrl || null, isOfficialKinetic: true,
});

export const sanitizeTerminalBusinessData = (biz: any, isAdminView: boolean, fallbackAvatarUrl?: string | null): any => {
  if (!biz || typeof biz !== 'object') return biz ?? null;
  const normName = String(biz?.nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (normName === 'terminal global kinetic' || normName === 'kinetic oficial') {
    return {
      ...biz, nombre: KINETIC_OFFICIAL_NAME, estrellas_exclusividad: biz.estrellas_exclusividad ?? 5,
      avatar_url: biz.avatar_url || fallbackAvatarUrl || null, isOfficialKinetic: true,
    };
  }
  if (isAdminView && biz.id === 'admin-global-terminal' && !normName) {
    return buildOfficialKineticBusiness(biz.avatar_url || fallbackAvatarUrl);
  }
  return biz;
};

export const resolvePassFinancials = (reserva: any) => {
  if (reserva?.costo_total != null && Number(reserva.costo_total) === 0) {
    return { costoTotal: 0, montoEscrow: 0, montoPuerta: 0, isMasterPass: true };
  }
  const rawTotal = reserva?.costo_total != null ? Math.max(0, Number(reserva.costo_total) || 0) : Math.max(0, Number(reserva?.precio_kc) || 0);
  const montoPuerta = reserva?.monto_puerta != null ? Math.max(0, Number(reserva.monto_puerta) || 0) : rawTotal === 0 ? 0 : Math.round(rawTotal * 0.6);
  const montoEscrow = reserva?.monto_escrow != null ? Math.max(0, Number(reserva.monto_escrow) || 0) : rawTotal === 0 || montoPuerta === 0 ? 0 : Math.max(0, rawTotal - montoPuerta);
  const costoTotal = montoPuerta === 0 && montoEscrow === 0 ? 0 : rawTotal > 0 ? rawTotal : montoEscrow + montoPuerta;
  return { costoTotal, montoEscrow, montoPuerta, isMasterPass: montoPuerta === 0 && montoEscrow === 0 };
};

export const filterValidServices = (
  servicios: any[],
  isGlobal: boolean,
  businessName?: string | null
): any[] => {
  if (!Array.isArray(servicios)) return [];
  const normBiz = String(businessName || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!isGlobal && !normBiz) return [];
  return servicios.filter((s) => {
    if (!s) return false;
    const st = String(s?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    if (DELETED_SERVICE_STATES_SET.has(st)) return false;
    if (!isGlobal && String(s?.negocio_nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase() !== normBiz) return false;
    return true;
  });
};

export const filterValidActivePasses = (
  passes: any[],
  servicios: any[],
  isGlobal: boolean,
  businessName?: string | null
): any[] => {
  if (!Array.isArray(passes) || passes.length === 0) return [];
  const cleanServs = filterValidServices(servicios, isGlobal, businessName);
  const servById = new Map<string, any>();
  const servByName = new Map<string, any>();
  cleanServs.forEach((s) => {
    const idStr = String(s?.id || '').trim();
    if (idStr) servById.set(idStr, s);
    [s?.nombre_servicio, s?.titulo, s?.nombre].forEach((n) => {
      const k = String(n || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      if (k && !servByName.has(k)) servByName.set(k, s);
    });
  });
  const normBiz = String(businessName || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!isGlobal && !normBiz) return [];

  const now = new Date();
  const hoyYmd = `${now.getFullYear()}-${String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  const nowMins = now.getHours() * 60 + now.getMinutes();

  const extractYmd = (raw: unknown): string => {
    const m = String(raw || '').trim().match(/^(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})/);
    return m ? m[1] : '';
  };
  const parseMins = (raw: unknown): number => {
    const m = String(raw || '08:00').trim().match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})\s*(am|pm)?/i);
    if (!m) return 8 * 60;
    let hh = parseInt(m[1], 10);
    const mm = parseInt(m[2], 10);
    const mer = (m[3] || '').toLowerCase();
    if (mer === 'pm' && hh < 12) hh += 12;
    if (mer === 'am' && hh === 12) hh = 0;
    return (Number.isFinite(hh) ? hh : 8) * 60 + (Number.isFinite(mm) ? mm : 0);
  };
  const isRecurring = (srv: any): boolean => {
    const rawDesc = String(srv?.descripcion || '');
    const freqMatch = rawDesc.match(/\[Frecuencia Programada:\s*(.*?)\]/i);
    const freq = String(srv?.frecuencia || freqMatch?.[1] || 'Única Vez').toLowerCase();
    return /diario|semanal|mensual|daily|weekly|monthly/.test(freq);
  };

  return passes.filter((r) => {
    if (!r) return false;
    const st = String(r?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    if (st !== 'activa' && st !== 'pendiente' && st !== 'confirmada') return false;
    if (!isGlobal && String(r?.negocio_nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase() !== normBiz) return false;
    const sid = String(r?.servicio_id || '').trim();
    const rName = [r?.nombre_servicio, r?.servicio, r?.titulo_clase]
      .map((v) => String(v || '').trim().toLowerCase())
      .find(Boolean);
    const matchedSrv = (sid ? servById.get(sid) : undefined) || (rName ? servByName.get(rName) : undefined);
    if (!matchedSrv) return false;

    if (isPassExpiredWithoutCheckIn(r, matchedSrv)) return false;

    if (!isRecurring(matchedSrv)) {
      const fechaYmd = extractYmd(r?.fecha_clase) || extractYmd(matchedSrv?.fecha) || extractYmd(r?.fecha_hora);
      if (fechaYmd) {
        if (fechaYmd < hoyYmd) return false;
        if (fechaYmd === hoyYmd) {
          const startMins = parseMins(r?.hora_inicio || r?.hora || matchedSrv?.hora_inicio || '08:00');
          const durMins = Math.max(180, Number(matchedSrv?.duracion_minutos ?? matchedSrv?.duracion_min) || 60);
          if (nowMins > startMins + durMins) return false;
        }
      }
    }
    return true;
  });
};

export const sanitizeTerminalSnapshot = (
  snap: any,
  isAdminView: boolean,
  fallbackAvatarUrl?: string | null
): any => {
  if (!snap || typeof snap !== 'object') return null;
  const cleanBiz = sanitizeTerminalBusinessData(snap.businessData, isAdminView, fallbackAvatarUrl);
  const bizName = cleanBiz?.nombre || '';
  const cleanServs = filterValidServices(snap.servicios || [], isAdminView, bizName);
  const cleanReservas = filterValidActivePasses(snap.reservas || [], cleanServs, isAdminView, bizName);
  const rawHist = Array.isArray(snap.metricas?.historialCompleto) ? snap.metricas.historialCompleto : [];
  const normBiz = String(bizName).trim().toLowerCase();
  const cleanHist = isAdminView
    ? rawHist
    : normBiz
      ? rawHist.filter((r: any) => String(r?.negocio_nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase() === normBiz)
      : [];
  return {
    ...snap,
    businessData: cleanBiz,
    servicios: cleanServs,
    reservas: cleanReservas,
    metricas: {
      ...(snap.metricas || {}),
      historialCompleto: cleanHist,
      hasBusiness: Boolean(snap.metricas?.hasBusiness ?? cleanBiz),
    },
  };
};

export const matchPassByCode = (passes: any[], rawCode: string): any | undefined => {
  const clean = String(rawCode || '').replace(/^#/, '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!clean || !Array.isArray(passes)) return undefined;
  return passes.find((r) => {
    const idLow = String(r?.id || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const tokLow = String(r?.qr_token || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const folioLow = String(r?.folio || idLow.slice(0, 8)).replace(/^#/, '').trim().toLowerCase();
    return (
      idLow === clean ||
      (tokLow !== '' && tokLow === clean) ||
      (clean.length >= 6 && (folioLow === clean || idLow.slice(0, 8) === clean))
    );
  });
};

export const partitionOperacionEstadioResult = (res: any, isGlobal: boolean, targetName: string) => {
  const rawReservas = Array.isArray(res?.reservas) ? res.reservas.filter(Boolean) : [];
  const validServicios = filterValidServices(Array.isArray(res?.servicios) ? res.servicios : [], isGlobal, targetName);
  const rawPerfiles = Array.isArray(res?.perfiles) ? res.perfiles.filter(Boolean) : [];

  const perfMap = new Map<string, any>();
  for (const p of rawPerfiles) if (p?.id) perfMap.set(p.id, p);

  const servById = new Map<string, any>();
  const servByName = new Map<string, any>();
  for (const s of validServicios) {
    const sid = String(s?.id || '').trim();
    if (sid) servById.set(sid, s);
    [s?.nombre_servicio, s?.titulo, s?.nombre].forEach((n) => {
      const norm = String(n || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      if (norm && !servByName.has(norm)) servByName.set(norm, s);
    });
  }

  const normTarget = targetName.toLowerCase();
  const enrichedReservas = rawReservas
    .filter((r: any) => isGlobal || String(r?.negocio_nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase() === normTarget)
    .map((r: any) => {
      const sid = String(r?.servicio_id || '').trim();
      const rName = [r?.nombre_servicio, r?.servicio, r?.titulo_clase]
        .map((v) => String(v || '').trim().toLowerCase())
        .find(Boolean);
      const matchedSrv = (sid ? servById.get(sid) : undefined) || (rName ? servByName.get(rName) : undefined);
      const srvDateMatch = String(matchedSrv?.fecha || '').trim().match(/^(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})/);
      const resDateMatch = String(r?.fecha_clase || r?.fecha_hora || '').trim().match(/^(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})/);
      const resolvedFechaClase = resDateMatch?.[1] || srvDateMatch?.[1] || r?.fecha_clase || '';
      const resolvedHora = String(r?.hora_inicio || r?.hora || matchedSrv?.hora_inicio || '').trim();
      return {
        ...r,
        perfiles: r?.perfiles || perfMap.get(r?.usuario_id) || null,
        fecha_clase: resolvedFechaClase || r?.fecha_clase || null,
        hora_inicio: resolvedHora || r?.hora_inicio || null,
        duracion_minutos: matchedSrv?.duracion_minutos ?? matchedSrv?.duracion_min ?? r?.duracion_minutos,
      };
    });

  const candidateActivos: any[] = [];
  const historial: any[] = [];
  let sumaHoy = 0;

  for (const r of enrichedReservas) {
    const estado = String(r?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    if (estado === 'activa' || estado === 'pendiente' || estado === 'confirmada') {
      const sid = String(r?.servicio_id || '').trim();
      const rName = [r?.nombre_servicio, r?.servicio, r?.titulo_clase]
        .map((v) => String(v || '').trim().toLowerCase())
        .find(Boolean);
      const matchedSrv = (sid ? servById.get(sid) : undefined) || (rName ? servByName.get(rName) : undefined);
      if (isPassExpiredWithoutCheckIn(r, matchedSrv)) {
        historial.push({ ...r, estado: 'no_show', penalizacion_aplicada: true });
      } else {
        candidateActivos.push(r);
      }
    } else if (
      estado === 'completada' ||
      estado === 'cancelada' ||
      estado === 'no_show' ||
      estado === 'expirada' ||
      estado === 'penalizada'
    ) {
      historial.push(r);
      if (estado === 'completada') sumaHoy += resolvePassFinancials(r).montoPuerta;
    }
  }
  const activos = filterValidActivePasses(candidateActivos, validServicios, isGlobal, targetName);
  return { validServicios, activos, historial, sumaHoy };
};

export const assertValidScanReserva = (
  reserva: any,
  settledSet: Set<string>,
  isAdminView: boolean,
  businessName: string | undefined,
  trFn: (es: string, en: string) => string
) => {
  if (!reserva) {
    throw new Error(trFn('No se encontró ninguna reserva asociada a esta llave QR.', 'No booking found associated with this QR key.'));
  }
  const estadoActual = String(reserva?.estado || '').toLowerCase();
  if (estadoActual === 'completada' || settledSet.has(String(reserva?.id || '').toLowerCase())) {
    throw new Error(trFn('Este pase ya fue escaneado y liquidado anteriormente.', 'This pass was already scanned and settled previously.'));
  }
  if (estadoActual === 'cancelada') {
    throw new Error(trFn('Esta reserva fue cancelada y ya no es válida.', 'This booking was canceled and is no longer valid.'));
  }
  if (
    !isAdminView &&
    String(reserva?.negocio_nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase() !== String(businessName || '').trim().toLowerCase()
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      trFn(
        `Este pase pertenece a "${reserva.negocio_nombre}", no a "${businessName}".`,
        `This pass belongs to "${reserva.negocio_nombre}", not "${businessName}".`
      )
    );
  }
};

    FILE: src/hooks/useTerminal.ts

const TERMINAL_EGRESS_TTL_MS = 120_000;

export function useTerminal(user: any, profile: any, session?: any) {
  const isScreenMounted = useRef(true);
  const [permission, requestPermission] = useCameraPermissions();
  const accessToken = session?.access_token as string | undefined;
