  const isAdminView = useMemo(
    () => isKineticAdminView(user, profile, accessToken),
    [user?.id, user?.email, user?.app_metadata?.rol, accessToken, profile?.rol, profile?.tipo_cuenta]
  );

  const rawCached =
    (terminalMemoryCache && user?.id && terminalMemoryCache.userId === user.id ? terminalMemoryCache : null) ||
    terminalOfflineService.readTerminalSwrCacheSync(user?.id);
  const cached = rawCached ? sanitizeTerminalSnapshot(rawCached, isAdminView, profile?.avatar_url) : null;
  if (cached) terminalMemoryCache = cached;
  const recognizedRole = resolveExplicitProfileRole(profile as Record<string, unknown> | null | undefined, String(user?.email || '').toLowerCase().trim());
  const hasRecognizedB2BRole = Boolean(
    hasValidJwt && (isAdminView || profile?.es_afiliado === true || isB2BRoleString(recognizedRole) || recognizedRole === 'admin' || recognizedRole === 'fundador')
  );
  const optimisticBiz = !cached && hasRecognizedB2BRole
    ? isAdminView
      ? buildOfficialKineticBusiness(profile?.avatar_url)
      : sanitizeTerminalBusinessData(
          { nombre: profile?.nombre || 'Sede Kinetic', avatar_url: profile?.avatar_url },
          false,
          profile?.avatar_url
        )
    : null;
  const instantReady = Boolean(cached);

  const lastValidDataRef = useRef(cached);
  const hasHydratedSnapshotRef = useRef<boolean>(Boolean(cached));
  const hasLoadedInitialRef = useRef<boolean>(instantReady);
  const [hasInitialLoaded, setHasInitialLoaded] = useState<boolean>(instantReady);
  const [isVerifying, setIsVerifying] = useState<boolean>(!instantReady && Boolean(user?.id));
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);

  const [hasBusiness, setHasBusiness] = useState<boolean>(
    Boolean(cached ? (cached?.metricas?.hasBusiness ?? cached?.businessData) : optimisticBiz)
  );
  const [businessData, setBusinessDataState] = useState<any | null>(cached?.businessData ?? optimisticBiz ?? null);
  const [pasesActivos, setPasesActivos] = useState<any[]>(cached?.reservas ?? []);
  const [historialCompleto, setHistorialCompleto] = useState<any[]>(cached?.metricas?.historialCompleto ?? []);
  const [misServicios, setMisServicios] = useState<any[]>(cached?.servicios ?? []);
  const [ingresosHoy, setIngresosHoy] = useState<number>(cached?.metricas?.ingresosHoy ?? 0);

  const [showScanner, setShowScanner] = useState(false);
  const [scanned, setScanned] = useState(false);
  const [isProcessing, setIsProcessing] = useState(false);
  const [showSuccessModal, setShowSuccessModal] = useState(false);
  const [successDetails, setSuccessDetails] = useState<ITerminalSuccessDetails>(INITIAL_SUCCESS_DETAILS);
  const [phantomList, setPhantomList] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [pendingOfflineCount, setPendingOfflineCount] = useState<number>(0);

  const isScanningLockRef = useRef(false);
  const recentlySettledIdsRef = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const isFetchingOpRef = useRef(false);
  const pendingOpRefreshRef = useRef<{ nombre?: string | null; isGlobal: boolean } | null>(null);
  const isCheckingBizRef = useRef(false);
  const pendingForceBizRef = useRef(false);
  const lastCheckedAdminRef = useRef<boolean | null>(null);
  const serverOffsetMsRef = useRef(terminalOfflineService.getLocalServerClockOffsetMs());
  const burstResetTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  const scanBridgeTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  const businessNameRef = useRef<string | undefined>(businessData?.nombre); businessNameRef.current = businessData?.nombre;
  const businessDataRef = useRef<any | null>(businessData); businessDataRef.current = businessData;
  const profileRef = useRef<any>(profile); profileRef.current = profile;
  const userRef = useRef<any>(user); userRef.current = user;
  const isAdminViewRef = useRef<boolean>(isAdminView); isAdminViewRef.current = isAdminView;
  const misServiciosRef = useRef<any[]>(misServicios); misServiciosRef.current = misServicios;
  const pasesActivosRef = useRef<any[]>(pasesActivos); pasesActivosRef.current = pasesActivos;
  const historialRef = useRef<any[]>(historialCompleto); historialRef.current = historialCompleto;
  const ingresosRef = useRef<number>(ingresosHoy); ingresosRef.current = ingresosHoy;

  const setBusinessData = useCallback((nextBizOrUpdater: any) => {
    setBusinessDataState((prev: any) => {
      const nextVal = typeof nextBizOrUpdater === 'function' ? nextBizOrUpdater(prev) : nextBizOrUpdater;
      const sanitized = sanitizeTerminalBusinessData(nextVal, isAdminViewRef.current, profileRef.current?.avatar_url);
      businessDataRef.current = sanitized; businessNameRef.current = sanitized?.nombre;
      return sanitized;
    });
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    isScreenMounted.current = true;
    return () => {
      isScreenMounted.current = false;
      if (burstResetTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(burstResetTimerRef.current);
      if (scanBridgeTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(scanBridgeTimerRef.current);
    };
  }, []);

  const hasBusinessRef = useRef<boolean>(hasBusiness);
  hasBusinessRef.current = hasBusiness;
  const prevUserIdRef = useRef<string | undefined>(user?.id);
  useEffect(() => {
    const switchedToDifferentUser = Boolean(prevUserIdRef.current && user?.id && prevUserIdRef.current !== user.id);
    if (switchedToDifferentUser || !user?.id) recentlySettledIdsRef.current.clear();
    if (!user?.id) terminalMemoryCache = null;
    prevUserIdRef.current = user?.id;
    const rawHit = (terminalMemoryCache?.userId === user?.id && user?.id ? terminalMemoryCache : null) || terminalOfflineService.readTerminalSwrCacheSync(user?.id);
    const applySnapshot = (rawSnap: any) => {
      const snap = sanitizeTerminalSnapshot(rawSnap, isAdminViewRef.current, profileRef.current?.avatar_url);
      if (!snap) return;
      terminalMemoryCache = snap; lastValidDataRef.current = snap;
      hasHydratedSnapshotRef.current = true;
      hasLoadedInitialRef.current = true; setHasInitialLoaded(true);
      setBusinessDataState(snap.businessData ?? null); setHasBusiness(Boolean(snap.metricas?.hasBusiness ?? snap.businessData));
      setMisServicios(snap.servicios ?? []); setPasesActivos(snap.reservas ?? []);
      setHistorialCompleto(snap.metricas?.historialCompleto ?? []); setIngresosHoy(snap.metricas?.ingresosHoy ?? 0); setIsVerifying(false);
    };
    if (rawHit) { applySnapshot(rawHit); return; }
    if (switchedToDifferentUser || !user?.id) {
      hasHydratedSnapshotRef.current = false;
      hasLoadedInitialRef.current = false; setHasInitialLoaded(false); setBusinessDataState(null); setHasBusiness(false);
      setMisServicios([]); setPasesActivos([]); setHistorialCompleto([]); setIngresosHoy(0); setIsVerifying(Boolean(user?.id));
    } else if (!hasLoadedInitialRef.current) {
      setIsVerifying(true);
    }
    if (user?.id) {
      terminalOfflineService.hydrateTerminalSwrCache(user.id).then((diskHit) => {
        if (diskHit && isScreenMounted.current && !hasHydratedSnapshotRef.current) applySnapshot(diskHit);
      }).catch(() => {});
    }
  }, [user?.id, isAdminView]);

  const syncOfflineQueue = useCallback(async (onSuccess?: (count: number) => void) => {
    const flushed = await terminalOfflineService.flushOfflineCheckInsQueue(onSuccess);
    const remaining = await terminalOfflineService.getPendingOfflineQueueCount();
    if (isScreenMounted.current) setPendingOfflineCount(remaining);
    return flushed;
  }, []);

  const syncServerClock = useCallback(async () => {
    serverOffsetMsRef.current = await terminalOfflineService.hydrateServerClockOffsetMs();
    try {
      const t0 = Date.now();
      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('obtener_tiempo_servidor_ms');
      if (!error && typeof data === 'number') {
        serverOffsetMsRef.current = data + Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 2) - Date.now();
        terminalOfflineService.persistServerClockOffsetMs(serverOffsetMsRef.current);
      }
    } catch {}
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (showScanner) { syncServerClock(); return; }
    isScanningLockRef.current = false; setScanned(false); setIsProcessing(false);
    if (burstResetTimerRef.current) { clearTimeout(burstResetTimerRef.current); burstResetTimerRef.current = null; }
  }, [showScanner, syncServerClock]);

  const fetchOperacionEstadio = useCallback(async (nombreEstadioParam?: string | null, arg2?: boolean, arg3?: boolean) => {
    const isGlobal = Boolean(isAdminViewRef.current || (arg3 !== undefined ? arg3 : arg2));
    const rawTarget = String(nombreEstadioParam ?? businessNameRef.current ?? '').trim();
    const targetName = isGlobal ? (isLegacyGlobalTerminalName(rawTarget) || !rawTarget ? KINETIC_OFFICIAL_NAME : rawTarget) : rawTarget;
    if (!isGlobal && !targetName) {
      if (isScreenMounted.current) { setMisServicios([]); setPasesActivos([]); setHistorialCompleto([]); setIngresosHoy(0); }
      return;
    }
    if (isFetchingOpRef.current) {
      pendingOpRefreshRef.current = { nombre: nombreEstadioParam, isGlobal };
      return;
    }
    isFetchingOpRef.current = true;
    void reconcileExpiredNoShowPassesSilent();
    try {
      const bizStatusLower = String(businessDataRef.current?.estado || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const hasActiveBusiness = Boolean(businessDataRef.current?.id && bizStatusLower === 'activo');
      const res = await terminalService.fetchOperacionEstadio(targetName, isGlobal, hasActiveBusiness);
      const { validServicios, activos: rawActivos, historial, sumaHoy } = partitionOperacionEstadioResult(res, isGlobal, targetName);
      const pendingOfflineIds = await terminalOfflineService.getPendingOfflineQueueIds();
      const activos = rawActivos.filter((p: any) => {
        const pid = String(p?.id || '').toLowerCase();
        const ptok = String(p?.qr_token || p?.qr_code_id || '').toLowerCase();
        return !pendingOfflineIds.has(pid) && !(ptok && pendingOfflineIds.has(ptok))
          && !recentlySettledIdsRef.current.has(pid) && !(ptok && recentlySettledIdsRef.current.has(ptok));
      });
      await terminalOfflineService.cacheActivePassesLocally(activos, userRef.current?.id);
      if (userRef.current?.id) {
        const cleanBiz = sanitizeTerminalBusinessData(businessDataRef.current, isAdminViewRef.current, profileRef.current?.avatar_url);
        terminalMemoryCache = {
          userId: userRef.current.id, fetchedAt: Date.now(), businessData: cleanBiz, servicios: validServicios, reservas: activos,
          metricas: { ingresosHoy: sumaHoy, historialCompleto: historial, hasBusiness: Boolean(cleanBiz) },
        };
        lastValidDataRef.current = terminalMemoryCache;
        hasHydratedSnapshotRef.current = true;
        terminalOfflineService.persistTerminalSwrCache(terminalMemoryCache);
      }
      if (!isScreenMounted.current) return;
      setMisServicios(validServicios); setPasesActivos(activos); setHistorialCompleto(historial); setIngresosHoy(sumaHoy);
    } catch {
      const cachedPasses = await terminalOfflineService.getCachedActivePassesLocally(userRef.current?.id);
      const normTarget = targetName.toLowerCase();
      const scopedOffline = misServiciosRef.current.length > 0
        ? filterValidActivePasses(cachedPasses, misServiciosRef.current, isGlobal, targetName)
        : cachedPasses.filter((p) => isGlobal || String(p?.negocio_nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase() === normTarget);
      if (scopedOffline.length > 0 && pasesActivosRef.current.length === 0 && isScreenMounted.current) setPasesActivos(scopedOffline);
    } finally {
      isFetchingOpRef.current = false;
      const queued = pendingOpRefreshRef.current;
      pendingOpRefreshRef.current = null;
      if (queued && isScreenMounted.current) {
        void fetchOperacionEstadio(queued.nombre, true, queued.isGlobal);
      } else if (isScreenMounted.current) {
        setRefreshing(false);
      }
    }
  }, []);

  const loadPhantomProfiles = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const ghosts = await terminalService.fetchPhantomProfiles();
      if (isScreenMounted.current) setPhantomList(Array.isArray(ghosts) ? ghosts : []);
    } catch {}
  }, []);

  const checkBusinessStatus = useCallback(
    async (_isMountedParam = true, silent = false, force = false) => {
      if (!user?.id) { setIsVerifying(false); return; }
      if (force && terminalMemoryCache) terminalMemoryCache.fetchedAt = 0;
      const hasHit = (terminalMemoryCache?.userId === user.id || lastValidDataRef.current?.userId === user.id);
      if (!force && hasHit && Date.now() - (terminalMemoryCache?.fetchedAt || 0) < TERMINAL_EGRESS_TTL_MS) {
        hasLoadedInitialRef.current = true;
        setHasInitialLoaded((p) => (p ? p : true));
        setIsVerifying((p) => (p ? false : p));
        setRefreshing((p) => (p ? false : p));
        return;
      }
      if (isCheckingBizRef.current && lastCheckedAdminRef.current === isAdminView) {
        if (force) pendingForceBizRef.current = true;
        return;
      }
      isCheckingBizRef.current = true; lastCheckedAdminRef.current = isAdminView;
      if (!silent && !hasLoadedInitialRef.current && !hasHit) setIsVerifying(true);
      try {
        void syncServerClock();
        const email = String(user?.email || '').toLowerCase().trim();
        const defaultCategory = email.startsWith('especialista@') ? 'Especialista' : 'Club';
        const profileName = profileRef.current?.nombre || tr('Sede Kinetic', 'Kinetic Venue');
        const roleHint = resolveExplicitProfileRole(profileRef.current as Record<string, unknown> | null | undefined, email);
        const rawBiz = await terminalService.fetchBusinessStatus(user.id, email, profileName, defaultCategory, roleHint);
        const negocios = Array.isArray(rawBiz) ? rawBiz.filter(Boolean) : [];

        if (negocios.length > 0) {
          const activeBiz = sanitizeTerminalBusinessData(negocios[0], isAdminView, profileRef.current?.avatar_url);
          businessDataRef.current = activeBiz; businessNameRef.current = activeBiz?.nombre;
          if (isScreenMounted.current) { setBusinessDataState(activeBiz); setHasBusiness(true); }
          await fetchOperacionEstadio(activeBiz.nombre, true, isAdminView);
        } else if (isAdminView) {
          const adminBiz = buildOfficialKineticBusiness(profileRef.current?.avatar_url);
          businessDataRef.current = adminBiz; businessNameRef.current = adminBiz.nombre;
          if (isScreenMounted.current) { setBusinessDataState(adminBiz); setHasBusiness(true); }
          await fetchOperacionEstadio(adminBiz.nombre, true, true);
        } else {
          businessDataRef.current = null; businessNameRef.current = undefined;
          terminalMemoryCache = { userId: user.id, fetchedAt: Date.now(), businessData: null, servicios: [], reservas: [], metricas: { ingresosHoy: 0, historialCompleto: [], hasBusiness: false } };
          lastValidDataRef.current = terminalMemoryCache;
          hasHydratedSnapshotRef.current = true;
          terminalOfflineService.persistTerminalSwrCache(terminalMemoryCache);
          if (isScreenMounted.current) {
            setBusinessDataState(null); setHasBusiness(false);
            setMisServicios([]); setPasesActivos([]); setHistorialCompleto([]); setIngresosHoy(0);
          }
          await loadPhantomProfiles();
        }
      } catch {} finally {
        isCheckingBizRef.current = false; hasLoadedInitialRef.current = true;
        const needsFollowUp = pendingForceBizRef.current;
        pendingForceBizRef.current = false;
        if (needsFollowUp && isScreenMounted.current && (hasBusinessRef.current || isAdminViewRef.current || businessNameRef.current)) {
          void fetchOperacionEstadio(businessNameRef.current || KINETIC_OFFICIAL_NAME, true, isAdminViewRef.current);
        }
        if (isScreenMounted.current) {
          setHasInitialLoaded((p) => (p ? p : true));
          setIsVerifying((p) => (p ? false : p));
          setRefreshing((p) => (p ? false : p));
        }
      }
    },
    [user?.id, user?.email, isAdminView, syncServerClock, fetchOperacionEstadio, loadPhantomProfiles]
  );

  const onRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    try {
      await syncOfflineQueue();
      if (hasBusiness && businessNameRef.current) await fetchOperacionEstadio(businessNameRef.current, true, isAdminViewRef.current);
      else await checkBusinessStatus(true, true, true);
    } catch {} finally { if (isScreenMounted.current) setRefreshing(false); }
  }, [hasBusiness, syncOfflineQueue, fetchOperacionEstadio, checkBusinessStatus]);

  useEffect(() => {
    terminalOfflineService.getPendingOfflineQueueCount().then((c) => { if (isScreenMounted.current) setPendingOfflineCount(c); });
    syncOfflineQueue();
    checkBusinessStatus(true, hasLoadedInitialRef.current, false);
    const debouncedSync = createDebouncedEventRunner(() => {
      if (terminalMemoryCache) terminalMemoryCache.fetchedAt = 0;
      syncOfflineQueue(); checkBusinessStatus(true, true, true);
    }, 150);
    const unsubRadar = subscribeKineticRadarUpdated(debouncedSync.trigger);
    const unsubRoot = subscribeKineticRootRefresh(debouncedSync.trigger);
    const hasWin = typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.addEventListener === 'function';
    if (hasWin) { window.addEventListener('kinetic:boveda-updated', debouncedSync.trigger); window.addEventListener('online', debouncedSync.trigger); }
    const appStateSub = Platform.OS !== 'web' ? AppState.addEventListener('change', (st) => { if (st === 'active') debouncedSync.trigger(); }) : null;
    return () => {
      debouncedSync.cancel(); unsubRadar(); unsubRoot(); appStateSub?.remove();
      if (hasWin) { window.removeEventListener('kinetic:boveda-updated', debouncedSync.trigger); window.removeEventListener('online', debouncedSync.trigger); }
    };
  }, [checkBusinessStatus, syncOfflineQueue]);

  const processScannedData = useCallback(
    async (
      data: string,
      triggerHaptic?: (type: string) => void,
      showTacticalAlert?: (title: string, msg: string, type?: string) => void,
      triggerLightningAnimation?: () => void
    ) => {
      if (!data || isScanningLockRef.current || scanned || isProcessing) return;
      isScanningLockRef.current = true; setScanned(true);
      const resetScanLock = (ms = 1800) => {
        if (burstResetTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(burstResetTimerRef.current);
        burstResetTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
          isScanningLockRef.current = false;
          if (isScreenMounted.current) { setShowSuccessModal(false); setScanned(false); }
        }, ms);
      };
      const showSuccessSafe = () => {
        setShowScanner(false); setIsProcessing(false);
        if (scanBridgeTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(scanBridgeTimerRef.current);
        scanBridgeTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => { if (isScreenMounted.current) setShowSuccessModal(true); }, 220);
        resetScanLock(2100);
      };
      const showAlertSafe = (title: string, msg: string) => {
        setShowScanner(false); setIsProcessing(false);
        if (scanBridgeTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(scanBridgeTimerRef.current);
        scanBridgeTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => { if (isScreenMounted.current) showTacticalAlert?.(title, msg, 'error'); }, 220);
      };
      try {
        if (!isAdminViewRef.current && !String(businessNameRef.current || '').trim()) {
          throw new Error(tr('Activa primero tu establecimiento B2B para validar pases.', 'Activate your B2B venue first to validate passes.'));
        }
        const nowSynced = Date.now() + serverOffsetMsRef.current;
        const parsedPacket = parseTerminalQrPacket(data, nowSynced);
        const qrId = parseAndValidateTerminalQr(data, nowSynced, tr);
        if (parsedPacket.isExpired || !parsedPacket.ok) throw new Error(tr('Formato de llave QR inválido o ajeno a Kinetic.', 'Invalid or non-Kinetic QR key format.'));
        if (!isAdminViewRef.current && (parsedPacket.format === 'manual' || parsedPacket.totpStep == null)) {
          throw new Error(tr('Escaneo rechazado: se requiere QR dinámico TOTP de 60s.', 'Scan rejected: dynamic 60s TOTP QR is required.'));
        }
        const settledHist = historialRef.current.filter((h) => String(h?.estado || '').toLowerCase() === 'completada');
        if (recentlySettledIdsRef.current.has(qrId.toLowerCase()) || Boolean(matchPassByCode(settledHist, qrId))) {
          throw new Error(tr('Este pase ya fue escaneado y liquidado anteriormente.', 'This pass was already scanned and settled previously.'));
        }

        let reserva = matchPassByCode(pasesActivosRef.current, qrId);
        if (!reserva) {
          setIsProcessing(true); triggerHaptic?.('Light');
          try {
            reserva = await terminalService.findReservaByQR(qrId, UUID_REGEX.test(qrId), isAdminViewRef.current ? null : businessNameRef.current);
          } catch (netErr: any) {
            if (terminalOfflineService.isNetworkOfflineError(netErr)) {
              const offRes = await terminalOfflineService.processOfflineCheckIn(data, pasesActivosRef.current, {
                isAdminView: isAdminViewRef.current, businessName: businessNameRef.current, nowSyncedMs: nowSynced,
              });
              if (offRes) {
                if (offRes.completedPass?.id) recentlySettledIdsRef.current.add(String(offRes.completedPass.id).toLowerCase());
                if (qrId) recentlySettledIdsRef.current.add(qrId.toLowerCase());
                triggerHaptic?.('Success'); triggerLightningAnimation?.();
                const nextPases = offRes.remainingPasses;
                const nextHist = [offRes.completedPass, ...historialRef.current];
                const nextIng = ingresosRef.current + offRes.montoPuerta;
                pasesActivosRef.current = nextPases; historialRef.current = nextHist; ingresosRef.current = nextIng;
                setPasesActivos(nextPases); setHistorialCompleto(nextHist); setIngresosHoy(nextIng); setPendingOfflineCount(offRes.pendingQueueCount);
                if (terminalMemoryCache && terminalMemoryCache.userId === userRef.current?.id) {
                  terminalMemoryCache = { ...terminalMemoryCache, reservas: nextPases, metricas: { ...terminalMemoryCache.metricas, ingresosHoy: nextIng, historialCompleto: nextHist } };
                  terminalOfflineService.persistTerminalSwrCache(terminalMemoryCache);
                }
                setSuccessDetails({
                  nombre: offRes.nombreAtleta, monto: offRes.montoPuerta, monto_escrow: offRes.montoEscrow,
                  costo_total: offRes.costoTotal, isMasterPass: offRes.montoPuerta === 0 && offRes.montoEscrow === 0, spotNumber: offRes.spotNumber,
                });
                showSuccessSafe();
                return;
              }
            }
            throw netErr;
          }
        }
        assertValidScanReserva(reserva, recentlySettledIdsRef.current, isAdminViewRef.current, businessNameRef.current, tr);

        const { costoTotal, montoEscrow, montoPuerta } = resolvePassFinancials(reserva);
        const nombreAtleta = reserva?.perfiles?.nombre || reserva?.nombre_atleta || tr('Atleta Kinetic', 'Kinetic Athlete');
        const spotNumber = extractReservaSpotNumber(reserva);
        const prevPases = pasesActivosRef.current; const prevHistorial = historialRef.current; const prevIngresos = ingresosRef.current;
        const prevCache = terminalMemoryCache ? { ...terminalMemoryCache, metricas: { ...terminalMemoryCache.metricas } } : null;

        const resIdLow = String(reserva.id || '').toLowerCase();
        const resTokLow = String(reserva.qr_token || '').toLowerCase();
        if (resIdLow) recentlySettledIdsRef.current.add(resIdLow);
        if (resTokLow) recentlySettledIdsRef.current.add(resTokLow);

        triggerHaptic?.('Success'); triggerArcadeHaptic('checkIn'); triggerLightningAnimation?.();

        const completedPass = { ...reserva, estado: 'completada', costo_total: costoTotal, monto_escrow: montoEscrow, monto_puerta: montoPuerta, spot_number: spotNumber };
        const nextPases = prevPases.filter((item) => item.id !== reserva.id);
        const nextHistorial = [completedPass, ...prevHistorial];
        const nextIngresos = prevIngresos + montoPuerta;
        pasesActivosRef.current = nextPases; historialRef.current = nextHistorial; ingresosRef.current = nextIngresos;

        setPasesActivos(nextPases); setHistorialCompleto(nextHistorial); setIngresosHoy(nextIngresos);
        if (terminalMemoryCache && terminalMemoryCache.userId === userRef.current?.id) {
          terminalMemoryCache = {
            ...terminalMemoryCache, reservas: nextPases,
            metricas: { ...terminalMemoryCache.metricas, ingresosHoy: nextIngresos, historialCompleto: nextHistorial },
          };
          terminalOfflineService.persistTerminalSwrCache(terminalMemoryCache);
        }
        setSuccessDetails({ nombre: nombreAtleta, monto: montoPuerta, monto_escrow: montoEscrow, costo_total: costoTotal, isMasterPass: montoPuerta === 0 && montoEscrow === 0, spotNumber });
        showSuccessSafe();

        const effectiveQrToken = reserva.qr_token || qrId;
        terminalService
          .liquidarPase(
            reserva.id,
            reserva.usuario_id,
            montoPuerta,
            effectiveQrToken,
            parsedPacket.totpStep,
            parsedPacket.qrNonce,
            { isMasterAdmin: isAdminViewRef.current, nowSyncedMs: nowSynced }
          )
          .catch((rpcErr: any) => {
            if (terminalOfflineService.isNetworkOfflineError(rpcErr)) {
              terminalOfflineService
                .enqueueOfflineSettledPass(reserva, montoPuerta, { qrToken: effectiveQrToken, totpStep: parsedPacket.totpStep, qrNonce: parsedPacket.qrNonce })
                .then((cnt) => { if (isScreenMounted.current) setPendingOfflineCount(cnt); }).catch(() => {});
              return;
            }
            if (resIdLow) recentlySettledIdsRef.current.delete(resIdLow);
            if (resTokLow) recentlySettledIdsRef.current.delete(resTokLow);
            if (prevCache) terminalMemoryCache = prevCache;
            pasesActivosRef.current = prevPases; historialRef.current = prevHistorial; ingresosRef.current = prevIngresos;
            if (isScreenMounted.current) { setPasesActivos(prevPases); setHistorialCompleto(prevHistorial); setIngresosHoy(prevIngresos); setShowSuccessModal(false); }
            triggerHaptic?.('Error');
            showAlertSafe(getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en' ? 'Check-In Reverted' : 'Check-In Revertido', rpcErr?.message || tr('Fallo al liquidar pase en servidor.', 'Error settling pass on server.'));
          })
          .catch(() => {});
      } catch (err: any) {
        triggerHaptic?.('Error');
        showAlertSafe(getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en' ? 'Validation Rejected' : 'Validación Rechazada', err?.message || tr('No se pudo procesar el código QR.', 'Could not process the QR code.'));
        resetScanLock(2000);
      }
    },
    [scanned, isProcessing]
  );

  return {
    isScreenMounted, permission, requestPermission, isVerifying, hasInitialLoaded, refreshing,
    hasBusiness, setHasBusiness, businessData, setBusinessData, isAdminView, pasesActivos,
    historialCompleto, misServicios, ingresosHoy, showScanner, setShowScanner, scanned, setScanned,
    isProcessing, setIsProcessing, showSuccessModal, setShowSuccessModal, successDetails, phantomList,
    pendingOfflineCount, syncOfflineQueue,
    loadPhantomProfiles, checkBusinessStatus, fetchOperacionEstadio, onRefresh, processScannedData,
  };
}

export default useTerminal;

    FILE: src/services/terminalOfflineService.ts

    // === src/services/terminalOfflineService.ts === //
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import { triggerArcadeHaptic } from '../components/SharedUI';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { isPassExpiredWithoutCheckIn } from './kineticNoShowEngine';
import { registerSessionCleanupHook } from './kineticSessionPersistence';
import { classifyOfflineFlushOutcome, parseTerminalQrPacket, sha256HexSync } from './terminalSpeiAndStudioService';
import { getActiveKineticLanguage } from '../utils/kineticSmartTranslator';
import {
  extractSpotFromPass, getLocalServerClockOffsetMs, getSwrCacheMemInternal, hydrateServerClockOffsetMs,
  hydrateTerminalSwrCache, isNetworkOfflineError, ITerminalSwrSnapshot, persistServerClockOffsetMs,
  persistTerminalSwrCache, readTerminalSwrCacheSync, SERVER_CLOCK_OFFSET_STORAGE_KEY, TERMINAL_SWR_CACHE_V2_KEY,
} from './terminalSwrCacheHelper';

export {
  getLocalServerClockOffsetMs, hydrateServerClockOffsetMs, hydrateTerminalSwrCache, isNetworkOfflineError,
  ITerminalSwrSnapshot, persistServerClockOffsetMs, persistTerminalSwrCache, readTerminalSwrCacheSync,
  SERVER_CLOCK_OFFSET_STORAGE_KEY, TERMINAL_SWR_CACHE_V2_KEY,
};

export const TERMINAL_OFFLINE_QUEUE_KEY = '@kinetic_terminal_offline_queue_v2';
export const TERMINAL_CACHED_PASSES_KEY = '@kinetic_cached_event_passes_v2';

export interface IOfflineCheckInQueueItem {
  reservaId: string; qrToken: string; qrNonce?: string | null; totpStep?: number | null;
  negocioNombre?: string; spotNumber?: number | null; usuarioId?: string | null;
  nombreAtleta: string; montoPuerta: number; scannedAtUtc: string;
}

export interface IOfflineCheckInResult {
  ok: true; completedPass: any; remainingPasses: any[]; montoPuerta: number; montoEscrow: number;
  costoTotal: number; isMasterPass: boolean; nombreAtleta: string; spotNumber: number | null; pendingQueueCount: number;
}

const resolveOfflineFinancials = (p: any) => {
  if (p?.costo_total != null && Number(p.costo_total) === 0) return { costoTotal: 0, montoEscrow: 0, montoPuerta: 0, isMasterPass: true };
  const rawTotal = p?.costo_total != null ? Math.max(0, Number(p.costo_total) || 0) : Math.max(0, Number(p?.precio_kc) || 0);
  const montoPuerta = p?.monto_puerta != null ? Math.max(0, Number(p.monto_puerta) || 0) : rawTotal === 0 ? 0 : Math.round(rawTotal * 0.6);
  const montoEscrow = p?.monto_escrow != null ? Math.max(0, Number(p.monto_escrow) || 0) : rawTotal === 0 || montoPuerta === 0 ? 0 : Math.max(0, rawTotal - montoPuerta);
  const costoTotal = montoPuerta === 0 && montoEscrow === 0 ? 0 : rawTotal > 0 ? rawTotal : montoEscrow + montoPuerta;
  return { costoTotal, montoEscrow, montoPuerta, isMasterPass: montoPuerta === 0 && montoEscrow === 0 };
};

let _cachedPassesMem: any[] | null = null;
let _offlineQueueMem: IOfflineCheckInQueueItem[] | null = null;
let _isFlushingQueue = false;
const _enqueuingIds = new Set<string>();

registerSessionCleanupHook(() => {
  _cachedPassesMem = null;
  _offlineQueueMem = null;
  _enqueuingIds.clear();
  AsyncStorage.multiRemove([TERMINAL_CACHED_PASSES_KEY, TERMINAL_OFFLINE_QUEUE_KEY]).catch(() => {});
});

const readQueueInternal = async (): Promise<IOfflineCheckInQueueItem[]> => {
  if (_offlineQueueMem !== null) return _offlineQueueMem;
  try {
    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(TERMINAL_OFFLINE_QUEUE_KEY);
    if (raw) {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (Array.isArray(parsed)) return (_offlineQueueMem = parsed);
    }
  } catch {}
  return (_offlineQueueMem = []);
};

const writeQueueInternal = async (queue: IOfflineCheckInQueueItem[]): Promise<void> => {
  _offlineQueueMem = queue;
  AsyncStorage.setItem(TERMINAL_OFFLINE_QUEUE_KEY, JSON.stringify(queue)).catch(() => {});
};

export async function cacheActivePassesLocally(passes: any[], ownerUserId?: string | null): Promise<void> {
  if (!Array.isArray(passes)) return;
  const queue = await readQueueInternal();
  const queuedIds = new Set(queue.map((q) => String(q.reservaId)));
  const cleanOwnerId = ownerUserId ? String(ownerUserId).trim() : null;
  const sanitized = passes
    .filter(
      (p) =>
        p &&
        !p.isOptimistic &&
        !String(p.id || '').startsWith('opt_') &&
        !queuedIds.has(String(p.id)) &&
        !isPassExpiredWithoutCheckIn(p)
    )
    .map((p) => {
      const fin = resolveOfflineFinancials(p);
      return {
        id: p.id, servicio_id: p.servicio_id || null,
        cached_owner_id: cleanOwnerId,
        folio: p.folio || String(p.id || '').slice(0, 8).toUpperCase(),
        qr_token_sha256: p.qr_token_sha256 || (p.qr_token ? sha256HexSync(String(p.qr_token).trim()) : ''),
        usuario_id: p.usuario_id || null,
        spot_number: extractSpotFromPass(p), notas: p.notas || null,
        nombre_atleta: p?.perfiles?.nombre || p?.nombre_atleta || 'Atleta Kinetic',
        perfiles: p?.perfiles ? { id: p.perfiles.id, nombre: p.perfiles.nombre, nivel: p.perfiles.nivel } : null,
        servicio: p.servicio || p.nombre_servicio || p.titulo_clase || 'Experiencia Kinetic',
        negocio_nombre: p.negocio_nombre || '', estado: p.estado || 'activa',
        costo_total: fin.costoTotal, precio_kc: fin.costoTotal,
        monto_escrow: fin.montoEscrow, monto_puerta: fin.montoPuerta,
        fecha_clase: p.fecha_clase || p.fecha_hora || null,
        hora_inicio: p.hora_inicio || p.hora || null,
        created_at: p.created_at || null,
      };
    });
  _cachedPassesMem = sanitized;
  AsyncStorage.setItem(TERMINAL_CACHED_PASSES_KEY, JSON.stringify(sanitized)).catch(() => {});
}

export async function getCachedActivePassesLocally(ownerUserId?: string | null): Promise<any[]> {
  const scopeByOwner = (arr: any[]): any[] => {
    if (ownerUserId === undefined) return arr;
    const cleanOwner = ownerUserId ? String(ownerUserId).trim() : '';
    if (!cleanOwner) return [];
    return arr.filter((p) => String(p?.cached_owner_id || '') === cleanOwner);
  };
  if (_cachedPassesMem !== null) {
    const fresh = _cachedPassesMem.filter((p) => !isPassExpiredWithoutCheckIn(p));
    if (fresh.length !== _cachedPassesMem.length) {
      _cachedPassesMem = fresh;
      AsyncStorage.setItem(TERMINAL_CACHED_PASSES_KEY, JSON.stringify(fresh)).catch(() => {});
    }
    return scopeByOwner(fresh);
  }
  try {
    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(TERMINAL_CACHED_PASSES_KEY);
    if (raw) {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
        const fresh = parsed.filter((p) => !isPassExpiredWithoutCheckIn(p));
        _cachedPassesMem = fresh;
        if (fresh.length !== parsed.length) {
          AsyncStorage.setItem(TERMINAL_CACHED_PASSES_KEY, JSON.stringify(fresh)).catch(() => {});
        }
        return scopeByOwner(fresh);
      }
    }
  } catch {}
  return [];
}

export async function getPendingOfflineQueueCount(): Promise<number> {
  return (await readQueueInternal()).length;
}

export async function getPendingOfflineQueueIds(): Promise<Set<string>> {
  const queue = await readQueueInternal();
  const ids = new Set<string>();
  for (const q of queue) {
    const rid = String(q.reservaId || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const tok = String(q.qrToken || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (rid) ids.add(rid);
    if (tok) ids.add(tok);
  }
  for (const id of _enqueuingIds) {
    if (id) ids.add(id.toLowerCase());
  }
  return ids;
}

export async function enqueueOfflineSettledPass(
  reserva: any,
  montoPuerta: number,
  meta?: { qrToken?: string | null; totpStep?: number | null; qrNonce?: string | null }
): Promise<number> {
  const resId = String(reserva?.id || '');
  const resIdLow = resId.toLowerCase();
  if (!resId || _enqueuingIds.has(resIdLow)) return _offlineQueueMem?.length ?? 0;
  _enqueuingIds.add(resIdLow);
  try {
    const queue = await readQueueInternal();
    if (queue.some((q) => String(q.reservaId).toLowerCase() === resIdLow)) return queue.length;
    const nextQueue: IOfflineCheckInQueueItem[] = [
      ...queue,
      {
        reservaId: resId,
        qrToken: String(meta?.qrToken || reserva?.qr_token || resId),
        qrNonce: meta?.qrNonce ?? null,
        totpStep: meta?.totpStep ?? null,
        negocioNombre: String(reserva?.negocio_nombre || ''),
        spotNumber: extractSpotFromPass(reserva),
        usuarioId: reserva?.usuario_id || null,
        nombreAtleta: reserva?.perfiles?.nombre || reserva?.nombre_atleta || 'Atleta Kinetic',
        montoPuerta,
        scannedAtUtc: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
    ];
    await writeQueueInternal(nextQueue);
    const cached = await getCachedActivePassesLocally();
    _cachedPassesMem = cached.filter((p) => String(p?.id) !== resId);
    AsyncStorage.setItem(TERMINAL_CACHED_PASSES_KEY, JSON.stringify(_cachedPassesMem)).catch(() => {});
    return nextQueue.length;
  } finally {
    _enqueuingIds.delete(resIdLow);
  }
}

export async function processOfflineCheckIn(
  scannedToken: string,
  activePasses: any[],
  options?: { isAdminView?: boolean; businessName?: string | null; nowSyncedMs?: number }
): Promise<IOfflineCheckInResult | null> {
  const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
  const syncClock = options?.nowSyncedMs ?? Date.now() + getLocalServerClockOffsetMs();
  const parsed = parseTerminalQrPacket(scannedToken, syncClock);
  if (parsed.isExpired) {
    throw new Error(isEn ? 'Expired QR key (older than 60s).' : 'Llave QR expirada (más de 60s).');
  }
  if (!options?.isAdminView && (parsed.format === 'manual' || parsed.totpStep == null)) {
    throw new Error(isEn ? 'Scan rejected: dynamic 60s TOTP QR is required.' : 'Escaneo rechazado: se requiere QR dinámico TOTP de 60s.');
  }
  const candidate = parsed.ok ? parsed.qrIdentifier : '';
  if (!candidate) return null;
  const norm = candidate.toLowerCase();
  const alreadyMsg = isEn
    ? 'This pass was already validated offline and awaits sync.'
    : 'Este pase ya fue validado en modo offline y espera sincronización.';
  if (_enqueuingIds.has(norm)) throw new Error(alreadyMsg);
  _enqueuingIds.add(norm);
  let lockedMatchedId = '';
  try {
    const storedPasses = await getCachedActivePassesLocally();
    const mergedMap = new Map<string, any>();
    for (const p of [...(activePasses || []), ...storedPasses]) {
      if (p?.id) mergedMap.set(String(p.id), p);
    }
    const queue = await readQueueInternal();
    const alreadyQueued = new Set(queue.map((q) => String(q.reservaId).toLowerCase()));

    const candSha = sha256HexSync(candidate).toLowerCase();
    let matched: any = null;
    for (const pass of mergedMap.values()) {
      const idLow = String(pass?.id || '').toLowerCase();
      const tokLow = String(pass?.qr_token || '').toLowerCase();
      const shaLow = String(pass?.qr_token_sha256 || '').toLowerCase();
      const folioLow = String(pass?.folio || idLow.slice(0, 8)).replace(/^#/, '').toLowerCase();
      if (
        idLow === norm ||
        (tokLow && tokLow === norm) ||
        (shaLow && shaLow === candSha) ||
        (norm.length >= 6 && (folioLow === norm || idLow.slice(0, 8) === norm))
      ) {
        if (alreadyQueued.has(idLow) || _enqueuingIds.has(idLow)) {
          throw new Error(alreadyMsg);
        }
        matched = pass;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (!matched) return null;
    lockedMatchedId = String(matched.id || '').toLowerCase();
    if (lockedMatchedId && lockedMatchedId !== norm) _enqueuingIds.add(lockedMatchedId);

    if (options && options.isAdminView === false) {
      const expectedBiz = String(options.businessName || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const passBiz = String(matched?.negocio_nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      if (!expectedBiz || passBiz !== expectedBiz) {
        throw new Error(
          isEn
            ? `This pass belongs to "${matched?.negocio_nombre || 'another venue'}", not "${options.businessName || ''}".`
            : `Este pase pertenece a "${matched?.negocio_nombre || 'otra sede'}", no a "${options.businessName || ''}".`,
        );
      }
    }

    const nowUtc = new Date().toISOString();
    const fin = resolveOfflineFinancials(matched);
    const nombreAtleta = matched?.perfiles?.nombre || matched?.nombre_atleta || 'Atleta Kinetic';
    const spotNumber = extractSpotFromPass(matched);
    const completedPass = {
      ...matched, estado: 'completada', costo_total: fin.costoTotal, spot_number: spotNumber,
      monto_escrow: fin.montoEscrow, monto_puerta: fin.montoPuerta, offline_synced: false, scanned_at_utc: nowUtc,
    };
    const remainingPasses = Array.from(mergedMap.values()).filter((p) => String(p.id) !== String(matched.id));
    _cachedPassesMem = remainingPasses;
    const latestQueue = await readQueueInternal();
    const nextQueue: IOfflineCheckInQueueItem[] = [
      ...latestQueue,
      {
        reservaId: String(matched.id), qrToken: String(matched.qr_token || candidate),
        qrNonce: parsed.qrNonce, totpStep: parsed.totpStep, negocioNombre: String(matched?.negocio_nombre || ''),
        spotNumber, usuarioId: matched.usuario_id || null, nombreAtleta, montoPuerta: fin.montoPuerta, scannedAtUtc: nowUtc,
      },
    ];
    await Promise.all([
      writeQueueInternal(nextQueue),
      AsyncStorage.setItem(TERMINAL_CACHED_PASSES_KEY, JSON.stringify(remainingPasses)).catch(() => {}),
    ]);
    triggerArcadeHaptic('checkIn');
    return {
      ok: true, completedPass, remainingPasses, montoPuerta: fin.montoPuerta, montoEscrow: fin.montoEscrow,
      costoTotal: fin.costoTotal, isMasterPass: fin.isMasterPass, nombreAtleta, spotNumber, pendingQueueCount: nextQueue.length,
    };
  } finally {
    _enqueuingIds.delete(norm);
    if (lockedMatchedId && lockedMatchedId !== norm) _enqueuingIds.delete(lockedMatchedId);
  }
}

export async function flushOfflineCheckInsQueue(onSuccess?: (count: number) => void): Promise<number> {
  if (_isFlushingQueue || (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && navigator.onLine === false)) return 0;
  _isFlushingQueue = true;
  try {
    const queue = await readQueueInternal();
    if (queue.length === 0) return 0;
    const curStep = Math.floor((Date.now() + getLocalServerClockOffsetMs()) / 60_000);
    const purgedIds = new Set<string>();
    let settledCount = 0;

    await Promise.all(
      queue.map(async (item) => {
        try {
          const isStaleStep = item.totpStep != null && Math.abs(curStep - item.totpStep) > 1;
          const liveTotp = item.totpStep != null && !isStaleStep ? item.totpStep : null;
          let totpRes = await supabase.rpc('liquidar_checkin_qr_totp', {
            p_reserva_id: item.reservaId,
            p_qr_token: item.qrToken || null,
            p_totp_step: item.totpStep ?? null,
            p_qr_nonce: item.qrNonce || null,
            p_offline_scanned_at: isStaleStep ? (item.scannedAtUtc || null) : null,
          });
          if (totpRes.error && totpRes.error.code === 'PGRST202') {
            totpRes = await supabase.rpc('liquidar_checkin_qr_totp', {
              p_reserva_id: item.reservaId,
              p_qr_token: item.qrToken || null,
              p_totp_step: liveTotp,
              p_qr_nonce: item.qrNonce || null,
            });
          }
          if (!totpRes.error) {
            purgedIds.add(String(item.reservaId));
            settledCount += 1;
            return;
          }
          const isMissingRpc =
            totpRes.error.code === 'PGRST202' || String(totpRes.error.message || '').includes('liquidar_checkin_qr_totp');
          if (!isMissingRpc) throw totpRes.error;

          const escrowRes = await supabase.rpc('liquidar_pase_escrow', { p_reserva_id: item.reservaId });
          if (!escrowRes.error) {
            purgedIds.add(String(item.reservaId));
            settledCount += 1;
            return;
          }
          throw escrowRes.error;
        } catch (err: any) {
          if (classifyOfflineFlushOutcome(err) === 'synced_or_terminal') {
            purgedIds.add(String(item.reservaId));
            if (/QR_YA_UTILIZADO/i.test(String(err?.message || ''))) settledCount += 1;
          }
        }
      })
    );

    if (purgedIds.size > 0) {
      const latest = await readQueueInternal();
      await writeQueueInternal(latest.filter((q) => !purgedIds.has(String(q.reservaId))));
      if (settledCount > 0) onSuccess?.(settledCount);
    }
    return settledCount;
  } finally {
    _isFlushingQueue = false;
  }
}

export async function purgePassById(reservaId: string): Promise<void> {
  const cleanId = String(reservaId || '').trim();
  if (!cleanId) return;
  const cached = await getCachedActivePassesLocally();
  _cachedPassesMem = cached.filter((p) => String(p?.id || '') !== cleanId);
  const queue = await readQueueInternal();
  const nextQueue = queue.filter((q) => String(q.reservaId) !== cleanId);
  const swr = getSwrCacheMemInternal();
  if (swr && Array.isArray(swr.reservas)) {
    persistTerminalSwrCache({
      ...swr,
      reservas: swr.reservas.filter((r) => String(r?.id || '') !== cleanId),
    });
  }
  await Promise.all([
    writeQueueInternal(nextQueue),
    AsyncStorage.setItem(TERMINAL_CACHED_PASSES_KEY, JSON.stringify(_cachedPassesMem)).catch(() => {}),
  ]);
}

export async function purgePassesForDeletedService(
  serviceId?: string | null,
  serviceName?: string | null
): Promise<void> {
  const cleanSid = String(serviceId || '').trim();
  const cleanName = String(serviceName || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const matchesTarget = (r: any): boolean => {
    if (cleanSid && String(r?.servicio_id || '') === cleanSid) return true;
    if (!r?.servicio_id && cleanName) {
      const nm = String(r?.nombre_servicio || r?.servicio || r?.titulo_clase || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      if (nm === cleanName) return true;
    }
    return false;
  };
  const cached = await getCachedActivePassesLocally();
  const removedIds = new Set<string>();
  _cachedPassesMem = cached.filter((p) => {
    if (!matchesTarget(p)) return true;
    if (p?.id) removedIds.add(String(p.id));
    return false;
  });
  const swr = getSwrCacheMemInternal();
  if (swr) {
    const nextServs = Array.isArray(swr.servicios) ? swr.servicios.filter((s) => String(s?.id || '') !== cleanSid) : [];
    const nextRes = Array.isArray(swr.reservas)
      ? swr.reservas.filter((r) => {
          if (!matchesTarget(r)) return true;
          if (r?.id) removedIds.add(String(r.id));
          return false;
        })
      : [];
    persistTerminalSwrCache({ ...swr, servicios: nextServs, reservas: nextRes });
  }
  const queue = await readQueueInternal();
  const nextQueue = removedIds.size > 0 ? queue.filter((q) => !removedIds.has(String(q.reservaId))) : queue;
  await Promise.all([
    writeQueueInternal(nextQueue),
    AsyncStorage.setItem(TERMINAL_CACHED_PASSES_KEY, JSON.stringify(_cachedPassesMem)).catch(() => {}),
  ]);
}

export const terminalOfflineService = {
  cacheActivePassesLocally, getCachedActivePassesLocally, getPendingOfflineQueueCount, getPendingOfflineQueueIds,
  enqueueOfflineSettledPass, processOfflineCheckIn, flushOfflineCheckInsQueue,
  isNetworkOfflineError, getLocalServerClockOffsetMs, persistServerClockOffsetMs, hydrateServerClockOffsetMs,
  readTerminalSwrCacheSync, hydrateTerminalSwrCache, persistTerminalSwrCache,
  purgePassById, purgePassesForDeletedService,
};

export default terminalOfflineService;

import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { emitKineticRadarUpdated } from '../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import {
  cascadeRefundAndDeleteServiceReservas,
  DELETED_SERVICE_STATES,
  isValidUuid,
} from './eventosHelpers';
import {
  executeTerminalMutation,
  fetchBusinessStatusHelper,
  fetchOperacionEstadioHelper,
  runResilientServiceWrite,
  sanitizeTerminalGeoPayload,
} from './terminalStadiumOpsHelper';
import { validateQrCheckinSettlementGuard } from './terminalSpeiAndStudioService';

const MASTER_ADMIN_EMAILS_TERMINAL = new Set([
  'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
  'administrador@kinetic.com',
  'kinetic@kinetic.com',
  'admin@kinetic.com',
]);

const RESERVAS_TERMINAL_SAFE_COLS =
  'id, usuario_id, negocio_id, negocio_nombre, servicio_id, servicio, nombre_servicio, titulo_clase, categoria, estado, spot_number, fecha_clase, hora_inicio, precio_kc, costo_total, monto_escrow, monto_puerta, escrow_membresia, escrow_topup, created_at';

export const terminalService = {
  // 1. ESTADO DEL NEGOCIO B2B (Con auto-vinculación inteligente y auto-aprovisionamiento)
  async fetchBusinessStatus(
    userId: string,
    userEmail?: string,
    profileName?: string,
    defaultCategory: string = 'Club',
    userRole?: string
  ) {
    return fetchBusinessStatusHelper(userId, userEmail, profileName, defaultCategory, userRole);
  },

  // 2. OPERACIÓN DEL ESTADIO (Blindado contra fugas B2B y reservas huérfanas)
  async fetchOperacionEstadio(nombreEstadio: string | null, isGlobal: boolean, hasActiveBusiness = false) {
    return fetchOperacionEstadioHelper(nombreEstadio, isGlobal, hasActiveBusiness);
  },

  // 3. ACTIVACIÓN Y VINCULACIÓN DE CLUBES
  async activarEstadio(payload: Record<string, unknown>) {
    const requestedEstado = String(payload?.estado || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const safeEstado = requestedEstado === 'activo' || !requestedEstado ? 'pendiente' : requestedEstado;
    const cleanPayload = sanitizeTerminalGeoPayload({ ...payload, estado: safeEstado });
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('afiliados')
      .insert([cleanPayload])
      .select('id, propietario_id, nombre, email_contacto, ubicacion, disciplina, categoria, estado, created_at')
      .single();
    if (error) throw new Error(`Error al activar estadio: ${error.message}`);
    return data || cleanPayload;
  },

  async activateOperatorProfile(
    userId: string,
    email: string,
    data: { nombre: string; ubicacion?: string; disciplina?: string; categoria?: string }
  ) {
    const cleanNombre = String(data?.nombre || '').trim();
    if (!cleanNombre) {
      throw new Error('Ingresa el nombre comercial de tu Club o Especialista.');
    }
    const cleanUbicacion = String(data?.ubicacion || '').trim();
    const cleanDisciplina = String(data?.disciplina || 'Fuerza').trim();
    const cleanCategoria = String(data?.categoria || data?.disciplina || 'Club').trim();
    const payload = {
      propietario_id: userId,
      email_contacto: String(email || '').trim().toLowerCase() || null,
      nombre: cleanNombre,
      ubicacion: cleanUbicacion,
      disciplina: cleanDisciplina,
      categoria: cleanCategoria,
      estado: 'pendiente',
    };

    const { error: rpcErr } = await supabase.rpc('registrar_operador_b2b_propio', {
      p_nombre: cleanNombre,
      p_tipo_socio: cleanCategoria,
      p_rfc: null,
      p_telefono: null,
      p_disciplina: cleanDisciplina,
      p_avatar_url: null,
    });

    if (rpcErr) {
      const rpcCode = String(rpcErr.code || '');
      const isMissingRpc = rpcCode === '42883' || rpcCode === 'PGRST202';
      if (!isMissingRpc) {
        throw new Error(rpcErr.message || 'No se pudo activar el perfil operador B2B.');
      }
      return this.activarEstadio(payload);
    }

    if (cleanUbicacion) {
      await supabase.from('afiliados').update({ ubicacion: cleanUbicacion }).eq('propietario_id', userId);
    }
    const { data: afRow } = await supabase
      .from('afiliados')
      .select('id, propietario_id, nombre, email_contacto, ubicacion, disciplina, categoria, estado, created_at')
      .eq('propietario_id', userId)
      .limit(1)
      .maybeSingle();
    return afRow || payload;
  },

  async fetchPhantomProfiles() {
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('afiliados')
      .select('id, nombre, ubicacion, disciplina, categoria, estado, created_at')
      .eq('estado', 'inactivo')
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false });

    if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
    return data || [];
  },

  async linkPhantomProfile(id: string, email: string, propietarioId?: string) {
    if (!isValidUuid(id)) {
      throw new Error('ID de perfil fantasma inválido.');
    }
    if (propietarioId && !isValidUuid(propietarioId)) {
      throw new Error('ID de propietario inválido.');
    }
    const updatePayload: Record<string, unknown> = { estado: 'pendiente', email_contacto: email };
    if (propietarioId) updatePayload.propietario_id = propietarioId;
    return executeTerminalMutation(
      supabase.from('afiliados').update(updatePayload).eq('id', id).is('propietario_id', null).eq('estado', 'inactivo'),
      'Error vinculando perfil fantasma'
    );
  },

  // 4. CRUD DE SERVICIOS CON REEMBOLSO AUTOMÁTICO DE ESCROW, CONSERVACIÓN DE RECIBO Y +1 STRIKE OPERATIVO
  async createService(payload: any) {
    const cleanPayload: Record<string, any> = sanitizeTerminalGeoPayload(payload);
    if (!cleanPayload.galeria && Array.isArray(payload?.atributos?.galeria) && payload.atributos.galeria.length > 0) {
      cleanPayload.galeria = payload.atributos.galeria;
    }
    return runResilientServiceWrite(cleanPayload, payload);
  },

  async updateService(id: string, payload: any) {
    return runResilientServiceWrite(sanitizeTerminalGeoPayload(payload), payload, id);
  },

  async deleteService(id: string) {
    await cascadeRefundAndDeleteServiceReservas(id, false);

    const { data: deletedService, error } = await supabase
      .from('servicios')
      .delete()
      .eq('id', id)
      .select();

    if (error) throw new Error(`Fricción eliminando: ${error.message}`);
    if (!deletedService || deletedService.length === 0) {
      throw new Error(
        'BLOQUEO RLS: Supabase denegó el borrado. Ajusta políticas en la tabla "servicios".'
      );
    }

    emitKineticRadarUpdated();
    return true;
  },

  // 5. ESCÁNER QR Y LIQUIDACIÓN ACID EN PUERTA (60% / 0 KC PASE MAESTRO)
  async findReservaByQR(qrId: string, isUUID: boolean, scopeBusinessName?: string | null) {
    const cleanQr = String(qrId || '').replace(/^#/, '').trim();
    if (!cleanQr) return null;
    if (scopeBusinessName !== undefined && scopeBusinessName !== null && !String(scopeBusinessName).trim()) {
      return null;
    }

    const cleanScope = scopeBusinessName ? String(scopeBusinessName).trim() : null;
    let reservaBD: any = null;
    let useLegacyTableFallback = false;

    const { data: rpcRow, error: rpcErr } = await supabase.rpc('resolver_reserva_escaneo_b2b', {
      p_qr_or_folio: cleanQr,
      p_negocio_nombre: cleanScope,
    });
    if (!rpcErr) {
      reservaBD = Array.isArray(rpcRow) ? rpcRow[0] || null : rpcRow || null;
    } else if (
      rpcErr.code === 'PGRST202' ||
      rpcErr.code === '42883' ||
      String(rpcErr.message || '').includes('resolver_reserva_escaneo_b2b')
    ) {
      useLegacyTableFallback = true;
    } else {
      throw new Error(rpcErr.message);
    }

    if (useLegacyTableFallback) {
      let query = supabase.from('reservas').select(RESERVAS_TERMINAL_SAFE_COLS);
      if (cleanScope) {
        query = query.eq('negocio_nombre', cleanScope);
      }
      query = isUUID && isValidUuid(cleanQr)
        ? query.or(`id.eq.${cleanQr},qr_token.eq.${cleanQr}`).limit(1)
        : query.eq('qr_token', cleanQr).limit(1);

      const { data: reservaArray, error } = await query;
      if (error) throw new Error(error.message);

      reservaBD = reservaArray && reservaArray.length > 0 ? reservaArray[0] : null;

      if (!reservaBD && !isUUID && /^[a-z0-9_-]{6,24}$/i.test(cleanQr)) {
        let folioQuery = supabase
          .from('reservas')
          .select(RESERVAS_TERMINAL_SAFE_COLS)
          .in('estado', ['activa', 'pendiente', 'confirmada'])
          .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
          .limit(80);
        if (cleanScope) {
          folioQuery = folioQuery.eq('negocio_nombre', cleanScope);
        }
        const { data: activeRows } = await folioQuery;
        const codeLow = cleanQr.toLowerCase();
        reservaBD = (activeRows || []).find((r: any) => {
          if (cleanScope && String(r?.negocio_nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase() !== cleanScope.toLowerCase()) {
            return false;
          }
          const idLow = String(r?.id || '').toLowerCase();
          const folioLow = String(r?.folio || idLow.slice(0, 8)).replace(/^#/, '').toLowerCase();
          return folioLow === codeLow || idLow.slice(0, 8) === codeLow;
        }) || null;
      }
    }

    if (!reservaBD) return null;

    const sid = String(reservaBD.servicio_id || '').trim();
    if (sid && isValidUuid(sid)) {
      const { data: srvRow } = await supabase
        .from('servicios')
        .select('id, estado')
        .eq('id', sid)
        .maybeSingle();
      if (!srvRow || DELETED_SERVICE_STATES.has(String(srvRow?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim())) {
        return null;
      }
    }

    let pData: any = null;
    const { data: rpcPerf, error: rpcPerfErr } = await supabase.rpc('obtener_perfil_checkin_b2b', {
      p_reserva_id: reservaBD.id,
    });
    if (!rpcPerfErr && rpcPerf) {
      pData = rpcPerf;
    } else if (rpcPerfErr?.code === 'PGRST202' || String(rpcPerfErr?.message || '').includes('obtener_perfil_checkin_b2b')) {
      const { data: fallbackPerf } = await supabase
        .from('perfiles')
        .select('id, nombre, avatar_url, nivel')
        .eq('id', reservaBD.usuario_id)
        .maybeSingle();
      pData = fallbackPerf;
    }

    reservaBD.perfiles = pData || { nombre: 'Atleta Kinetic' };
    return reservaBD;
  },

  // 6. CONEXIÓN AL MOTOR ACID: Liquidación atómica del 60% o Pase Maestro 0 KC en puerta
  async liquidarPase(
    reservaId: string,
    _usuarioId?: string | null,
    montoPuertaHint?: number,
    qrToken?: string | null,
    totpStep?: number | null,
    qrNonce?: string | null,
    options?: { isMasterAdmin?: boolean; nowSyncedMs?: number }
  ) {
    let isVerifiedMasterAdmin = false;
    if (options?.isMasterAdmin) {
      try {
        const { data: authRes } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
        const userEmail = String(authRes?.user?.email || '').trim().toLowerCase();
        isVerifiedMasterAdmin = MASTER_ADMIN_EMAILS_TERMINAL.has(userEmail);
      } catch {
        isVerifiedMasterAdmin = false;
      }
    }

    const guard = validateQrCheckinSettlementGuard({
      qrToken,
      totpStep,
      nowSyncedMs: options?.nowSyncedMs,
      isMasterAdmin: isVerifiedMasterAdmin,
    });
    if (!guard.ok) {
      throw new Error(`VALIDACION_QR_TOTP_REQUERIDA: ${guard.reason}`);
    }

    const { data: totpData, error: totpErr } = await supabase.rpc('liquidar_checkin_qr_totp', {
      p_reserva_id: reservaId,
      p_qr_token: qrToken || null,
      p_totp_step: totpStep ?? null,
      p_qr_nonce: qrNonce || null,
    });
    if (!totpErr) {
      emitKineticRadarUpdated();
      return totpData;
    }

    if (totpErr.code !== 'PGRST202' && !String(totpErr.message || '').includes('liquidar_checkin_qr_totp')) {
      throw new Error(totpErr.message);
    }

    const isZeroDoorPass = montoPuertaHint !== undefined && Number(montoPuertaHint) <= 0;
    if (isZeroDoorPass && isVerifiedMasterAdmin) {
      const { data: directRows, error: directErr } = await supabase
        .from('reservas')
        .update({ estado: 'completada', monto_puerta: 0 })
        .eq('id', reservaId)
        .eq('monto_puerta', 0)
        .eq('monto_escrow', 0)
        .in('estado', ['activa', 'pendiente', 'confirmada'])
        .select('id');
      if (!directErr && Array.isArray(directRows) && directRows.length > 0) {
        emitKineticRadarUpdated();
        return directRows[0];
      }
    }

    const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('liquidar_pase_escrow', {
      p_reserva_id: reservaId,
    });
    if (!error) {
      emitKineticRadarUpdated();
      return data;
    }
    throw new Error(error.message);
  },
};

import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { saveEventAttrsDualCache } from '../utils/eventEditorHelpers';
import { emitKineticRadarUpdated } from '../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import { claimRefundAndDeleteOrphanRow, DELETED_SERVICE_STATES, isValidUuid } from './eventosHelpers';
import { isValidGeoCoordPair, parseStrictCoord, resolveServiceWriteOnceGeoPatch } from './kineticCloudAiAndGeoService';

const ADMIN_EMAILS_SET = new Set([
  'administrador@kinetic.com.mx', 'administrador@kinetic.com', 'kinetic@kinetic.com', 'admin@kinetic.com',
]);
const GENERIC_NON_BIZ_NAMES = new Set([
  'atleta kinetic', 'atleta elite', 'miembro kinetic', 'kinetic member',
  'sede kinetic', 'kinetic venue', 'terminal global kinetic', 'administrador', 'admin kinetic',
]);

export const executeTerminalMutation = async (
  query: PromiseLike<any>,
  errorPrefix: string = 'Fricción operativa'
) => {
  const { data, error } = await query;
  if (error) throw new Error(`${errorPrefix}: ${error.message}`);
  return data || true;
};

export const sanitizeTerminalGeoPayload = (payload: any) => {
  if (!payload || typeof payload !== 'object') return payload;
  const clean = { ...payload };
  if (clean.coords) {
    const cLat = parseStrictCoord(clean.coords.latitude);
    const cLng = parseStrictCoord(clean.coords.longitude);
    if (isValidGeoCoordPair(cLat, cLng)) {
      clean.latitud = cLat as number;
      clean.longitud = cLng as number;
    } else {
      delete clean.latitud;
      delete clean.longitud;
    }
    delete clean.coords;
  } else if ('latitud' in clean || 'longitud' in clean) {
    if (!isValidGeoCoordPair(clean.latitud, clean.longitud)) {
      delete clean.latitud;
      delete clean.longitud;
    }
  }
  delete clean.cupo_actual;
  return clean;
};

const normalizeAdminAffiliateRow = async (row: any) => {
  if (!row) return row;
  const norm = String(row?.nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (norm === 'terminal global kinetic') {
    await supabase.from('afiliados').update({ nombre: 'Kinetic Oficial' }).eq('id', row.id);
    return { ...row, nombre: 'Kinetic Oficial', isOfficialKinetic: true };
  }
  return norm === 'kinetic oficial' ? { ...row, nombre: 'Kinetic Oficial', isOfficialKinetic: true } : row;
};

export const runResilientServiceWrite = async (
  cleanPayload: Record<string, any>,
  rawPayload: any,
  idForUpdate?: string
) => {
  if (String(cleanPayload.negocio_nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase() === 'terminal global kinetic') {
    cleanPayload.negocio_nombre = 'Kinetic Oficial';
  }
  const geoPatch = await resolveServiceWriteOnceGeoPatch({
    serviceId: idForUpdate,
    ubicacion: String(cleanPayload.ubicacion || ''),
    existingPlaceId: typeof rawPayload?.place_id === 'string' ? rawPayload.place_id : null,
    existingGeoVerifiedAt: typeof rawPayload?.geo_verified_at === 'string' ? rawPayload.geo_verified_at : null,
    prevUbicacion: typeof rawPayload?.prev_ubicacion === 'string' ? rawPayload.prev_ubicacion : null,
  });
  Object.assign(cleanPayload, geoPatch);
  let lastError: any = null;
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 10; attempt++) {
    const query = idForUpdate
      ? supabase.from('servicios').update(cleanPayload).eq('id', idForUpdate).select('*').maybeSingle()
      : supabase.from('servicios').insert([cleanPayload]).select('*').maybeSingle();
    const { data, error } = await query;
    if (!error) {
      const savedLayout = rawPayload?.studio_layout_json || rawPayload?.atributos?.studio_layout;
      if (rawPayload?.atributos && typeof rawPayload.atributos === 'object') {
        await saveEventAttrsDualCache(
          [idForUpdate || data?.id, data?.nombre_servicio || rawPayload.nombre_servicio],
          rawPayload.atributos,
          savedLayout
        );
      }
      emitKineticRadarUpdated();
      return idForUpdate ? data || true : data;
    }
    lastError = error;
    const errMsg = String(error.message || '');
    const colMatch = errMsg.match(/Could not find the '([^']+)' column/i) || errMsg.match(/column "([^"]+)" of relation/i);
    if (colMatch?.[1] && colMatch[1] in cleanPayload) {
      delete cleanPayload[colMatch[1]];
      continue;
    }
    break;
  }
  const prefix = idForUpdate ? 'Error actualizando servicio' : 'Fricción creando servicio';
  throw new Error(`${prefix}: ${lastError?.message || 'Error desconocido'}`);
};

export async function fetchBusinessStatusHelper(
  userId: string,
  userEmail?: string,
  profileName?: string,
  defaultCategory: string = 'Club',
  userRole?: string
) {
  const cleanEmail = String(userEmail || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const roleNorm = String(userRole || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const isAdminAccount = ADMIN_EMAILS_SET.has(cleanEmail) || roleNorm === 'admin' || roleNorm === 'fundador';

  if (isAdminAccount) {
    const { data: officialRows, error: offErr } = await supabase.from('afiliados').select('*').ilike('nombre', 'Kinetic Oficial').limit(1);
    if (offErr) throw new Error(offErr.message);
    if (officialRows && officialRows.length > 0) {
      return [{ ...officialRows[0], nombre: 'Kinetic Oficial', isOfficialKinetic: true }];
    }
  }

  const { data, error } = await supabase.from('afiliados').select('*').eq('propietario_id', userId).limit(1);
  if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
  if (data && data.length > 0) {
    return [isAdminAccount ? await normalizeAdminAffiliateRow(data[0]) : data[0]];
  }

  const isExplicitPartnerRole = ['negocio', 'socio', 'estadio', 'especialista', 'club', 'afiliado', 'partner b2b', 'partner_b2b', 'b2b'].includes(roleNorm);
  const escapeLike = (v: string) => v.replace(/[\\%_]/g, '\\$&');

  if (cleanEmail && cleanEmail.includes('@') && isExplicitPartnerRole) {
    const { data: byEmail } = await supabase
      .from('afiliados')
      .select('id, nombre, email_contacto, propietario_id, estado')
      .is('propietario_id', null)
      .ilike('email_contacto', escapeLike(cleanEmail))
      .limit(1);
    if (byEmail && byEmail.length > 0 && String(byEmail[0]?.email_contacto || '').trim().toLowerCase() === cleanEmail) {
      const { data: claimed, error: claimErr } = await supabase
        .from('afiliados')
        .update({ propietario_id: userId, estado: 'pendiente' })
        .eq('id', byEmail[0].id)
        .is('propietario_id', null)
        .select('*');
      if (!claimErr && claimed && claimed.length > 0) {
        return [isAdminAccount ? await normalizeAdminAffiliateRow(claimed[0]) : claimed[0]];
      }
    }
  }

  if (isAdminAccount) {
    const { data: officialRows } = await supabase.from('afiliados').select('*').ilike('nombre', 'Kinetic Oficial').limit(1);
    return officialRows && officialRows.length > 0
      ? [{ ...officialRows[0], nombre: 'Kinetic Oficial', isOfficialKinetic: true }]
      : [];
  }

  if (profileName && profileName.trim() !== '') {
    const cleanName = profileName.trim();
    const lowerName = cleanName.toLowerCase();
    const isGenericAthleteName = GENERIC_NON_BIZ_NAMES.has(lowerName) || lowerName.startsWith('atleta ');

    if (isExplicitPartnerRole && !isGenericAthleteName) {
      const newPayload = {
        propietario_id: userId, nombre: cleanName, email_contacto: cleanEmail,
        ubicacion: '', categoria: defaultCategory, disciplina: 'Fuerza', estado: 'pendiente',
      };
      const { data: createdRows } = await supabase.from('afiliados').insert([newPayload]).select('*');
      if (createdRows && createdRows.length > 0) return createdRows;
    }
  }

  return [];
}

export async function fetchOperacionEstadioHelper(
  nombreEstadio: string | null,
  isGlobal: boolean,
  hasActiveBusiness = false
) {
  const cleanEstadio = String(nombreEstadio || '').trim();
  const normEstadio = cleanEstadio.toLowerCase();
  if (!isGlobal && !cleanEstadio) return { reservas: [], servicios: [], perfiles: [] };

  let queryActivas = supabase
    .from('reservas')
    .select('id, usuario_id, negocio_id, negocio_nombre, servicio_id, servicio, nombre_servicio, titulo_clase, categoria, estado, monto_escrow, monto_puerta, precio_kc, costo_total, escrow_membresia, escrow_topup, fecha_hora, fecha_clase, hora_inicio, spot_number, created_at, updated_at')
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false });
  let queryServicios = supabase.from('servicios').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false });
  if (!isGlobal) {
    queryActivas = queryActivas.eq('negocio_nombre', cleanEstadio);
    queryServicios = queryServicios.eq('negocio_nombre', cleanEstadio);
  }

  const [resReservas, resServs] = await Promise.all([queryActivas, queryServicios]);
  if (resReservas.error) throw new Error(resReservas.error.message);
  if (resServs.error) throw new Error(resServs.error.message);

  const rawReservas = resReservas.data || [];
  const validServicios = (resServs.data || [])
    .filter((s: any) => {
      if (!s) return false;
      if (DELETED_SERVICE_STATES.has(String(s?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim())) return false;
      if (!isGlobal && String(s?.negocio_nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase() !== normEstadio) return false;
      return true;
    })
    .map((s: any) => {
      const rawNeg = String(s?.negocio_nombre || '').trim();
      return !rawNeg || rawNeg.toLowerCase() === 'terminal global kinetic' ? { ...s, negocio_nombre: 'Kinetic Oficial' } : s;
    });

  const validServIds = new Set<string>();
  const validServNames = new Set<string>();
  for (const s of validServicios) {
    if (s?.id) validServIds.add(String(s.id).trim());
    [s?.nombre_servicio, s?.titulo, s?.nombre].forEach((n) => {
      const norm = String(n || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      if (norm) validServNames.add(norm);
    });
  }

  const orphansInTerminal: any[] = [];
  const reservasData = rawReservas.filter((r: any) => {
    if (!r) return false;
    if (!isGlobal && String(r?.negocio_nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase() !== normEstadio) return false;
    const st = String(r?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    const sid = String(r?.servicio_id || '').trim();
    const exists = sid
      ? isValidUuid(sid) && validServIds.has(sid)
      : [r?.nombre_servicio, r?.servicio, r?.titulo_clase].map((v) => String(v || '').trim().toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean).some((nm) => validServNames.has(nm));
    if (!exists) {
      if (st === 'pendiente' || st === 'activa' || st === 'confirmada') orphansInTerminal.push(r);
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  });

  if (orphansInTerminal.length > 0) {
    await Promise.all(orphansInTerminal.map((orp) => claimRefundAndDeleteOrphanRow(orp, String(orp?.usuario_id || ''), 0)));
  }

  const uniqueUserIds = Array.from(new Set(reservasData.map((r: any) => r.usuario_id).filter(Boolean)));
  let perfData: any[] = [];
  if (uniqueUserIds.length > 0) {
    const byId = new Map<string, any>();
    const pubRes = await supabase.from('perfiles_publicos').select('id, nombre, avatar_url, nivel').in('id', uniqueUserIds);
    if (!pubRes.error && Array.isArray(pubRes.data)) {
      for (const p of pubRes.data) {
        if (p?.id) byId.set(String(p.id), p);
      }
    }
    const missingIds = uniqueUserIds.filter((uid) => {
      const existing = byId.get(String(uid));
      return !existing || !String(existing?.nombre || '').trim();
    });
    if (missingIds.length > 0) {
      const { data: fallbackProfiles } = await supabase
        .from('perfiles')
        .select('id, nombre, avatar_url, nivel')
        .in('id', missingIds);
      if (Array.isArray(fallbackProfiles)) {
        for (const p of fallbackProfiles) {
          if (p?.id) byId.set(String(p.id), { ...(byId.get(String(p.id)) || {}), ...p });
        }
      }
    }
    perfData = Array.from(byId.values());
  }

  if ((isGlobal || hasActiveBusiness) && reservasData.length > 0) {
    try {
      const { data: shaRows, error: shaErr } = await supabase.rpc('obtener_huellas_offline_b2b', {
        p_negocio_nombre: isGlobal ? null : cleanEstadio,
      });
      if (!shaErr && Array.isArray(shaRows) && shaRows.length > 0) {
        const shaById = new Map<string, string>();
        shaRows.forEach((row: { id?: string; qr_token_sha256?: string }) => {
          if (row?.id && row?.qr_token_sha256) shaById.set(String(row.id), String(row.qr_token_sha256));
        });
        if (shaById.size > 0) {
          for (const r of reservasData as { id?: string; qr_token_sha256?: string }[]) {
            const digest = shaById.get(String(r?.id || ''));
            if (digest) r.qr_token_sha256 = digest;
          }
        }
      }
    } catch {}
  }

  return { reservas: reservasData, servicios: validServicios, perfiles: perfData };
}

import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import {
  CIPHER_PREFIX,
  decryptKineticString,
  encryptKineticString,
  ensureDeviceCipherSaltAsync,
  getOrCreateWebSaltSync,
} from './kineticVaultCrypto';
import { isPassExpiredWithoutCheckIn } from './kineticNoShowEngine';
import { registerSessionCleanupHook } from './kineticSessionPersistence';

export const TERMINAL_SWR_CACHE_V2_KEY = '@kinetic_terminal_cache_v2';
export const SERVER_CLOCK_OFFSET_STORAGE_KEY = '@kinetic_server_clock_offset_ms_v1';

let _serverClockOffsetMem = 0;
export const getLocalServerClockOffsetMs = (): number => _serverClockOffsetMem;
export const persistServerClockOffsetMs = async (offsetMs: number): Promise<void> => {
  if (!Number.isFinite(offsetMs)) return;
  _serverClockOffsetMem = Math.round(offsetMs);
  AsyncStorage.setItem(SERVER_CLOCK_OFFSET_STORAGE_KEY, String(_serverClockOffsetMem)).catch(() => {});
};
export const hydrateServerClockOffsetMs = async (): Promise<number> => {
  if (_serverClockOffsetMem !== 0) return _serverClockOffsetMem;
  try {
    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(SERVER_CLOCK_OFFSET_STORAGE_KEY);
    const parsed = Number(raw);
    if (raw !== null && Number.isFinite(parsed)) { _serverClockOffsetMem = parsed; return parsed; }
  } catch {}
  return 0;
};
export const extractSpotFromPass = (p: any): number | null => {
  const direct = Number(p?.spot_number ?? p?.estacion ?? p?.spot ?? NaN);
  if (Number.isInteger(direct) && direct >= 1 && direct <= 50) return direct;
  const m = String(p?.notas || '').match(/(?:#|spot\s*|estaci[oó]n\s*)(\d+)\b/i);
  const parsed = m ? Number(m[1]) : NaN;
  return Number.isInteger(parsed) && parsed >= 1 && parsed <= 50 ? parsed : null;
};

  fetchedAt: number;

  servicios: any[];
  reservas: any[];
