}

export const isNetworkOfflineError = (err: unknown): boolean => {
  if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && navigator.onLine === false) return true;
  const msg = String((err as any)?.message || err || '').toLowerCase();
  return /network request failed|failed to fetch|fetch failed|networkerror|load failed|timeout|timed out|aborted|offline|enotfound|econnrefused/.test(msg);
};

let _swrCacheMem: ITerminalSwrSnapshot | null = null;
const _memSwrByUser = new Map<string, ITerminalSwrSnapshot>();
registerSessionCleanupHook(() => {
  _swrCacheMem = null;
  _memSwrByUser.clear();
  if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      window.localStorage?.removeItem(TERMINAL_SWR_CACHE_V2_KEY);
    } catch {}
  }
  AsyncStorage.removeItem(TERMINAL_SWR_CACHE_V2_KEY).catch(() => {});
});

export const getSwrCacheMemInternal = (): ITerminalSwrSnapshot | null => _swrCacheMem;

const DELETED_SWRSERVICE_STATES = new Set([
  'eliminado', 'eliminada', 'borrado', 'borrada', 'deleted',
  'cancelado', 'cancelada', 'inactivo', 'inactiva', 'archivado', 'archivada',
]);

export const sanitizeSwrSnapshotOrphans = (snap: ITerminalSwrSnapshot): ITerminalSwrSnapshot => {
  if (!snap || typeof snap !== 'object') return snap;
  let nextBiz = snap.businessData;
  const rawBizName = String(nextBiz?.nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (nextBiz && (rawBizName === 'terminal global kinetic' || rawBizName === 'kinetic oficial')) {
    nextBiz = { ...nextBiz, nombre: 'Kinetic Oficial', isOfficialKinetic: true };
  }
  const rawServicios = Array.isArray(snap.servicios) ? snap.servicios : [];
  const cleanServicios = rawServicios.filter(
    (s) => s && !DELETED_SWRSERVICE_STATES.has(String(s?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim())
  );
  if (!Array.isArray(snap.reservas)) {
    return nextBiz === snap.businessData && cleanServicios.length === rawServicios.length
      ? snap
      : { ...snap, businessData: nextBiz, servicios: cleanServicios };
  }
  const servIds = new Set(cleanServicios.map((s) => String(s?.id || '').trim()).filter(Boolean));
  const servNames = new Set(
    cleanServicios
      .flatMap((s) => [s?.nombre_servicio, s?.titulo, s?.nombre])
      .map((n) => String(n || '').trim().toLowerCase())
      .filter(Boolean)
  );
  const expiredPasses: any[] = [];
  const cleanReservas = snap.reservas.filter((r) => {
    const st = String(r?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    if (!['pendiente', 'activa', 'confirmada'].includes(st)) return false;
    const sid = String(r?.servicio_id || '').trim();
    const matchedSrv = sid
      ? cleanServicios.find((s) => String(s?.id || '').trim() === sid)
      : cleanServicios.find((s) => {
          const nm = String(r?.nombre_servicio || r?.servicio || r?.titulo_clase || '').trim().toLowerCase();
          return [s?.nombre_servicio, s?.titulo, s?.nombre].some((n) => String(n || '').trim().toLowerCase() === nm);
        });
    if (!matchedSrv) return false;
    if (isPassExpiredWithoutCheckIn(r, matchedSrv)) {
      expiredPasses.push({ ...r, estado: 'no_show', penalizacion_aplicada: true });
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  });
  const baseHist = Array.isArray(snap.metricas?.historialCompleto) ? snap.metricas.historialCompleto : [];
  const seenHistIds = new Set(baseHist.map((h: any) => String(h?.id || '')));
  const appendedExpired = expiredPasses.filter((e) => !seenHistIds.has(String(e?.id || '')));
  if (
    nextBiz === snap.businessData &&
    cleanServicios.length === rawServicios.length &&
    cleanReservas.length === snap.reservas.length &&
    appendedExpired.length === 0
  ) {
    return snap;
  }
  const nextMetricas = snap.metricas
    ? {
        ...snap.metricas,
        historialCompleto: appendedExpired.length > 0 ? [...appendedExpired, ...baseHist] : baseHist,
      }
    : snap.metricas;
  return { ...snap, businessData: nextBiz, servicios: cleanServicios, reservas: cleanReservas, metricas: nextMetricas };
};

export function readTerminalSwrCacheSync(userId?: string): ITerminalSwrSnapshot | null {
  if (!userId) return null;
  const cachedMem = _swrCacheMem?.userId === userId ? _swrCacheMem : _memSwrByUser.get(userId) || null;
  if (cachedMem) {
    _swrCacheMem = sanitizeSwrSnapshotOrphans(cachedMem);
    _memSwrByUser.set(userId, _swrCacheMem);
    return _swrCacheMem;
  }
  if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      const raw = window.localStorage?.getItem(TERMINAL_SWR_CACHE_V2_KEY);
      if (raw) {
        const plain = raw.startsWith(CIPHER_PREFIX) ? decryptKineticString(raw, getOrCreateWebSaltSync()) : raw;
        const parsed = plain ? JSON.parse(plain) : null;
        if (parsed && parsed.userId === userId) {
          _swrCacheMem = sanitizeSwrSnapshotOrphans({ ...parsed, fetchedAt: 0 });
          _memSwrByUser.set(userId, _swrCacheMem);
          return _swrCacheMem;
        }
      }
    } catch {}
  }
  return null;
}

export async function hydrateTerminalSwrCache(userId?: string): Promise<ITerminalSwrSnapshot | null> {
  if (!userId) return null;
  const cachedMem = _swrCacheMem?.userId === userId ? _swrCacheMem : _memSwrByUser.get(userId) || null;
  if (cachedMem) {
    _swrCacheMem = sanitizeSwrSnapshotOrphans(cachedMem);
    _memSwrByUser.set(userId, _swrCacheMem);
    return _swrCacheMem;
  }
  try {
    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(TERMINAL_SWR_CACHE_V2_KEY);
    if (!raw) return null;
    const salt = raw.startsWith(CIPHER_PREFIX) ? await ensureDeviceCipherSaltAsync() : '';
    const plain = raw.startsWith(CIPHER_PREFIX) ? decryptKineticString(raw, salt) : raw;
    const parsed = plain ? JSON.parse(plain) : null;
    if (parsed && parsed.userId === userId) {
      _swrCacheMem = sanitizeSwrSnapshotOrphans({ ...parsed, fetchedAt: 0 });
      _memSwrByUser.set(userId, _swrCacheMem);
      return _swrCacheMem;
    }
  } catch {}
  return null;
}

export function persistTerminalSwrCache(snapshot: ITerminalSwrSnapshot | null): void {
  if (!snapshot?.userId) return;
  const cleanSnap = sanitizeSwrSnapshotOrphans(snapshot);
  _swrCacheMem = cleanSnap;
  _memSwrByUser.set(cleanSnap.userId, cleanSnap);
  const serialized = JSON.stringify(cleanSnap);
  if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      window.localStorage?.setItem(
        TERMINAL_SWR_CACHE_V2_KEY,
        encryptKineticString(serialized, getOrCreateWebSaltSync())
      );
    } catch {}
  }
  ensureDeviceCipherSaltAsync()
    .then((salt) => AsyncStorage.setItem(TERMINAL_SWR_CACHE_V2_KEY, encryptKineticString(serialized, salt)))
    .catch(() => {});
}
